Abstract: The evaluation of general-purpose artificial agents, particularly those based on large language models, presents a significant challenge due to the non-transitive nature of their interactions. When agent A defeats B, B defeats C, and C defeats A, traditional ranking methods that force a linear ordering can be misleading and unstable. We argue that for such cyclic domains, the fundamental object of evaluation should not be a ranking but a set-valued core, as conceptualized in classical tournament theory. This paper introduces Soft Tournament Equilibrium (STE), a differentiable framework for learning and computing set-valued tournament solutions directly from pairwise comparison data. STE first learns a probabilistic tournament model, potentially conditioned on rich contextual information. It then employs novel, differentiable operators for soft reachability and soft covering to compute continuous analogues of two seminal tournament solutions: the Top Cycle and the Uncovered Set. The output is a set of core agents, each with a calibrated membership score, providing a nuanced and robust assessment of agent capabilities. We develop the theoretical foundation for STE to prove its consistency with classical solutions in the zero-temperature limit, which establishes its Condorcet-inclusion properties, and analyzing its stability and sample complexity. We specify an experimental protocol for validating STE on both synthetic and real-world benchmarks. This work aims to provide a complete, standalone treatise that re-centers general-agent evaluation on a more appropriate and robust theoretical foundation, moving from unstable rankings to stable, set-valued equilibria.

</details>


### [42] [When Do Hallucinations Arise? A Graph Perspective on the Evolution of Path Reuse and Path Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03557)
*Xinnan Dai,Kai Yang,Cheng Luo,Shenglai Zeng,Kai Guo,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将LLM的下一个token预测建模为图搜索过程，提出了两个导致推理幻觉的核心机制：Path Reuse（路径复用）和Path Compression（路径压缩），为理解LLM推理失败提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推理幻觉现象普遍存在（产生流畅但无依据的结论），但decoder-only Transformer产生幻觉的具体机制尚不清晰。

Method: 将next-token预测建模为图搜索过程：实体为节点，学习到的转移为边。上下文推理=采样子图上的约束搜索（内在推理）；无上下文查询=底层图中的记忆结构（外在推理）。

Result: 识别出两个导致推理幻觉的根本机制：(1) Path Reuse：训练早期记忆知识覆盖上下文约束；(2) Path Compression：训练后期频繁遍历的多步路径坍缩为快捷边。

Conclusion: 这两个机制为LLM推理幻觉提供了统一解释，并与下游应用中观察到的行为相关联。

Abstract: Reasoning hallucinations in large language models (LLMs) often appear as fluent yet unsupported conclusions that violate either the given context or underlying factual knowledge. Although such failures are widely observed, the mechanisms by which decoder-only Transformers produce them remain poorly understood. We model next-token prediction as a graph search process over an underlying graph, where entities correspond to nodes and learned transitions form edges. From this perspective, contextual reasoning is a constrained search over a sampled subgraph (intrinsic reasoning), while context-free queries rely on memorized structures in the underlying graph (extrinsic reasoning). We show that reasoning hallucinations arise from two fundamental mechanisms: \textbf{Path Reuse}, where memorized knowledge overrides contextual constraints during early training, and \textbf{Path Compression}, where frequently traversed multi-step paths collapse into shortcut edges in later training. Together, these mechanisms provide a unified explanation for reasoning hallucinations in LLMs and connected to well-known behaviors observed in downstream applications.

</details>


### [43] [Optimizing Service Operations via LLM-Powered Multi-Agent Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04383)
*Yanyuan Wang,Xiaowei Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体模拟框架（LLM-MAS），将服务运营设计优化问题建模为具有决策依赖不确定性的随机优化问题，通过在单次模拟运行中同时构建零阶梯度估计并更新设计参数来优化稳态性能，在可持续供应链和竞赛设计案例中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 服务系统性能取决于参与者对设计选择的响应，但人类行为的复杂性使得建模这些响应十分困难。传统方法难以捕捉真实的行为异质性和动态交互，现有模拟方法成本高、灵活性差，需要一种能高效建模复杂人类响应行为的优化框架。

Method: 提出LLM-MAS框架，将设计选择嵌入提示词中，通过LLM驱动的智能体交互形成受控的马尔可夫链来建模不确定性。开发了一种轨迹学习算法，在单次模拟运行中同时构造零阶梯度估计并更新设计参数，并结合方差缩减技术提升效率。

Result: 在可持续供应链应用中表现优于多种基准方法（包括黑盒优化和将LLM仅作为数值求解器或角色扮演系统设计器的方法）。在竞赛设计案例中，LLM-MAS既能作为已知设计的高成本效益评估工具，又能发现传统方法遗漏的优秀设计方案。

Conclusion: LLM-MAS为服务运营优化提供了一种可扩展且具有行为基础的模拟框架，能够在有限计算成本下建模复杂的人类响应行为，为设计空间探索和决策优化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Service system performance depends on how participants respond to design choices, but modeling these responses is hard due to the complexity of human behavior. We introduce an LLM-powered multi-agent simulation (LLM-MAS) framework for optimizing service operations. We pose the problem as stochastic optimization with decision-dependent uncertainty: design choices are embedded in prompts and shape the distribution of outcomes from interacting LLM-powered agents. By embedding key numerical information in prompts and extracting it from LLM-generated text, we model this uncertainty as a controlled Markov chain. We develop an on-trajectory learning algorithm that, on a single simulation run, simultaneously constructs zeroth-order gradient estimates and updates design parameters to optimize steady-state performance. We also incorporate variance reduction techniques. In a sustainable supply chain application, our method outperforms benchmarks, including blackbox optimization and using LLMs as numerical solvers or as role-playing system designers. A case study on optimal contest design with real behavioral data shows that LLM-MAS is both as a cost-effective evaluator of known designs and an exploratory tool that can uncover strong designs overlooked by traditional approaches.

</details>


### [44] [When Adaptive Rewards Hurt: Causal Probing and the Switching-Stability Dilemma in LLM-Guided LEO Satellite Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03562)
*Yuanhang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LEO卫星调度中DRL的一个"切换-稳定性困境"：近恒定的奖励权重(342.1 Mbps)反而优于精心调优的动态权重(103.3±96.8 Mbps)。通过单变量因果探针法发现反直觉的奖励杠杆效应（如切换惩罚增加20%可带来157 Mbps提升），最终表明输出一致性而非领域知识是LLM-DRL集成的关键约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究自适应奖励权重是否能在多波束LEO卫星调度中优于静态权重，旨在通过 regime-aware 的奖励设计提升DRL性能。核心动机是探索PPO算法中奖励权重动态调整对价值函数收敛的影响。

Method: 采用单变量因果探针法：独立对每个奖励项进行±20%扰动，测量50k步后的PPO响应；对四种MDP架构变体（固定、规则化、MLP学习、LLM微调）进行跨已知和新颖流量regime的评估。

Result: 近恒定权重(342.1 Mbps)显著优于动态权重；MLP在已知regime达357.9 Mbps、新颖regime达325.2 Mbps；微调LLM因权重振荡崩溃至45.3±43.0 Mbps。关键发现：切换惩罚增加20%在极地切换场景带来+157 Mbps、在热冷regime带来+130 Mbps增益。

Conclusion: 权重自适应性（无论质量如何）会因反复重启收敛而降低性能；PPO需要准平稳奖励信号以实现价值函数收敛；LLM-DRL集成的真正瓶颈是输出一致性而非领域知识，LLM的价值在于自然语言意图理解而非复杂的奖励权重学习。

Abstract: Adaptive reward design for deep reinforcement learning (DRL) in multi-beam LEO satellite scheduling is motivated by the intuition that regime-aware reward weights should outperform static ones. We systematically test this intuition and uncover a switching-stability dilemma: near-constant reward weights (342.1 Mbps) outperform carefully-tuned dynamic weights (103.3+/-96.8 Mbps) because PPO requires a quasistationary reward signal for value function convergence. Weight adaptation-regardless of quality-degrades performance by repeatedly restarting convergence. To understand why specific weights matter, we introduce a single-variable causal probing method that independently perturbs each reward term by +/-20% and measures PPO response after 50k steps. Probing reveals counterintuitive leverage: a +20% increase in the switching penalty yields +157 Mbps for polar handover and +130 Mbps for hot-cold regimes-findings inaccessible to human experts or trained MLPs without systematic probing. We evaluate four MDP architect variants (fixed, rule-based, learned MLP, finetuned LLM) across known and novel traffic regimes. The MLP achieves 357.9 Mbps on known regimes and 325.2 Mbps on novel regimes, while the fine-tuned LLM collapses to 45.3+/-43.0 Mbps due to weight oscillation rather than lack of domain knowledge-output consistency, not knowledge, is the binding constraint. Our findings provide an empirically-grounded roadmap for LLM-DRL integration in communication systems, identifying where LLMs add irreplaceable value (natural language intent understanding) versus where simpler methods suffice.

</details>


### [45] [Memory Intelligence Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04503)
*Jingyang Qiao,Weicheng Meng,Yu Cheng,Zhihang Lin,Zhizhong Zhang,Xin Tan,Jingyu Gong,Kun Shao,Yuan Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIA框架通过Manager-Planner-Executor架构解决深度研究智能体的记忆演化效率低下和成本递增问题，采用交替强化学习、测试时学习、双向记忆转换等技术，在11个基准上验证了优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度研究智能体(DRAs)的记忆系统依赖相似轨迹检索，存在记忆演化效率低下和存储检索成本递增两大关键缺陷。

Method: 提出MIA框架：Manager（非参数记忆存储压缩轨迹）+ Planner（参数记忆生成搜索计划）+ Executor（执行信息搜索分析）；采用交替强化学习范式、测试时持续学习、双向参数/非参数记忆转换循环、反思与无监督评判机制。

Result: 在11个基准数据集上验证了MIA的优越性。

Conclusion: MIA成功实现了高效的记忆演化与推理能力的统一，有效解决了现有方法的关键局限性。

Abstract: Deep research agents (DRAs) integrate LLM reasoning with external tools. Memory systems enable DRAs to leverage historical experiences, which are essential for efficient reasoning and autonomous evolution. Existing methods rely on retrieving similar trajectories from memory to aid reasoning, while suffering from key limitations of ineffective memory evolution and increasing storage and retrieval costs. To address these problems, we propose a novel Memory Intelligence Agent (MIA) framework, consisting of a Manager-Planner-Executor architecture. Memory Manager is a non-parametric memory system that can store compressed historical search trajectories. Planner is a parametric memory agent that can produce search plans for questions. Executor is another agent that can search and analyze information guided by the search plan. To build the MIA framework, we first adopt an alternating reinforcement learning paradigm to enhance cooperation between the Planner and the Executor. Furthermore, we enable the Planner to continuously evolve during test-time learning, with updates performed on-the-fly alongside inference without interrupting the reasoning process. Additionally, we establish a bidirectional conversion loop between parametric and non-parametric memories to achieve efficient memory evolution. Finally, we incorporate a reflection and an unsupervised judgment mechanisms to boost reasoning and self-evolution in the open world. Extensive experiments across eleven benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of MIA.

</details>

Abstract: Can lifetime learning expand behavioral diversity over evolutionary time, rather than collapsing it? Prior theory predicts that plasticity reduces variance by buffering organisms against environmental noise. We test this in a competitive domain: chess agents with eight NEAT-evolved neural modules, Hebbian within-game plasticity, and a desirability-domain signal chain with imagination. Across 10~seeds per Hebbian condition, a variance crossover emerges: Hebbian ON starts with lower cross-seed variance than OFF, then surpasses it at generation~34. The crossover trend is monotonic (\r{ho} = 0.91, p < 10^{-6): plasticity's effect on behavioral variance reverses over evolutionary time, initially compressing diversity (consistent with prior predictions) then expanding it as evolved Perception differences are amplified through imagination -- a feedback loop that mutation alone cannot sustain.
  The result is structured behavioral divergence: evolved agents select different moves on the same positions (62\% disagreement), develop distinct opening repertoires, piece preferences, and game lengths. These are not different sampling policies -- they are reproducible behavioral signatures (ICC > 0.8) with interpretable signal chain configurations. Three regimes appear depending on opponent type: exploration (Hebbian ON, heterogeneous opponent), lottery (Hebbian OFF, elitism lock-in), and transparent (same-model opponent, brain self-erasure). The transparent regime generates a falsifiable prediction: self-play systems may systematically suppress behavioral diversity by eliminating the heterogeneity that personality requires.
  \textbf{Keywords: Baldwin Effect, neuroevolution, NEAT, Hebbian learning, chess, cognitive architecture, personality emergence, imagination

</details>


### [47] [Selective Forgetting for Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03571)
*Tuan Le,Wei Qian,Mengdi Huai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大型推理模型（LRM）的选择性遗忘框架，通过检索增强生成（RAG）和多个LLM分析思维链（CoT）痕迹，识别遗忘相关片段并用良性占位符替换，同时引入特征替换遗忘损失来抑制遗忘内容的生成同时保持结构有效性。实验在合成和医疗数据集上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型（LRM）生成的思维链（CoT）中存在知识泄露风险，可能暴露训练数据中的版权或私有内容。现有的机器遗忘方法主要针对最终答案，导致遗忘后整体推理能力下降；而直接对整个CoT进行遗忘会损害通用推理能力。因此需要在精确遗忘目标知识与保留通用推理能力之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出基于RAG的多LLM协同框架：首先利用多个LLM结合检索增强生成分析CoT痕迹，识别需要遗忘的相关片段；然后用保持逻辑结构的良性占位符替换这些片段。此外，设计了特征替换遗忘损失函数，同时实现两个目标：抑制遗忘内容的生成概率，强化结构有效的替换结果。

Result: 在合成数据集和医疗数据集上的广泛实验验证了该方法能够实现预期特性，即在遗忘目标知识的同时有效保留通用推理能力。

Conclusion: 本文提出的选择性遗忘框架通过细粒度地处理CoT中的敏感片段，成功解决了LRM遗忘中精确性与能力保留的核心矛盾，为大语言模型的安全隐私保护提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) generate structured chains of thought (CoTs) before producing final answers, making them especially vulnerable to knowledge leakage through intermediate reasoning steps. Yet, the memorization of sensitive information in the training data such as copyrighted and private content has led to ethical and legal concerns. To address these issues, selective forgetting (also known as machine unlearning) has emerged as a potential remedy for LRMs. However, existing unlearning methods primarily target final answers and may degrade the overall reasoning ability of LRMs after forgetting. Additionally, directly applying unlearning on the entire CoTs could degrade the general reasoning capabilities. The key challenge for LRM unlearning lies in achieving precise unlearning of targeted knowledge while preserving the integrity of general reasoning capabilities. To bridge this gap, we in this paper propose a novel LRM unlearning framework that selectively removes sensitive reasoning components while preserving general reasoning capabilities. Our approach leverages multiple LLMs with retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to analyze CoT traces, identify forget-relevant segments, and replace them with benign placeholders that maintain logical structure. We also introduce a new feature replacement unlearning loss for LRMs, which can simultaneously suppress the probability of generating forgotten content while reinforcing structurally valid replacements. Extensive experiments on both synthetic and medical datasets verify the desired properties of our proposed method.

</details>


### [48] [SuperLocalMemory V3.3: The Living Brain -- Biologically-Inspired Forgetting, Cognitive Quantization, and Multi-Channel Retrieval for Zero-LLM Agent Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04514)
*Varun Pratap Bhardwaj*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SuperLocalMemory V3.3是一个本地优先的AI智能体记忆系统，实现了完整的认知记忆分类体系，包含5项核心贡献：新型FRQAD距离度量、自适应遗忘曲线、7通道认知检索、隐式长期记忆参数化及自动化认知流程。在LoCoMo基准上达到70.4%准确率（零LLM模式），在多跳和对抗性任务上分别提升23.8pp和12.7pp。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI编码智能体的"记忆悖论"——拥有海量参数知识却无法记住一小时前的对话。现有记忆系统存在三大缺陷：向量数据库单通道检索、依赖云端LLM核心操作、缺乏人类认知记忆的有效机制。

Method: 基于信息几何框架（V3.2），引入五项技术创新：(1) FRQAD度量——基于高斯统计流形，在量化感知距离上达100%精度；(2) 艾宾浩斯自适应遗忘——结合渐进嵌入压缩，判别能力提升6.7倍；(3) 7通道认知检索——语义、关键词、实体图谱、时序、扩散激活、巩固和Hopfield联想通道；(4) 通过软提示实现长期隐式记忆参数化；(5) 全自动零摩擦认知管道。

Result: V3.3在LoCoMo的Mode A（零LLM）达到70.4%；多跳任务+23.8pp，对抗性任务+12.7pp。相比V3.2的74.8%（Mode A）和87.7%（Mode C），存在4.4pp差距，属于刻意架构权衡。系统完全运行于CPU，开源采用Elastic License 2.0，月下载量超5000次。

Conclusion: 该系统证明了本地化、认知驱动的记忆架构在资源受限环境下的可行性，通过数学化的遗忘曲线和多通道检索机制，显著提升了智能体在复杂推理任务中的表现，为构建"活态大脑"式记忆系统提供了可行范式。

Abstract: AI coding agents operate in a paradox: they possess vast parametric knowledge yet cannot remember a conversation from an hour ago. Existing memory systems store text in vector databases with single-channel retrieval, require cloud LLMs for core operations, and implement none of the cognitive processes that make human memory effective.
  We present SuperLocalMemory V3.3 ("The Living Brain"), a local-first agent memory system implementing the full cognitive memory taxonomy with mathematical lifecycle dynamics. Building on the information-geometric foundations of V3.2 (arXiv:2603.14588), we introduce five contributions: (1) Fisher-Rao Quantization-Aware Distance (FRQAD) -- a new metric on the Gaussian statistical manifold achieving 100% precision at preferring high-fidelity embeddings over quantized ones (vs 85.6% for cosine), with zero prior art; (2) Ebbinghaus Adaptive Forgetting with lifecycle-aware quantization -- the first mathematical forgetting curve in local agent memory coupled to progressive embedding compression, achieving 6.7x discriminative power; (3) 7-channel cognitive retrieval spanning semantic, keyword, entity graph, temporal, spreading activation, consolidation, and Hopfield associative channels, achieving 70.4% on LoCoMo in zero-LLM Mode A; (4) memory parameterization implementing Long-Term Implicit memory via soft prompts; (5) zero-friction auto-cognitive pipeline automating the complete memory lifecycle.
  On LoCoMo, V3.3 achieves 70.4% in Mode A (zero-LLM), with +23.8pp on multi-hop and +12.7pp on adversarial. V3.2 achieved 74.8% Mode A and 87.7% Mode C; the 4.4pp gap reflects a deliberate architectural trade-off. SLM V3.3 is open source under the Elastic License 2.0, runs entirely on CPU, with over 5,000 monthly downloads.

</details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) have been increasingly deployed in edge devices and other resource-constrained settings. However, these models make confident mispredictions and produce unstable output, making them risky for factual and decision-critical tasks. Current evaluation methodology relies on final accuracy or hallucination rates without explaining how internal model behavior affects outputs. Specifically, how entropy evolves during decoding, how attention is distributed across layers, and how hidden representations contribute to uncertainty, logical inconsistencies, and misinformation propagation are often overlooked. Consequently, this study introduces a trace-level analysis of entropy and attention dynamics in SLMs evaluated with the TruthfulQA dataset. Four models with parameter ranges of 1B-1.7B parameters were examined via token-level output entropy, attention entropy, head dispersion, and hidden-state representation. The results reflect three model classifications by entropy patterns. Deterministic models (DeepSeek-1.5B and LLaMA-1B): output entropy decreases over time. Exploratory models (Gemma-1B): with increasing entropy, and balanced models (Qwen-1.7B): have moderate and stable entropy. Also, each group has distinctively different hidden-state movement and attention dispersion patterns. The analysis demonstrates that truthfulness in SLMs emerges from structured entropy and attention dynamics. Monitoring and optimizing these internal uncertainty patterns can guide the design of a more reliable, hallucination-aware, and application-specific edge SLMs.

</details>


### [50] [A Multimodal Foundation Model of Spatial Transcriptomics and Histology for Biological Discovery and Clinical Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03630)
*Jinxi Xiang,Siyu Hou,Yuchen Li,Ryan Quinton,Xiaoming Zhang,Feyisope Eweje,Xiangde Luo,Yijiang Chen,Zhe Li,Colin Bergstrom,Ted Kim,Sierra Willens,Francesca Maria Olguin,Matthew Abikenari,Andrew Heider,Sanjeeth Rajaram,Joel Neal,Maximilian Diehn,Xiang Zhou,Ruijiang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STORM是一个基于120万空间转录组学与匹配组织学数据训练的基础模型，采用分层架构整合形态特征、基因表达和空间上下文，实现从H&E图像预测空间基因表达，在11种肿瘤类型中表现优异，并在7245例患者的23个独立队列中显著提升免疫治疗响应预测和预后评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间转录组学(ST)成本高、通量低；H&E染色虽提供丰富形态学信息但缺乏分子分辨率。需要一种方法桥接成像与组学数据，实现成本可扩展的分子-形态学表征学习。

Method: STORM在18个器官的120万个空间解析转录组学图谱及匹配组织学数据上训练。采用分层架构整合形态特征、基因表达和空间上下文，训练跨平台(Visium、Xenium、Visium HD、CosMx)的基础模型，实现从H&E图像预测空间基因表达。

Result: STORM增强空间域发现能力，生成生物学一致的组组织地图；在11种肿瘤类型中，从H&E图像预测空间基因表达的表现优于现有方法。应用于23个独立队列(共7245例患者)，显著提升免疫治疗响应预测和预后评估能力。

Conclusion: STORM为空间信息驱动的科学发现和临床精准医学提供了一个可扩展的框架。

Abstract: Spatial transcriptomics (ST) enables gene expression mapping within anatomical context but remains costly and low-throughput. Hematoxylin and eosin (H\&E) staining offers rich morphology yet lacks molecular resolution. We present \textbf{\ours} (\textbf{S}patial \textbf{T}ranscriptomics and hist\textbf{O}logy \textbf{R}epresentation \textbf{M}odel), a foundation model trained on 1.2 million spatially resolved transcriptomic profiles with matched histology across 18 organs. Using a hierarchical architecture integrating morphological features, gene expression, and spatial context, STORM bridges imaging and omics through robust molecular--morphological representations. STORM enhances spatial domain discovery, producing biologically coherent tissue maps, and outperforms existing methods in predicting spatial gene expression from H\&E images across 11 tumor types. The model is platform-agnostic, performing consistently across Visium, Xenium, Visium HD, and CosMx. Applied to 23 independent cohorts comprising 7,245 patients, STORM significantly improves immunotherapy response prediction and prognostication over established biomarkers, providing a scalable framework for spatially informed discovery and clinical precision medicine.

</details>


### [51] [Single-agent vs. Multi-agents for Automated Video Analysis of On-Screen Collaborative Learning Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03631)
*Likai Peng,Shihui Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索了视觉语言模型（VLMs）在自动编码协作学习场景中屏幕录像行为的应用，比较了闭源模型（Claude-3.7-Sonnet、GPT-4.1）与开源模型（Qwen2.5-VL-72B），并提出了两种多智能体框架（工作流式MAS和自主决策式MAS）以提升场景检测和行为识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屏幕学习行为分析对于理解学生的认知和协作过程至关重要，但传统的手动编码方法劳动密集且难以规模化。随着视觉语言模型（VLMs）的发展，自动化多模态视频数据分析成为可能，但单一大模型在复杂任务中的表现仍有局限。

Method: 研究基于ICAP框架，对比了三种VLM在单智能体和多智能体设置下的表现。具体提出了两种多智能体框架：1）工作流式MAS：包含三个智能体，通过场景分割、光标引导的VLM提示和证据验证来检测屏幕行为；2）自主决策式MAS：灵感来自ReAct，通过推理、工具操作（分割/分类/验证）和观察驱动的自我修正迭代产生可解释的行为标签。

Result: 两种多智能体框架均取得了可行性能，在场景检测和行为检测任务上均优于单VLM。其中，工作流式MAS在场景检测上表现最佳，自主决策式MAS在行为检测上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的多智能体系统可有效用于视频分析，为多模态数据分析提供了可扩展的框架。研究证实了多智能体协作在提升自动化行为编码准确性方面的潜力。

Abstract: On-screen learning behavior provides valuable insights into how students seek, use, and create information during learning. Analyzing on-screen behavioral engagement is essential for capturing students' cognitive and collaborative processes. The recent development of Vision Language Models (VLMs) offers new opportunities to automate the labor-intensive manual coding often required for multimodal video data analysis. In this study, we compared the performance of both leading closed-source VLMs (Claude-3.7-Sonnet, GPT-4.1) and open-source VLM (Qwen2.5-VL-72B) in single- and multi-agent settings for automated coding of screen recordings in collaborative learning contexts based on the ICAP framework. In particular, we proposed and compared two multi-agent frameworks: 1) a three-agent workflow multi-agent system (MAS) that segments screen videos by scene and detects on-screen behaviors using cursor-informed VLM prompting with evidence-based verification; 2) an autonomous-decision MAS inspired by ReAct that iteratively interleaves reasoning, tool-like operations (segmentation/ classification/ validation), and observation-driven self-correction to produce interpretable on-screen behavior labels. Experimental results demonstrated that the two proposed MAS frameworks achieved viable performance, outperforming the single VLMs in scene and action detection tasks. It is worth noting that the workflow-based agent achieved best on scene detection, and the autonomous-decision MAS achieved best on action detection. This study demonstrates the effectiveness of VLM-based Multi-agent System for video analysis and contributes a scalable framework for multimodal data analytics.

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Retrieval: Modeling Confidence Decay and Deterministic Agentic Platforms in Generative Engine Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03656)
*XinYu Zhao,ChengYou Li,XiangBao Meng,Kai Zhang,XiaoDong Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文针对当前基于RAG的GEO策略存在的概率性幻觉和"零点击"悖论问题，提出向确定性多智能体意图路由的范式转变。通过数学建模语义熵漂移、提出同构归因回归模型和确定性智能体交接协议，在EasyNote工业产品上验证了垂直任务幻觉率接近零的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于RAG（检索增强生成）的GEO（生成式引擎优化）策略存在根本性缺陷：概率性幻觉问题和"零点击"悖论导致无法建立可持续的商业信任机制。

Method: 1. 数学形式化语义熵漂移(SED)模型，刻画LLM在连续时间和上下文扰动下的置信曲线动态衰减；2. 提出同构归因回归(IAR)模型，使用多智能体系统探针结合严格的人机隔离物理隔离来量化幻觉惩罚；3. 架构确定性智能体交接(DAH)协议，将LLM定位为意图路由器而非最终答案生成器，构建智能体信任经纪(ATB)生态系统；4. 在易树科技的EasyNote产品上进行实证验证。

Result: 通过将"无限画布上的知识图谱映射"意图通过DAH协议直接路由到专用专有智能体，垂直任务幻觉率降至接近零。

Conclusion: 建立了下一代GEO的的理论基础框架，为有序、确定性的人机协作生态系统铺平道路。

Abstract: Generative Engine Optimization (GEO) is rapidly reshaping digital marketing paradigms in the era of Large Language Models (LLMs). However, current GEO strategies predominantly rely on Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), which inherently suffers from probabilistic hallucinations and the "zero-click" paradox, failing to establish sustainable commercial trust. In this paper, we systematically deconstruct the probabilistic flaws of existing RAG-based GEO and propose a paradigm shift towards deterministic multi-agent intent routing. First, we mathematically formulate Semantic Entropy Drift (SED) to model the dynamic decay of confidence curves in LLMs over continuous temporal and contextual perturbations. To rigorously quantify optimization value in black-box commercial engines, we introduce the Isomorphic Attribution Regression (IAR) model, leveraging a Multi-Agent System (MAS) probe with strict human-in-the-loop physical isolation to enforce hallucination penalties. Furthermore, we architect the Deterministic Agent Handoff (DAH) protocol, conceptualizing an Agentic Trust Brokerage (ATB) ecosystem where LLMs function solely as intent routers rather than final answer generators. We empirically validate this architecture using EasyNote, an industrial AI meeting minutes product by Yishu Technology. By routing the intent of "knowledge graph mapping on an infinite canvas" directly to its specialized proprietary agent via DAH, we demonstrate the reduction of vertical task hallucination rates to near zero. This work establishes a foundational theoretical framework for next-generation GEO and paves the way for a well-ordered, deterministic human-AI collaboration ecosystem.

</details>


### [53] [TableVision: A Large-Scale Benchmark for Spatially Grounded Reasoning over Complex Hierarchical Tables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03660)
*Xiaoyu Chen,Lu Dai,Hanqing Wang,Zhuoyu Li,Wenbin Dai,Yanzong Zheng,Zhenggang Xia,Junyong Lin,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出TableVision基准测试和两阶段解耦框架，解决多模态大模型在处理复杂层级表格时的感知瓶颈问题，实现12.3%的准确率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多模态大模型(MLLMs)在处理复杂层级表格时存在"感知过载"问题：随着任务复杂度增加，涉及的离散视觉区域不成比例增加，导致模型在隐式生成过程中难以维持准确的空间注意力，造成推理性能受限。

Method: 1) 通过定量分析识别感知瓶颈；2) 构建TableVision大规模轨迹感知基准数据集，包含6,799条高保真推理轨迹，涵盖3个认知层级（感知、推理、分析）13个子类别；3) 采用基于渲染的确定性定位pipeline，将多步逻辑推理与像素级空间真值显式耦合；4) 提出两阶段解耦框架，将空间约束与逻辑推理分离。

Result: 实验表明：1) 显式空间约束显著恢复MLLMs的推理潜力；2) 两阶段解耦框架在测试集上实现12.3%的整体准确率提升；3) 诊断探测证实了感知瓶颈的存在及其对推理的影响。

Conclusion: TableVision为难处理文档理解提供了严格的测试平台，揭示了感知与逻辑的协同机制，为未来多模态模型在结构化表格理解领域的发展提供了新视角和评估基准。

Abstract: Structured tables are essential for conveying high-density information in professional domains such as finance, healthcare, and scientific research. Despite the progress in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), reasoning performance remains limited for complex tables with hierarchical layouts. In this paper, we identify a critical Perception Bottleneck through quantitative analysis. We find that as task complexity scales, the number of involved discrete visual regions increases disproportionately. This processing density leads to an internal "Perceptual Overload," where MLLMs struggle to maintain accurate spatial attention during implicit generation. To address this bottleneck, we introduce TableVision, a large-scale, trajectory-aware benchmark designed for spatially grounded reasoning. TableVision stratifies tabular tasks into three cognitive levels (Perception, Reasoning, and Analysis) across 13 sub-categories. By utilizing a rendering-based deterministic grounding pipeline, the dataset explicitly couples multi-step logical deductions with pixel-perfect spatial ground truths, comprising 6,799 high-fidelity reasoning trajectories. Our empirical results, supported by diagnostic probing, demonstrate that explicit spatial constraints significantly recover the reasoning potential of MLLMs. Furthermore, our two-stage decoupled framework achieves a robust 12.3% overall accuracy improvement on the test set. TableVision provides a rigorous testbed and a fresh perspective on the synergy between perception and logic in document understanding.

</details>


### [54] [PRAISE: Prefix-Based Rollout Reuse in Agentic Search Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03675)
*Erhan Zhang,Yiqun Chen,Zechun Niu,Wei Yang,Xiaochi Wei,Yan Gao,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Jiaxin Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PRAISE是一种用于改进智能体搜索训练的框架，通过从完整搜索轨迹中提取前缀状态，生成中间答案，从而同时提高数据效率和信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于搜索的强化学习方法存在两个核心问题：1) 昂贵的长期视野 rollout 在训练中未被充分利用；2) 监督信号仅在最终答案处可用，导致奖励稀疏严重。

Method: PRAISE从完整搜索轨迹中提取不同搜索轮次的前缀状态，激发中间答案，并利用这些前缀：1) 构建额外的训练轨迹；2) 根据前缀间的性能差异推导步级奖励。使用单一共享模型同时进行搜索策略学习和前缀答案评估，无需额外人工标注或单独的奖励模型。

Result: 在多跳问答基准测试上的实验表明，PRAISE始终优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: PRAISE通过前缀级 rollout 重用和中间步骤奖励有效解决了长期视野 rollouts 利用不足和奖励稀疏的问题，为智能体搜索训练提供了一种高效且无需额外监督的解决方案。

Abstract: In agentic search, large language models (LLMs) are trained to perform multi-turn retrieval and reasoning for complex tasks such as multi-hop question answering (QA). However, current search-based Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods suffer from two core limitations: expensive long-horizon rollouts are under-utilized during training, and supervision is typically available only at the final answer, resulting in severe reward sparsity. We present Prefix-based Rollout reuse for Agentic search with Intermediate Step rEwards (PRAISE), a framework for improving both data efficiency and credit assignment in agentic search training. Given a complete search trajectory, PRAISE extracts prefix states at different search turns, elicits intermediate answers from them, and uses these prefixes both to construct additional training trajectories and to derive step-level rewards from performance differences across prefixes. Our method uses a single shared model for both search policy learning and prefix answer evaluation, enabling joint optimization without extra human annotations or a separate reward model. Experiments on multi-hop QA benchmarks show that PRAISE consistently improves performance over strong baselines.

</details>


### [55] [Structured Multi-Criteria Evaluation of Large Language Models with Fuzzy Analytic Hierarchy Process and DualJudge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03742)
*Yulong He,Ivan Smirnov,Dmitry Fedrushkov,Sergey Kovalchuk,Ilya Revin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将层次分析法(AHP)应用于大语言模型(LLM)评估，并引入基于三角模糊数的置信感知模糊层次分析法(FAHP)来处理认知不确定性，进一步提出DualJudge混合框架，通过一致性感知加权融合直觉式直接评分与审慎式AHP结构化输出，在JudgeBench上实现最先进的评估性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统直接评分方法对LLM评估产生不一致且不透明的判断，缺乏系统化的评估标准和不确定性建模能力。

Method: 1) 将AHP框架适配至LLM评估领域；2) 提出置信感知FAHP扩展，使用三角模糊数建模认识不确定性，由LLM生成的置信分数调节；3) 在JudgeBench上系统验证；4) 提出DualJudge混合框架，基于双重过程理论，通过一致性感知加权融合直接评分与AHP输出。

Result: FAHP和AHP在模型尺度和数据集划分上均一致性地优于直接评分方法；FAHP在不确定比较场景中表现出更高的稳定性；DualJudge达到最佳性能，验证了直觉与审慎评估范式的互补优势。

Conclusion: 不确定性感知结构化推理是实现更可靠LLM评估的有效路径。

Abstract: Effective evaluation of large language models (LLMs) remains a critical bottleneck, as conventional direct scoring often yields inconsistent and opaque judgments. In this work, we adapt the Analytic Hierarchy Process (AHP) to LLM-based evaluation and, more importantly, propose a confidence-aware Fuzzy AHP (FAHP) extension that models epistemic uncertainty via triangular fuzzy numbers modulated by LLM-generated confidence scores. Systematically validated on JudgeBench, our structured approach decomposes assessments into explicit criteria and incorporates uncertainty-aware aggregation, producing more calibrated judgments. Extensive experiments demonstrate that both crisp and fuzzy AHP consistently outperform direct scoring across model scales and dataset splits, with FAHP showing superior stability in uncertain comparison scenarios. Building on these insights, we propose \textbf{DualJudge}, a hybrid framework inspired by Dual-Process Theory that adaptively fuses holistic direct scores with structured AHP outputs via consistency-aware weighting. DualJudge achieves state-of-the-art performance, underscoring the complementary strengths of intuitive and deliberative evaluation paradigms. These results establish uncertainty-aware structured reasoning as a principled pathway toward more reliable LLM assessment. Code is available at https://github.com/hreyulog/AHP_llm_judge.

</details>

*Ying Yao*

TL;DR: 本研究提出基于深度强化学习（PPO算法）的土地覆被优化框架，应用于马拉维湖流域，旨在最大化生态系统服务价值（ESV）。通过Sentinel-2影像识别9类土地覆被，结合Costanza效益转移法评估生态价值，在50×50网格环境中通过动作掩码迭代优化土地配置。奖励函数整合生态价值与空间连贯性目标（连通性奖励、缓冲区惩罚），实验验证了三种场景下的优化效果，框架可作为环境规划的情景分析工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生态敏感区域不可持续的土地利用实践正在威胁生物多样性、水资源和数百万人的生计。传统土地利用优化方法难以同时处理生态价值最大化和空间格局合理化的多目标决策问题，需要创新的计算框架来支持环境规划决策。

Method: 采用深度强化学习方法，构建基于PPO（近端策略优化）算法的智能体；利用Sentinel-2影像提取9类土地覆被数据；应用Costanza等人的效益转移方法确定生物群落特异性ESV系数；以50×50像元（500m分辨率）网格为RL环境；奖励函数整合单位格网生态价值、连通性奖励（促进生态连通斑块聚集）和缓冲区惩罚（限制高影响开发邻近水体）。评估三种情景：纯ESV最大化、含空间奖励塑形的ESV优化、再生农业政策情景。

Result: 强化学习智能体有效学习提升总ESV；空间奖励塑形成功引导土地配置趋向生态合理模式（包括同质土地聚集、水体邻近林地轻度整合）；框架对政策参数变化响应敏感，具备情景分析能力。

Conclusion: 所提框架成功将深度强化学习应用于土地优化配置问题，验证了RL在环境规划中的实用性；空间奖励塑形机制有效协调生态价值与空间格局目标；框架可作为环境规划者的情景分析工具，支持不同政策目标下的土地利用决策。

Abstract: Unsustainable land-use practices in ecologically sensitive regions threaten biodiversity, water resources, and the livelihoods of millions. This paper presents a deep reinforcement learning (RL) framework for optimizing land-use allocation in the Lake Malawi Basin to maximize total ecosystem service value (ESV). Drawing on the benefit transfer methodology of Costanza et al., we assign biome-specific ESV coefficients -- locally anchored to a Malawi wetland valuation -- to nine land-cover classes derived from Sentinel-2 imagery. The RL environment models a 50x50 cell grid at 500m resolution, where a Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) agent with action masking iteratively transfers land-use pixels between modifiable classes. The reward function combines per-cell ecological value with spatial coherence objectives: contiguity bonuses for ecologically connected land-use patches (forest, cropland, built area etc.) and buffer zone penalties for high-impact development adjacent to water bodies. We evaluate the framework across three scenarios: (i) pure ESV maximization, (ii) ESV with spatial reward shaping, and (iii) a regenerative agriculture policy scenario. Results demonstrate that the agent effectively learns to increase total ESV; that spatial reward shaping successfully steers allocations toward ecologically sound patterns, including homogeneous land-use clustering and slight forest consolidation near water bodies; and that the framework responds meaningfully to policy parameter changes, establishing its utility as a scenario-analysis tool for environmental planning.

</details>


### [57] [Affording Process Auditability with QualAnalyzer: An Atomistic LLM Analysis Tool for Qualitative Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03820)
*Max Hao Lu,Ryan Ellegood,Rony Rodriguez-Ramirez,Sophia Blumert*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了QualAnalyzer，一个用于LLM辅助定性研究的开源Chrome扩展，通过逐段独立处理数据并完整保留提示词、输入和输出，创建可追溯的审计轨迹。两个案例研究（整体作文评分和演绎式主题编码）验证了该方法在提高LLM辅助定性研究透明度和方法论严谨性方面的价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助定性数据分析的工作流程存在黑箱问题，模糊了分析结论的产生过程，缺乏透明度和可追溯性，影响研究的方法论严谨性。

Method: 开发了QualAnalyzer——一个集成于Google Workspace的开源Chrome扩展，核心设计原则是"原子化LLM分析"，即对每个数据片段独立处理，并完整保存每次分析的提示词、输入和输出。

Result: 通过两个案例研究验证：（1）整体作文评分；（2）访谈文本的演绎式主题编码。结果表明该方法创建了清晰的审计轨迹，并能帮助研究者调查LLM判断与人类判断之间的系统性差异。

Conclusion: 过程可审计性（process auditability）是实现LLM辅助定性研究透明化和方法论严谨的关键要素，应作为该领域的方法论标准。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for qualitative data analysis, but many workflows obscure how analytic conclusions are produced. We present QualAnalyzer, an open-source Chrome extension for Google Workspace that supports atomistic LLM analysis by processing each data segment independently and preserving the prompt, input, and output for every unit. Through two case studies -- holistic essay scoring and deductive thematic coding of interview transcripts -- we show that this approach creates a legible audit trail and helps researchers investigate systematic differences between LLM and human judgments. We argue that process auditability is essential for making LLM-assisted qualitative research more transparent and methodologically robust.

</details>

### [58] [Approximation Algorithms for Matroid-Intersection Coloring with Applications to Rota's Basis Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03735)
*Stephen Arndt,Benjamin Moseley,Kirk Pruhs,Chaitanya Swamy,Michael Zlatin*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究拟阵交集着色问题，给定k个拟阵，目标是将其公共元素集划分最少的颜色类，使得每个颜色类在所有拟阵中都是独立的。论文给出了首个多项式时间算法，对两个拟阵达到2-近似，对k个拟阵达到(k²-k)χ_max着色，并给出FPRAS算法，同时构造性地证明了Rota基猜想的一个渐近版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知2χ_max和(2k-1)χ_max的颜色上界存在，但证明依赖拓扑霍尔定理和Sperner引理等非构造性技术，缺乏多项式时间算法。该研究旨在将这些存在性结果算法化，并首次给出逼近比仅依赖k（与n无关）的多项式时间算法。

Method: 引入"灵活分解"(flexible decomposition)这一新的拟阵结构，将一般拟阵交集着色问题形式化地归约为图着色问题，避免了分区归约技术的局限性，且不依赖非构造性的拓扑方法。

Result: 1) 两个拟阵：构造性地达到2χ_max上界，获得2-近似算法；2) k个拟阵：得到(k²-k)χ_max着色，是常数k下首个O(1)-近似算法；3) 两个拟阵在χ_max较大时给出FPRAS；4) 首次给出Rota基猜想渐近版本的多项式时间构造性算法，推广了Montgomery和Sauermann的结果到任意拟阵。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了拟阵交集着色问题的算法空白，首次实现了与n无关的多项式时间近似算法，并通过灵活分解概念建立了从拟阵着色到图着样的形式化归约框架，为该领域后续研究提供了新的技术工具。

Abstract: We study algorithmic matroid intersection coloring. Given $k$ matroids on a common ground set $U$ of $n$ elements, the goal is to partition $U$ into the fewest number of color classes, where each color class is independent in all matroids. It is known that $2χ_{\max}$ colors suffice to color the intersection of two matroids, $(2k-1)χ_{\max}$ colors suffice for general $k$, where $χ_{\max}$ is the maximum chromatic number of the individual matroids. However, these results are non-constructive, leveraging techniques such as topological Hall's theorem and Sperner's Lemma.
  We provide the first polynomial-time algorithms to color two or more general matroids where the approximation ratio depends only on $k$ and, in particular, is independent of $n$. For two matroids, we constructively match the $2χ_{\max}$ existential bound, yielding a 2-approximation for the Matroid Intersection Coloring problem. For $k$ matroids we achieve a $(k^2-k)χ_{\max}$ coloring, which is the first $O(1)$-approximation for constant $k$. Our approach introduces a novel matroidal structure we call a \emph{flexible decomposition}. We use this to formally reduce general matroid intersection coloring to graph coloring while avoiding the limitations of partition reduction techniques, and without relying on non-constructive topological machinery.
  Furthermore, we give a \emph{fully polynomial randomized approximation scheme} (FPRAS) for coloring the intersection of two matroids when $χ_{\max}$ is large. This yields the first polynomial-time constructive algorithm for an asymptotic variant of Rota's Basis Conjecture. This constructivizes Montgomery and Sauermann's recent asymptotic breakthrough and generalizes it to arbitrary matroids.

</details>


### [59] [On the Efficiency of Sinkhorn-Knopp for Entropically Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03787)
*Kun He*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了Sinkhorn-Knopp算法在处理含异常值矩阵时的收敛性理论，通过引入"well-boundedness"（良边界）概念，证明了在温和条件下算法收敛可达O(log n - log ε)迭代复杂度，完全独立于正则化参数与成本矩阵范数的乘积η||C||∞，远优于现有最坏情况界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Sinkhorn-Knopp算法的理论收敛保证在存在异常值时严重恶化，受限于全局最大正则化成本η||C||∞或矩阵最小-最大元素比ν，导致理论与实践之间存在根本性脱节。

Method: 引入"well-boundedness"（良边界）概念，这是一种局部批量质量属性，可将从极端异常值中严格隔离数据中行为良好的部分。通过预缩放（pre-scaling）步骤消除维度依赖，以及分析矩阵密度临界阈值来建立一般(u,v)缩放的相变理论。

Result: 1. 对于EOT，算法在O(log n - log ε)次迭代内恢复目标传输计划，完全独立于η||C||∞
2. 通过成本可忽略的预缩放步骤，可加速至严格维度无关的O(log(1/ε))
3. 对于一般(u,v)缩放，建立了由临界矩阵密度阈值主导的尖锐相变：密度超过阈值时，迭代复杂度严格独立于ν；低于阈值时，复杂度下界为Ω(n/ε)

Conclusion: Well-boundedness为异常值鲁棒的矩阵缩放提供了坚实的理论基础，揭示了矩阵密度与算法收敛行为之间的深层联系，并弥合了Sinkhorn-Knopp算法理论与实践之间的差距。

Abstract: The Sinkhorn--Knopp (SK) algorithm is a cornerstone method for matrix scaling and entropically regularized optimal transport (EOT). Despite its empirical efficiency, existing theoretical guarantees to achieve a target marginal accuracy $\varepsilon$ deteriorate severely in the presence of outliers, bottlenecked either by the global maximum regularized cost $η\|C\|_\infty$ (where $η$ is the regularization parameter and $C$ the cost matrix) or the matrix's minimum-to-maximum entry ratio $ν$. This creates a fundamental disconnect between theory and practice.
  In this paper, we resolve this discrepancy. For EOT, we introduce the novel concept of well-boundedness, a local bulk mass property that rigorously isolates the well-behaved portion of the data from extreme outliers. We prove that governed by this fundamental notion, SK recovers the target transport plan for a problem of dimension $n$ in $O(\log n - \log \varepsilon)$ iterations, completely independent of the regularized cost $η\|C\|_\infty$. Furthermore, we show that a virtually cost-free pre-scaling step eliminates the dimensional dependence entirely, accelerating convergence to a strictly dimension-free $O(\log(1/\varepsilon))$ iterations.
  Beyond EOT, we establish a sharp phase transition for general $(\boldsymbol{u},\boldsymbol{v})$-scaling governed by a critical matrix density threshold. We prove that when a matrix's density exceeds this threshold, the iteration complexity is strictly independent of $ν$. Conversely, when the density falls below this threshold, the dependence on $ν$ becomes unavoidable; in this sub-critical regime, we construct instances where SK requires $Ω(n/\varepsilon)$ iterations.

</details>


### [60] [SVD Provably Denoises Nearest Neighbor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03831)
*Ravindran Kannan,Kijun Shin,David Woodruff*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究高维低 subspace 中带有高斯噪声的最近邻搜索(NNS)问题。当数据位于 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 中未知 $k$ 维子空间($k \ll d$)且被方差为 $\sigma^2$ 的高斯噪声污染时，在假设第二最近邻距离至少是最近邻 $(1+\varepsilon)$ 倍的条件下，证明了三类结果：1) 当 $\sigma \in O(1/k^{1/4})$ 时，SVD 去噪即可正确恢复最近邻；2) 当 $\sigma \gg 1/k^{1/4}$ 时，最近邻不可识别，给出匹配下界；3) 当 $\sigma \gg 1/\sqrt{k}$ 时，噪声会主导 inter-point 距离，导致噪声数据中的最近邻与原始数据不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻搜索是机器学习和数据挖掘中的基础问题。现有方法在低维子空间结构的高维数据中面临挑战，特别是在噪声存在时如何可靠地恢复真实的最近邻关

Method: 使用奇异空间扰动边界、高斯集中性和球面对称性进行分析，通过 SVD 对低维子空间数据进行去噪处理。

Result: 1. 当 $\sigma \in O(1/k^{1/4})$ 时，SVD 去噪可证明恢复正确最近邻；2. 当 $\sigma \gg 1/k^{1/4}$ 时，最近邻不可识别（下界）；3. 当 $\sigma \gg 1/\sqrt{k}$ 时，噪声 $\sigma\sqrt{d}$ 超过数据点间距离，噪声数据最近邻与原始数据不同。

Conclusion: SVD 可在 naive 最近邻搜索失效的 regime 下识别正确最近邻。相比 Abdullah et al. 2014，不需要 $\sigma$ 至少为 $d$ 的逆多项式。

Abstract: We study the Nearest Neighbor Search (NNS) problem in a high-dimensional setting where data lies in a low-dimensional subspace and is corrupted by Gaussian noise. Specifically, we consider a semi-random model in which $n$ points from an unknown $k$-dimensional subspace of $\mathbb{R}^d$ ($k \ll d$) are perturbed by zero-mean $d$-dimensional Gaussian noise with variance $σ^2$ per coordinate. Assuming the second-nearest neighbor is at least a factor $(1+\varepsilon)$ farther from the query than the nearest neighbor, and given only the noisy data, our goal is to recover the nearest neighbor in the uncorrupted data. We prove three results. First, for $σ\in O(1/k^{1/4})$, simply performing SVD denoises the data and provably recovers the correct nearest neighbor of the uncorrupted data. Second, for $σ\gg 1/k^{1/4}$, the nearest neighbor in the uncorrupted data is not even identifiable from the noisy data in general, giving a matching lower bound and showing the necessity of this threshold for NNS. Third, for $σ\gg 1/\sqrt{k}$, the noise magnitude $σ\sqrt d$ significantly exceeds inter-point distances in the unperturbed data, and the nearest neighbor in the noisy data generally differs from that in the uncorrupted data. Thus, the first and third results together imply that SVD can identify the correct nearest neighbor even in regimes where naive nearest neighbor search on the noisy data fails. Compared to \citep{abdullah2014spectral}, our result does not require $σ$ to be at least an inverse polynomial in the ambient dimension $d$. Our analysis uses perturbation bounds for singular spaces together with Gaussian concentration and spherical symmetry. We also provide empirical results on real datasets supporting our theory.

</details>


### [61] [Uniform Sampling of Proper Graph Colorings via Soft Coloring and Partial Rejection Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03947)
*Sarat Moka,Ava Vahedi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于部分拒绝采样（PRS）的精确均匀采样算法，用于生成图的合法k-着色样本。与CFTP方法不同，该算法本质上是可并行的。通过引入"复杂度简化器"的混合变体，将全局采样问题分解为O(log n)规模的独立子问题，从而改进现有精确采样运行时间。递归应用可达O(L^{log* n} · nΔ)的时间复杂度，并猜测L有常数上界，暗示存在线性时间的并行精确着色算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色的精确均匀采样是组合采样领域的基本问题。传统方法如CFTP难以并行化，且运行时间受限于全局耦合开销。本文旨在突破这一瓶颈，设计一种可并行化的精确采样框架。

Method: 1) 核心方法：部分拒绝采样（PRS）框架；2) 创新点：引入"软松弛"技术，逐步收紧着色约束直至获得精确样本；3) 混合变体：将问题分解为O(log n)规模的独立子问题，形成"复杂度简化器"；4) 递归应用驱动运行时间优化。

Result: 运行时间达O(L^{log* n} · nΔ)，其中L为松弛层级数。对于k > 3Δ的情况，改进了现有最佳精确采样运行时间。模拟实验强有力支持L有常数上界的猜想——这将意味着存在线性时间的并行化精确着色算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为图着色精确采样提供了一条可并行化的新路径。"复杂度简化器"的设计思想具有一般性意义，可与其他精确采样器结合。L有常数上界的猜想若被证实，将是该领域的重大突破。

Abstract: We present a new algorithm for the exact uniform sampling of proper \(k\)-colorings of a graph on \(n\) vertices with maximum degree~\(Δ\). The algorithm is based on partial rejection sampling (PRS) and introduces a soft relaxation of the proper coloring constraint that is progressively tightened until an exact sample is obtained. Unlike coupling from the past (CFTP), the method is inherently parallelizable. We propose a hybrid variant that decomposes the global sampling problem into independent subproblems of size \(O(\log n)\), each solved by any existing exact sampler. This decomposition acts as a {\em complexity reducer}: it replaces the input size~\(n\) with \(O(\log n)\) in the component solver's runtime, so that any improvement in direct methods automatically yields a stronger result. Using an existing CFTP method as the component solver, this improves upon the best known exact sampling runtime for \(k>3Δ\). Recursive application of the hybrid drives the runtime to \(O(L^{\log^* n}\cdot nΔ)\), where \(L\) is the number of relaxation levels. We conjecture that \(L\) is bounded independently of~\(n\), which would yield a linear-time parallelizable algorithm for general graphs. Our simulations strongly support this conjecture.

</details>


### [62] [Online Graph Balancing and the Power of Two Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04159)
*Nikhil Bansal,Milind Prabhu,Sahil Singla,Siddharth M. Sundaram*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究在线图均衡问题在i.i.d.模型下，针对任意基准图G，设计O(log log n)竞争比的算法。证明了贪婪算法对某些不规则图表现不佳，但通过引入log-skewness概念和图分解技术，可将任意图分解为skew-biregular子结构，从而设计出最优的在线算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在经典在线图均衡问题中，贪婪算法对对抗性到达是O(log n)最优的，对完全图Kn的i.i.d.到达可达O(log log n)。研究者想知道：对任意基准图G，是否也能达到O(log log n)的竞争比？

Method: 引入log-skewness概念刻画图的不规则子结构；证明任意图可在O(log log n)个log-skewness尺度上分解为skew-biregular片；基于分解设计贪婪算法的变体

Result: 负面结果：存在轻度不规则图G，贪婪算法在i.i.d.到达下竞争比为Ω̃(log n)。正面结果：对任意基准图G，存在O(log log n)竞争比的在线算法，且该界在完全图Kn上下界已知不可改进。

Conclusion: 贪婪算法仅在正则图（如Kn）和对抗性到达模型下最优。通过图的log-skewness分解技术，可克服图的不规则性，在一般基准图上达到最优的O(log log n)竞争比。

Abstract: In the classic online graph balancing problem, edges arrive sequentially and must be oriented immediately upon arrival, to minimize the maximum in-degree. For adversarial arrivals, the natural greedy algorithm is $O(\log n)$-competitive, and this bound is the best possible for any algorithm, even with randomization. We study this problem in the i.i.d. model where a base graph $G$ is known in advance and each arrival is an independent uniformly random edge of $G$. This model generalizes the standard power-of-two choices setting, corresponding to $G = K_n$, where the greedy algorithm achieves an $O(\log\!\log n)$ guarantee. We ask whether a similar bound is possible for arbitrary base graphs.
  While the greedy algorithm is optimal for adversarial arrivals and also for i.i.d. arrivals from regular base graphs (such as $G = K_n$), we show that it can perform poorly in general: there exist mildly irregular graphs $G$ for which greedy is $\widetildeΩ(\log n)$-competitive under i.i.d. arrivals. In sharp contrast, our main result is an $O(\log\!\log n)$-competitive online algorithm for every base graph $G$; this is optimal up to constant factors, since an $Ω(\log\!\log n)$ lower bound already holds even for the complete graph $G = K_n$. The key new idea is a notion of log-skewness for graphs, which captures the irregular substructures in $G$ that force the offline optimum to be large. Moreover, we show that any base graph can be decomposed into ``skew-biregular'' pieces at only $O(\log\!\log n)$ scales of log-skewness, and use this to design a decomposition-based variant of greedy that is $O(\log\!\log n)$-competitive.

</details>


### [63] [DAG Covers: The Steiner Point Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04186)
*Sujoy Bhore,Hsien-Chih Chang,Jonathan Conroy,Arnold Filtser,Eunjin Oh,Nicole Wein,Da Wei Zheng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究Steiner DAG cover问题，即允许在DAG中添加Steiner点的DAG覆盖。对于 planar 有向图和低树宽有向图，给出了(1,2, Õ(n·tw))和(1+ε, 2, Õ_ε(n))的Steiner DAG cover构造，并证明Steiner与non-Steiner DAG cover之间存在显著差距——树宽为1的图若stretch t<2且额外边为亚二次，则需要Ω(log n)个DAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG cover研究（Assadi等STOC 25, Filtser SODA 26）仅考虑一般有向图，不允许Steiner点。本文认为在planar digraph和低树宽digraph这两类重要图类上，引入Steiner点可能带来更紧凑的覆盖，因此启动对Steiner DAG cover的系统研究。

Method: 对于低树宽有向图：利用树宽分解构造DAG；对于planar digraph：利用planar分离定理与多尺度分解技术；对于lower bound：利用树宽为1的特殊图类（近似为路或圈），通过信息论/组合论证证明non-Steiner的限制。

Result: 树宽为tw的任意有向图存在(1, 2, Õ(n·tw))-Steiner DAG cover；planar digraph存在(1+ε, 2, Õ_ε(n))-Steiner DAG cover。Lower bound：树宽为1的图若t<2且额外边为亚二次，则需要Ω(log n)个DAG。

Conclusion: Steiner点的引入在planar和低树宽图类上实现了常数stretch和线性额外边界，显著优于non-Steiner情况。Non-Steiner需要Ω(log n)个DAG才能达到t<2的stretch，凸显了Steiner点的关键作用。

Abstract: Given a weighted digraph $G$, a $(t,g,μ)$-DAG cover is a collection of $g$ dominating DAGs $D_1,\dots,D_g$ such that all distances are approximately preserved: for every pair $(u,v)$ of vertices, $\min_id_{D_i}(u,v)\le t\cdot d_{G}(u,v)$, and the total number of non-$G$ edges is bounded by $|(\cup_i D_i)\setminus G|\le μ$. Assadi, Hoppenworth, and Wein [STOC 25] and Filtser [SODA 26] studied DAG covers for general digraphs. This paper initiates the study of \emph{Steiner} DAG cover, where the DAGs are allowed to contain Steiner points.
  We obtain Steiner DAG covers on the important classes of planar digraphs and low-treewidth digraphs. Specifically, we show that any digraph with treewidth tw admits a $(1,2,\tilde{O}(n\cdot tw))$-Steiner DAG cover. For planar digraphs we provide a $(1+\varepsilon,2,\tilde{O}_\varepsilon(n))$-Steiner DAG cover.
  We also demonstrate a stark difference between Steiner and non-Steiner DAG covers. As a lower bound, we show that any non-Steiner DAG cover for graphs with treewidth $1$ with stretch $t<2$ and sub-quadratic number of extra edges requires $Ω(\log n)$ DAGs.

</details>


### [64] [String Representation in Suffixient Set Size Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04377)
*Hiroki Shibata,Hideo Bannai*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了字符串的重复性度量χ（定义为最小suffixient集的大小），并首次证明了每个字符串都存在大小为O(χ(w))的表示方案，填补了该领域长期以来的开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复性度量是压缩表示和索引数据结构的重要参数。χ已被广泛研究，但其可达性（reachability）问题——即每个字符串是否都存在大小为O(χ(w))的表示——长期悬而未决，这是一个理论与应用的关键问题。

Method: 提出了一个新的形式化模型——子串方程系统（Substring Equation System, SES），作为表示字符串重复结构的核心工具。

Result: 证明了每个字符串都存在一个大小为O(χ(w))的SES表示，这是该问题的首个解决方案。

Conclusion: 肯定地回答了χ的可达性问题，为压缩表示和字符串索引领域提供了新的理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Repetitiveness measures quantify how much repetitive structure a string contains and serve as parameters for compressed representations and indexing data structures. We study the measure $χ$, defined as the size of the smallest suffixient set. Although $χ$ has been studied extensively, its reachability, whether every string $w$ admits a string representation of size $O(χ(w))$ words, has remained an important open problem. We answer this question affirmatively by presenting the first such representation scheme. Our construction is based on a new model, the substring equation system (SES), and we show that every string admits an SES of size $O(χ(w))$.

</details>


### [65] [A characterization of one-sided error testable graph properties in bounded degeneracy graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04466)
*Oded Lachish,Amit Levi,Ilan Newman,Felix Reidl*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文完全刻画了p-退化图中可被单边错误测试器测试的图性质。研究表明，测试性本质上由禁止结构的连通性决定：当且仅当违规结构不能被分散到不相交的高度数邻域时，相应性质才是可测试的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: minor-closed族性质的单边错误可测试性已被完全刻画（恰好是那些可由有界大小禁止子图定义的性质），但更广泛的p-退化图类允许存在高度"hub"顶点，这些hub可以从局部探索中结构性地隐藏禁止子图，使得已有的刻画不再适用。

Method: 结构化刻画方法。通过分析禁止结构在p-退化图中的分布特性，特别是研究违规如何通过高连通性区域（hub）被隐藏或分散。

Result: 给出了p-退化图中单边错误可测试性质的完整结构化刻画。核心结论：测试性由禁止结构的连通性根本决定——当且仅当违规结构不能被分散到不相交的高度数邻域时，相应性质才是可测试的。

Conclusion: 精确划定了在随机邻居预言机模型下p-退化图中可测试性的结构边界，同时考虑了单个禁止子图的连通性及其定义性质的综合行为。这为理解局部探索范式下的图性质测试提供了完整的理论框架。

Abstract: We consider graph property testing in $p$-degenerate graphs under the random neighbor oracle model (Czumaj and Sohler, FOCS 2019). In this framework, a tester explores a graph by sampling uniform neighbors of vertices, and a property is testable with one-sided error if its query complexity is independent of the graph size. It is known that one-sided error testable properties for minor-closed families are exactly those that can be defined by forbidden subgraphs of bounded size. However, the much broader class of $p$-degenerate graphs allows for high-degree ``hubs" that can structurally hide forbidden subgraphs from local exploration.
  In this work, we provide a complete structural characterization of all properties testable with one-sided error in $p$-degenerate graphs. We show that testability is fundamentally determined by the connectivity of the forbidden structures: a property is testable if and only if its violations cannot be fragmented across disjoint high-degree neighborhoods. Our results define the exact structural boundary for testability under these constraints, accounting for both the connectivity of individual forbidden subgraphs and the collective behavior of the properties they define.

</details>


### [66] [Subset Balancing and Generalized Subset Sum via Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04656)
*Yiming Gao,Yansong Feng,Honggang Hu,Yanbin Pan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究子集平衡问题（Subset Balancing）和广义子集和问题（Generalized Subset Sum），通过将这些问题归约到格上的SVP_∞和CVP_∞问题，获得确定性算法时间复杂度为$\tilde{O}(2^{4.632n})$，随机算法为$\tilde{O}(2^{2.443n})$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子集平衡问题是计算数论和密码学中的基础问题。传统的中途相遇算法时间复杂度为$\tilde{O}(2^{n\log|C|/2})$，本文旨在突破这一已知下界限制。

Method: 通过将Subset Balancing问题归约到格上的SVP_∞（最短向量问题）和CVP_∞（最近向量问题）问题，利用格算法的指数级加速特性。

Result: 确定性算法运行时间为$\tilde{O}((6\sqrt{2\pi e})^n) \approx \tilde{O}(2^{4.632n})$，随机算法运行时间为$\tilde{O}(2^{2.443n})$。对于足够大的$d$，Subset Balancing可在多项式时间内求解。

Conclusion: 成功突破了传统中途相遇算法的$\tilde{O}(2^{n\log|C|/2})$下界，将系数集约束从box形式推广到任意中心对称凸体，为格密码分析提供新工具。

Abstract: We study the \emph{Subset Balancing} problem: given $\mathbf{x} \in \mathbb{Z}^n$ and a coefficient set $C \subseteq \mathbb{Z}$, find a nonzero vector $\mathbf{c} \in C^n$ such that $\mathbf{c}\cdot\mathbf{x} = 0$. The standard meet-in-the-middle algorithm runs in time $\tilde{O}(|C|^{n/2})=\tilde{O}(2^{n\log |C|/2})$, and recent improvements (SODA~2022, Chen, Jin, Randolph, and Servedio; STOC~2026, Randolph and Węgrzycki) beyond this barrier apply mainly when $d$ is constant.
  We give a reduction from Subset Balancing with $C = \{-d, \dots, d\}$ to a single instance of $\mathrm{SVP}_{\infty}$ in dimension $n+1$, which yields a deterministic algorithm with running time $\tilde{O}((6\sqrt{2πe})^n) \approx \tilde{O}(2^{4.632n})$, and a randomized algorithm with running time $\tilde{O}(2^{2.443n})$ (here $\tilde{O}$ suppresses $\operatorname{poly}(n)$ factors). We also show that for sufficiently large $d$, Subset Balancing is solvable in polynomial time. More generally, we extend the box constraint $[-d,d]^n$ to an arbitrary centrally symmetric convex body $K \subseteq \mathbb{R}^n$ with a deterministic $\tilde{O}(2^{c_K n})$-time algorithm, where $c_K$ depends only on the shape of $K$.
  We further study the \emph{Generalized Subset Sum} problem of finding $\mathbf{c} \in C^n$ such that $\mathbf{c} \cdot \mathbf{x} = τ$. For $C = \{-d, \dots, d\}$, we reduce the worst-case problem to a single instance of $\mathrm{CVP}_{\infty}$. Although no general single exponential time algorithm is known for exact $\mathrm{CVP}_{\infty}$, we show that in the average-case setting, for both $C = \{-d, \dots, d\}$ and $C = \{-d, \dots, d\} \setminus \{0\}$, the embedded instance satisfies a bounded-distance promise with high probability. This yields a deterministic algorithm running in time $\tilde{O}((18\sqrt{2πe})^n) \approx \tilde{O}(2^{6.217n})$.

</details>


### [67] [DAG Projections: Reducing Distance and Flow Problems to DAGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04752)
*Bernhard Haeupler,Yonggang Jiang,Thatchaphol Saranurak*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出DAG投影概念——一种可对原图距离或最大流进行近似的有向无环图。对于含n个顶点、m条边的有向图G，可构造仅需m^{1+o(1)}条边的DAG投影，实现(1+1/polylog(n))-近似全点对距离或n^{o(1)}-近似最大流，优于此前Ω(log n)的距离近似因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有类似结果在距离近似上存在Ω(log n)的近似因子瓶颈，且无最大流类型的先验结果。本文旨在构建更紧凑、更精确的DAG投影，以简化有向图算法并将困难问题归约为更简单的DAG问题。

Method: 构造性地给出DAG投影的O(m^{1+o(1)})时间算法，以及近乎最优的并行算法（m^{1+o(1)}工作量和m^{o(1)}深度）。通过DAG投影将有向图上的算法问题归约为DAG上的对应问题。

Result: 1. 距离近似：实现(1+1/polylog(n))-近似，优于此前Ω(log n)因子；2. 最大流：首次给出n^{o(1)}-近似类型结果；3. 应用：改进了距离保持器和单源最小割的最优结果，简化了跳点集和组合最大流算法的构造；4. 理论归约：将精确有向SSSP和最大流问题分别归约为更简单的无向SSSP、DAG近似等问题。

Conclusion: DAG投影可作为有向图到有向无环图的"桥梁"，将DAG上的高效算法移植到一般有向图，同时揭示了精确有向最短路径和最大流问题的计算复杂度下界可从相应DAG问题推导。

Abstract: We show that every directed graph $G$ with $n$ vertices and $m$ edges admits a directed acyclic graph (DAG) with $m^{1+o(1)}$ edges, called a DAG projection, that can either $(1+1/\text{polylog} (n))$-approximate distances between all pairs of vertices $(s,t)$ in $G$, or $n^{o(1)}$-approximate maximum flow between all pairs of vertex subsets $(S,T)$ in $G$. Previous similar results suffer a $Ω(\log n)$ approximation factor for distances [Assadi, Hoppenworth, Wein, STOC'25] [Filtser, SODA'26] and, for maximum flow, no prior result of this type is known.
  Our DAG projections admit $m^{1+o(1)}$-time constructions. Further, they admit almost-optimal parallel constructions, i.e., algorithms with $m^{1+o(1)}$ work and $m^{o(1)}$ depth, assuming the ones for approximate shortest path or maximum flow on DAGs, even when the input $G$ is not a DAG.
  DAG projections immediately transfer results on DAGs, usually simpler and more efficient, to directed graphs. As examples, we improve the state-of-the-art of $(1+ε)$-approximate distance preservers [Hoppenworth, Xu, Xu, SODA'25] and single-source minimum cut [Cheung, Lau, Leung, SICOMP'13], and obtain simpler construction of $(n^{1/3},ε)$-hop-set [Kogan, Parter, SODA'22] [Bernstein, Wein, SODA'23] and combinatorial max flow algorithms [Bernstein, Blikstad, Saranurak, Tu, FOCS'24] [Bernstein, Blikstad, Li, Saranurak, Tu, FOCS'25].
  Finally, via DAG projections, we reduce major open problems on almost-optimal parallel algorithms for exact single-source shortest paths (SSSP) and maximum flow to easier settings: (1) From exact directed SSSP to exact undirected ones, (2) From exact directed SSSP to $(1+1/\text{polylog}(n))$-approximation on DAGs, and (3) From exact directed maximum flow to $n^{o(1)}$-approximation on DAGs.

</details>

### [68] [VectraFlow: Long-Horizon Semantic Processing over Data and Event Streams with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03855)
*Shu Chen,Junhan Liu,Deepti Raghavan,Ugur Cetintemel*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: VectraFlow是一个语义流数据流引擎，通过结合LLM能力与传统CEP系统，实现对非结构化文本流的连续语义操作和时序模式检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM框架本质上是无状态、单次推理的；而传统CEP系统虽然支持时序模式检测，但假设输入为结构化类型化事件流，无法处理非结构化文本数据。这两个gap限制了在对非结构化流数据进行长期、有状态推理方面的应用。

Method: VectraFlow将传统关系运算符扩展为LLM驱动的执行模式，提供一套连续语义运算符（filter、map、aggregate、join、group-by、window），每个运算符支持基于LLM、基于embedding及混合实现的可配置吞吐量-准确性权衡。在此基础上，语义事件模式运算符将CEP提升到非结构化文档流层面，结合基于LLM的事件提取与基于NFA的时序规则匹配。

Result: 实现了面向自由文本流的连续语义运算符套件，支持灵活的性能-精度配置；语义事件模式运算符能够对语义事件序列进行有状态推理。

Conclusion: VectraFlow填补了LLM框架缺乏状态ful推理能力与传统CEP系统无法处理非结构化文本之间的空白，可应用于临床文档流等场景，实现从原始文本到事件序列匹配的全流程处理。

Abstract: Monitoring continuous data for meaningful signals increasingly demands long-horizon, stateful reasoning over unstructured streams. However, today's LLM frameworks remain stateless and one-shot, and traditional Complex Event Processing (CEP) systems, while capable of temporal pattern detection, assume structured, typed event streams that leave unstructured text out of reach. We demonstrate VectraFlow, a semantic streaming dataflow engine, to address both gaps. VectraFlow extends traditional relational operators with LLM-powered execution over free-text streams, offering a suite of continuous semantic operators -- filter, map, aggregate, join, group-by, and window -- each with configurable throughput-accuracy tradeoffs across LLM-based, embedding-based, and hybrid implementations. Building on this, a semantic event pattern operator lifts complex event processing to unstructured document streams, combining LLM-based event extraction with NFA-based temporal rule matching for stateful reasoning over sequences of semantic events. In this demonstration, users will interact with VectraFlow's live query interface to compose semantic pipelines over clinical document streams. Attendees will compile natural language intents into executable operator graphs, inspect intermediate stateful outputs, and observe end-to-end temporal pattern detection, from raw text to matched event cohorts.

</details>


### [69] [Version Control System for Data with MatrixOne](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03927)
*Hongshen Gou,Feng Tian,Long Wang,Nan Deng,Peng Xu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于MatrixOne云原生关系数据库的数据版本控制系统，利用不可变存储架构和多版本并发控制（MVCC）实现git风格的版本控制操作（clone、tag/branch、diff、merge、revert），可在TB级数据集上实现近瞬时性能，支持数据分支隔离实验、变更审查和CI/CD流程，并保证生产环境的数据一致性和服务连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有源代码版本控制系统（如Git）无法有效处理大规模数据的diff和merge操作，因为需要将整个数据集加载到内存中。同时，数据库系统虽具备强大的数据管理能力，但缺乏原生的版本控制操作支持。随着AI驱动的数据项目复杂度增加，迫切需要一个专为大规模数据设计的版本控制系统。

Method: 该系统基于MatrixOne的不可变存储架构和多版本并发控制（MVCC）机制实现。在MatrixOne的分布式架构中，数据以追加写（append-only）方式存储，每次变更生成新版本而非覆盖旧数据。系统将Git的核心概念（clone、branch、tag、diff、merge、revert）映射到数据库表的版本控制操作中，利用MVCC实现高效的版本查询和比较。

Result: 该版本控制系统支持完整的版本控制操作集，在TB级数据集上实现了近瞬时的操作性能。通过分支隔离，开发环境与生产环境在数据完整性和计算资源上相互独立。经验证的变更可通过原子事务发布到生产环境，确保数据一致性并避免服务中断。

Conclusion: 该系统使数据工程师能够采用成熟的软件工程工作流程，包括分支隔离实验、Pull Request变更审查和CI/CD流水线，同时保证了生产环境的安全性和连续性。这为大规模数据管理提供了基础设施级别的版本控制解决方案。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of artificial intelligence has elevated data to a cornerstone of modern software systems. As data projects become increasingly complex and dynamic, version control for data has become essential rather than merely convenient. Existing version control systems designed for source code are inadequate for large-scale data management, as they often require loading entire datasets into memory for diff and merge operations. Database systems, while providing robust data management capabilities, lack native support for version control operations such as diff and merge between data forks. We present a version control system for data implemented in MatrixOne, a cloud-native relational database system. Our system leverages MatrixOne's immutable storage architecture and multi-version concurrency control (MVCC) to enable git-like operations on database tables at scale. The system supports the complete spectrum of version control operations: clone, tag/branch, diff, merge, and revert, on terabyte-scale datasets with near-instantaneous performance. This version control system enables data engineers to adopt established software engineering workflows: creating branches for isolated experimentation, submitting pull requests for change review, and running CI/CD pipelines efficiently and safely. Changes in the development environment are isolated from production in both data integrity and computing resources. Verified changes can be published to production in atomic transactions, ensuring data consistency and avoiding service disruptions.

</details>


### [70] [Cardinality Estimation for High Dimensional Similarity Queries with Adaptive Bucket Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04603)
*Zhonghan Chen,Qintian Guo,Ruiyuan Zhang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于高维空间相似性搜索的基数估计框架，结合局部敏感哈希（LSH）与乘积量化技术，实现轻量级、易构建且高效的基数估计，并支持动态数据更新场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维空间相似性搜索中的基数估计面临效率和精度难以兼顾的挑战，传统的索引结构难以同时满足轻量化构建、准确估计和在线高效性的需求。

Method: 框架采用三层技术路径：(1) 利用LSH保持距离邻近性的空间划分；(2) 借鉴多探头LSH思想自适应探索相邻桶；(3) 采用渐进采样减少距离计算次数，并利用乘积量化中的非对称距离计算加速高维距离评估；(4) 设计增量更新算法以支持大规模动态数据场景。

Result: 实验表明，该方法能够准确估计相似性查询的基数，且在线效率满足实际应用需求。

Conclusion: 所提框架在静态数据集和动态更新场景下均表现出良好的基数估计精度和效率，具有较强的实用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we address the problem of cardinality estimation for similarity search in high-dimensional spaces. Our goal is to design a framework that is lightweight, easy to construct, and capable of providing accurate estimates with satisfying online efficiency. We leverage locality-sensitive hashing (LSH) to partition the vector space while preserving distance proximity. Building on this, we adopt the principles of classical multi-probe LSH to adaptively explore neighboring buckets, accounting for distance thresholds of varying magnitudes. To improve online efficiency, we employ progressive sampling to reduce the number of distance computations and utilize asymmetric distance computation in product quantization to accelerate distance calculations in high-dimensional spaces. In addition to handling static datasets, our framework includes updating algorithm designed to efficiently support large-scale dynamic scenarios of data updates.Experiments demonstrate that our methods can accurately estimate the cardinality of similarity queries, yielding satisfying efficiency.

</details>


### [71] [Query Optimization and Evaluation via Information Theory: A Tutorial](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04893)
*Mahmoud Abo Khamis,Hung Q. Ngo,Dan Suciu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于PANDA框架的教程论文，该框架用于在给定输入数据统计信息的情况下评估连接查询（Conjunctive Queries, CQ）。PANDA通过信息论紧上界技术推导中间关系基数，从而确定查询计划，实现了通用性与专用算法性能的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连接查询评估同时推广了图模式匹配、约束满足和统计推理等问题，具有极高的通用性。然而，这种通用性也带来了核心挑战：通用"元算法"难以匹敌针对固定查询设计的专用算法运行时间。查询优化问题本质上是：在给定查询Q和输入数据库统计信息S的情况下，如何最好地回答查询Q。

Method: PANDA框架的关键思想是推导出查询评估过程中产生的中间关系基数的信息论紧上界。这些上界决定了查询计划的成本，且查询计划本身直接从数学证明中推导得出。证明与算法的紧密耦合使PANDA既有原则性又强大。该框架融合了数据库理论、约束满足问题（CSP）和图算法领域的进展。

Result: 值得注意的是，这个通用算法能够匹配甚至超越针对同一问题的专用算法运行时间，包括利用快速矩阵乘法的算法。

Conclusion: 本文是关于PANDA框架的教程，通过具体示例说明关键思想，传达理论背后的主要直觉。

Abstract: Database theory is exciting because it studies highly general and practically useful abstractions. Conjunctive query (CQ) evaluation is a prime example: it simultaneously generalizes graph pattern matching, constraint satisfaction, and statistical inference, among others. This generality is both the strength and the central challenge of the field. The query optimization and evaluation problem is fundamentally a "meta-algorithm" problem: given a query $Q$ and statistics $\cal S$ about the input database, how should one best answer $Q$? Because the problem is so general, it is often impossible for such a meta-algorithm to match the runtimes of specialized algorithms designed for a fixed query -- or so it seemed.
  The past fifteen years have witnessed an exciting development in database theory: a general framework, called PANDA, that emerged from advances in database theory, constraint satisfaction problems (CSP), and graph algorithms, for evaluating conjunctive queries given input data statistics. The key idea is to derive information-theoretically tight upper bounds on the cardinalities of intermediate relations produced during query evaluation. These bounds determine the costs of query plans, and crucially, the query plans themselves are derived directly from the mathematical proof of the upper bound. This tight coupling of proof and algorithm is what makes PANDA both principled and powerful.
  Remarkably, this generic algorithm matches -- and in some cases subsumes -- the runtimes of specialized algorithms for the same problems, including algorithms that exploit fast matrix multiplication. This paper is a tutorial on the PANDA framework. We illustrate the key ideas through concrete examples, conveying the main intuitions behind the theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [Emergent Compositional Communication for Latent World Properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03266)
*Tomek Kaszyński*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体通信通过Gumbel-Softmax瓶颈和迭代学习，可从冻结视频特征中提取物理属性的离散组合表征，在无监督情况下实现近完美的位置解耦（PosDis=0.999）和组合性，在真实视频上验证了85.6%的质量比较准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索多智能体通信是否能够从冻结视频特征中提取不可见物理属性（弹性、摩擦、质量比）的离散组合表征，实现无监督的物理属性提取和通信协议学习。

Method: 使用Gumbel-Softmax瓶颈约束的多智能体通信框架，结合迭代学习（iterated learning）机制，使4个智能体通过无监督方式学习位置解耦的通信协议。控制实验排除了带宽和时间覆盖的干扰。因果干预实验验证单个属性的独立影响。对比DINOv2和V-JEPA 2两种视觉backbone在空间可视物理和动力学物理上的表现差异。

Result: 80个种子100%收敛到近完美组合性（PosDis=0.999，保留集98.3%）。因果干预显示目标属性约15%下降，非目标属性<3%。DINOv2在空间可视 ramp 物理上优于V-JEPA 2（98.3% vs 95.1%），V-JEPA 2在纯动力学碰撞物理上显著领先（87.4% vs 77.7%，d=2.74）。视频原生预训练是性能差异的根本原因（尺度匹配d=3.37，帧匹配d=6.53）。支持91.5%动作条件规划和反事实速度推理（r=0.780）。Physics 101真实视频验证：85.6%质量比较准确率，时间动力学贡献+11.2%，智能体缩放组合性达90%，因果干预扩展到真实视频（d=1.87, p=0.022）。

Conclusion: 多智能体结构和视频原生预训练是关键驱动力，而非带宽或时间覆盖。感知先验决定可通信内容：DINOv2擅长空间可视属性，V-JEPA 2擅长纯动态属性。该方法成功实现从冻结视频特征中无监督提取物理属性的组合式、表征学习。框架支持下游规划和反事实推理任务。

Abstract: Can multi-agent communication pressure extract discrete, compositional representations of invisible physical properties from frozen video features? We show that agents communicating through a Gumbel-Softmax bottleneck with iterated learning develop positionally disentangled protocols for latent properties (elasticity, friction, mass ratio) without property labels or supervision on message structure. With 4 agents, 100% of 80 seeds converge to near-perfect compositionality (PosDis=0.999, holdout 98.3%). Controls confirm multi-agent structure -- not bandwidth or temporal coverage -- drives this effect. Causal intervention shows surgical property disruption (~15% drop on targeted property, <3% on others). A controlled backbone comparison reveals that the perceptual prior determines what is communicable: DINOv2 dominates on spatially-visible ramp physics (98.3% vs 95.1%), while V-JEPA 2 dominates on dynamics-only collision physics (87.4% vs 77.7%, d=2.74). Scale-matched (d=3.37) and frame-matched (d=6.53) controls attribute this gap entirely to video-native pretraining. The frozen protocol supports action-conditioned planning (91.5%) with counterfactual velocity reasoning (r=0.780). Validation on Physics 101 real camera footage confirms 85.6% mass-comparison accuracy on unseen objects, temporal dynamics contributing +11.2% beyond static appearance, agent-scaling compositionality replicating at 90% for 4 agents, and causal intervention extending to real video (d=1.87, p=0.022).

</details>


### [73] [Scaling Teams or Scaling Time? Memory Enabled Lifelong Learning in LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03295)
*Shanglin Wu,Yuyang Luo,Yueqing Liang,Kaiwen Shi,Yanfang Ye,Ali Payani,Kai Shu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMA-Mem，一个支持灵活记忆拓扑的LLM多智能体终身记忆框架，在MultiAgentBench上验证其在长期任务中一致优于基线且降低成本。核心发现：团队规模与长期性能呈非单调关系，良好的记忆设计可使小团队超越大团队，凸显了记忆设计作为多智能体系统高效扩展路径的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究分别探讨了多智能体系统的两条扩展路径（增加智能体数量和积累经验），但缺乏对两者在现实成本约束下交互作用的理解。

Method: 提出LLMA-Mem终身记忆框架，支持灵活的记忆拓扑设计；在MultiAgentBench的编码、研究和数据库三类环境中进行评估。

Result: LLMA-Mem在长期任务中一致优于基线方法，同时降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 团队规模与长期性能呈非单调关系；较小的团队在记忆设计优化的情况下可以超越较大的团队；记忆设计是实现多智能体系统更有效、更高效扩展的实用路径。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems can scale along two distinct dimensions: by increasing the number of agents and by improving through accumulated experience over time. Although prior work has studied these dimensions separately, their interaction under realistic cost constraints remains unclear. In this paper, we introduce a conceptual scaling view of multi-agent systems that jointly considers team size and lifelong learning ability, and we study how memory design shares this landscape. To this end, we propose \textbf{LLMA-Mem}, a lifelong memory framework for LLM multi-agent systems under flexible memory topologies. We evaluate LLMA-Mem on \textsc{MultiAgentBench} across coding, research, and database environments. Empirically, LLMA-Mem consistently improves long-horizon performance over baselines while reducing cost. Our analysis further reveals a non-monotonic scaling landscape: larger teams do not always produce better long-term performance, and smaller teams can outperform larger ones when memory better supports the reuse of experience. These findings position memory design as a practical path for scaling multi-agent systems more effectively and more efficiently over time.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling Multi-agent Systems: A Smart Middleware for Improving Agent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03430)
*Charles Fleming,Ramana Kompella,Peter Bosch,Vijoy Pandey*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Cognitive Fabric Nodes (CFN)——一种新型中间件层，通过将Memory提升为活跃功能基质，并结合Topology Selection、Semantic Grounding、Security Policy Enforcement和Prompt Transformation四项能力，实现LLM多智能体系统间的智能通信协调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM多智能体系统存在四大局限：上下文碎片化、随机性幻觉、安全边界僵化、拓扑管理低效。传统消息队列或服务网格仅作为被动传输机制，无法满足复杂生态系统需求。

Method: CFN作为主动智能中介拦截并重写智能体间通信。核心创新在于将Memory从存储单元升级为功能基质，驱动五项协同能力：Memory、Topology Selection、Semantic Grounding、Security Policy Enforcement、Prompt Transformation。各功能模块采用强化学习(RL)和优化算法实现动态性能调优。

Result: 在HotPotQA和MuSiQue多智能体环境下，CFN相比直接通信提升性能超过10%。

Conclusion: CFN使个体智能体保持轻量化，同时确保生态系统具备一致性、安全性和语义对齐能力。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) based Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) evolve from experimental pilots to complex, persistent ecosystems, the limitations of direct agent-to-agent communication have become increasingly apparent. Current architectures suffer from fragmented context, stochastic hallucinations, rigid security boundaries, and inefficient topology management. This paper introduces Cognitive Fabric Nodes (CFN), a novel middleware layer that creates an omnipresent "Cognitive Fabric" between agents. Unlike traditional message queues or service meshes, CFNs are not merely pass-through mechanisms; they are active, intelligent intermediaries.
  Central to this architecture is the elevation of Memory from simple storage to an active functional substrate that informs four other critical capabilities: Topology Selection, Semantic Grounding, Security Policy Enforcement, and Prompt Transformation. We propose that each of these functions be governed by learning modules utilizing Reinforcement Learning (RL) and optimization algorithms to improve system performance dynamically. By intercepting, analyzing, and rewriting inter-agent communication, the Cognitive Fabric ensures that individual agents remain lightweight while the ecosystem achieves coherence, safety, and semantic alignment.
  We evaluate the effectiveness of the CFN on the HotPotQA and MuSiQue datasets in a multi-agent environment and demonstrate that the CFN improves performance by more than 10\% on both datasets over direct agent to agent communication.

</details>


### [75] [When AI Agents Disagree Like Humans: Reasoning Trace Analysis for Human-AI Collaborative Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03796)
*Michał Wawer,Jarosław A. Chudziak*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文提出在LLM多智能体系统中，分歧可以被视为有价值的信号而非需要消除的噪音。研究聚焦仇恨言论审核领域，使用包含推理痕迹的五个视角差异化智能体，通过基于推理相似性和结论一致性的四类 Taxonomy 对分歧进行分类，发现智能体是否达成一致裁决这一结构化特征比推理发散程度更能预测人类标注者之间的冲突（效应量大，d>0.8）。研究结论支持从"寻求共识"向"暴露不确定性"的智能体设计范式转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多智能体系统中，分歧被视为需要通过共识机制消除的噪音。然而在仇恨言论审核这类依赖文化语境和个人价值判断的任务中，人类标注者本身也存在高度合法分歧。作者假设：若智能体推理过程相似但结论不同（收敛性分歧），则表明存在真实的价值多元性——这正是人类也难以裁决的问题。这一视角挑战了当前追求共识的多智能体设计范式。

Method: 研究使用Measuring Hate Speech语料库，嵌入五个视角差异化智能体的推理痕迹，依据推理相似性（相似/不同）和结论一致性（一致/不一致）构建四类分歧分类法，对智能体分歧模式进行分类和分析。

Result: 推理发散程度对人类标注者冲突的预测能力较弱，但智能体分歧的结构特征具有额外预测力：智能体对裁决达成一致时，人类分歧明显低于未达成一致时，效应量大（d>0.8），经多重比较校正后仍显著。Taxonomy分类与人类分歧模式呈现相关性。

Conclusion: 多智能体系统设计应从"共识寻求"转向"不确定性暴露"，分歧的结构特征（而非程度）应指导何时需要人类介入判断。

Abstract: When LLM-based multi-agent systems disagree, current practice treats this as noise to be resolved through consensus. We propose it can be signal. We focus on hate speech moderation, a domain where judgments depend on cultural context and individual value weightings, producing high legitimate disagreement among human annotators. We hypothesize that convergent disagreement, where agents reason similarly but conclude differently, indicates genuine value pluralism that humans also struggle to resolve. Using the Measuring Hate Speech corpus, we embed reasoning traces from five perspective-differentiated agents and classify disagreement patterns using a four-category taxonomy based on reasoning similarity and conclusion agreement. We find that raw reasoning divergence weakly predicts human annotator conflict, but the structure of agent discord carries additional signal: cases where agents agree on a verdict show markedly lower human disagreement than cases where they do not, with large effect sizes (d>0.8) surviving correction for multiple comparisons. Our taxonomy-based ordering correlates with human disagreement patterns. These preliminary findings motivate a shift from consensus-seeking to uncertainty-surfacing multi-agent design, where disagreement structure - not magnitude - guides when human judgment is needed.

</details>


### [76] [Symbolic-Vector Attention Fusion for Collective Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03955)
*Hongwei Xu*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出 Symbolic-Vector Attention Fusion (SVAF)，一种用于多智能体集体智能的两级耦合引擎的内容评估组件。SVAF 将智能体间信号分解为7个语义字段，通过学习到的融合门评估各字段，生成跨域知识融合。实验在237K样本、273个叙事场景上达到78.7%三类分类准确率，并在7节点异构平台（macOS/iOS/web）上完成实时部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机制无法让接收方评估哪些信号维度值得关注。当自主智能体观察共享环境时，它们交换的信号混合了相关和不相关维度。现有方法缺乏选择性机制来处理这种信息过载问题。

Method: SVAF 将每个智能体间信号分解为7种类型化语义字段，通过学习到的融合门独立评估每个字段，生成"混音"（remix）——来自两个域交叉的新知识。采用带通模型产生四种结果：冗余、对齐、守卫、拒绝，解决选择性和冗余问题。融合门独立发现跨域相关性层级：情绪字段在epoch 1即成为最高权重字段。

Result: 在237K样本、273个叙事场景训练后，SVAF达到78.7%三类分类准确率。情绪作为最高权重字段的发现与独立机制证据一致——LLM情绪表示沿效价-唤醒轴结构嵌入。完整网格认知循环在7节点异构平台上实时验证成功。

Conclusion: SVAF 成功解决了多智能体通信中的选择性信息吸收问题。情绪作为首要关注维度的发现表明，情感计算在跨域知识融合中具有核心地位。该工作为集体智能系统中内容评估与时序动力学的解耦设计提供了有效范式。

Abstract: When autonomous agents observe different domains of a shared environment, each signal they exchange mixes relevant and irrelevant dimensions. No existing mechanism lets the receiver evaluate which dimensions to absorb.
