We introduce Symbolic-Vector Attention Fusion (SVAF), the content-evaluation half of a two-level coupling engine for collective intelligence. SVAF decomposes each inter-agent signal into 7 typed semantic fields, evaluates each through a learned fusion gate, and produces a remix -- new knowledge from the intersection of two domains. A band-pass model yields four outcomes (redundant, aligned, guarded, rejected), solving both selectivity and redundancy. The fusion gate independently discovers a cross-domain relevance hierarchy: mood emerges as the highest-weight field by epoch 1, before accuracy plateaus -- consistent with independent mechanistic evidence that LLM emotion representations are structurally embedded along valence-arousal axes. SVAF forms Layer 4 of the Mesh Memory Protocol (MMP); the other half of the coupling engine is a per-agent Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) neural network at Layer 6, whose learned per-neuron time constants (tau) create the temporal dynamics from which collective intelligence emerges: fast neurons synchronise affect across agents in seconds, while slow neurons preserve domain expertise indefinitely. SVAF determines what enters each agent's cognitive state; CfC determines how that state evolves. Trained on 237K samples from 273 narrative scenarios, SVAF achieves 78.7% three-class accuracy. We verify the complete mesh cognition loop -- from per-field evaluation through remix, CfC state evolution, tau-modulated peer blending, and autonomous action -- in a live deployment with 7 nodes across macOS, iOS, and web.

</details>


### [77] [Agentization of Digital Assets for the Agentic Web: Concepts, Techniques, and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04226)
*Linyao Chen,Bo Huang,Qinlao Zhao,Shuai Shao,Zhi Han,Zicai Cui,Ziheng Zhang,Guangtao Zeng,Wenzheng Tang,Yikun Wang,Yuanjian Zhou,Zimian Peng,Yong Yu,Weiwen Liu,Hiroki Kobayashi,Weinan Zhang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出A2A-Agentization框架，将数字资产自动转化为Agent以推动Agentic Web发展，并构建了首个评估Agent化质量的基准测试A2A-Agentization Bench。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic Web作为通过自主、目标驱动交互重新定义互联网的新范式，数字资产作为其基础语义原语至关重要。然而，缺乏自动化Agent生成方法限制了数字资产的广泛使用和Agentic Web的推进。

Method: 1) 形式化定义A2A-Agentization过程，将其分解为关键阶段并识别技术障碍；2) 在A2A协议基础上开发Agentization Agent；3) 提出A2A-Agentization Bench基准测试，从保真度和互操作性两个维度评估Agent化质量。

Result: 实验表明该方法能有效激活数字资产的功能能力，并实现可互操作的A2A多Agent协作。

Conclusion: 该工作将进一步促进数字资产向Agentic Web生态系统的可扩展、标准化集成。

Abstract: Agentic Web, as a new paradigm that redefines the internet through autonomous, goal-driven interactions, plays an important role in group intelligence. As the foundational semantic primitives of the Agentic Web, digital assets encapsulate interactive web elements into agents, which expand the capacities and coverage of agents in agentic web. The lack of automated methodologies for agent generation limits the wider usage of digital assets and the advancement of the Agentic Web. In this paper, we first formalize these challenges by strictly defining the A2A-Agentization process, decomposing it into critical stages and identifying key technical hurdles on top of the A2A protocol. Based on this framework, we develop an Agentization Agent to agentize digital assets for the Agentic Web. To rigorously evaluate this capability, we propose A2A-Agentization Bench, the first benchmark explicitly designed to evaluate agentization quality in terms of fidelity and interoperability. Our experiments demonstrate that our approach effectively activates the functional capabilities of digital assets and enables interoperable A2A multi-agent collaboration. We believe this work will further facilitate scalable and standardized integration of digital assets into the Agentic Web ecosystem.

</details>


### [78] [Decentralized Ergodic Coverage Control in Unknown Time-Varying Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04280)
*Maria G. Mendoza,Victoria Marie Tuck,Chinmay Maheshwari,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种去中心化多智能体覆盖框架，用于在部分可观测的未知时变环境中进行自适应覆盖控制。该框架通过高斯过程更新重要性地图的信念，并利用马尔可夫链转移模型实现自适应遍历策略，使智能体能够动态平衡探索与利用，应对灾难场景中的环境演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾难响应中的态势感知需要在探索未观测区域与持续监控感兴趣区域(ROI)之间取得平衡。现有方法假设重要性地图已知、需要集中协调或假设环境静态，难以应对现实灾难场景中有限感知、时变信息地图和动态演化的挑战。

Method: 提出去中心化多智能体覆盖框架，包含：(1)基于高斯过程的在线信念更新，用于追踪时变重要性地图；(2)马尔可夫链转移模型实现的适应性遍历策略，使智能体按估计重要性比例分配时间，同时保持足够探索以检测环境变化。

Result: 与替代覆盖策略相比，该框架在模拟灾难演化场景中展现出更好的适应性和瞬态性能，能够有效响应环境变化。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了未知时变分布与部分可观测环境下多智能体覆盖的联合挑战，在去中心化设置中实现了对动态场景的自适应响应。

Abstract: A key challenge in disaster response is maintaining situational awareness of an evolving landscape, which requires balancing exploration of unobserved regions with sustained monitoring of changing Regions of Interest (ROIs). Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) have emerged as an effective response tool, particularly in applications like environmental monitoring and search-and-rescue, due to their ability to provide aerial coverage, withstand hazardous conditions, and navigate quickly and flexibly. However, efficient and adaptable multi-robot coverage with limited sensing in disaster settings and evolving time-varying information maps remains a significant challenge, necessitating better methods for UAVs to continuously adapt their trajectories in response to changes. In this paper, we propose a decentralized multi-agent coverage framework that serves as a high-level planning strategy for adaptive coverage in unknown, time-varying environments under partial observability. Each agent computes an adaptive ergodic policy, implemented via a Markov-chain transition model, that tracks a continuously updated belief over the underlying importance map. Gaussian Processes are used to perform those online belief updates. The resulting policy drives agents to spend time in ROIs proportional to their estimated importance, while preserving sufficient exploration to detect and adapt to time-varying environmental changes. Unlike existing approaches that assume known importance maps, require centralized coordination, or assume a static environment, our framework addresses the combined challenges of unknown, time-varying distributions in a more realistic decentralized and partially observable setting. We compare against alternative coverage strategies and analyze our method's response to simulated disaster evolution, highlighting its improved adaptability and transient performance in dynamic scenarios.

</details>


### [79] [Agentic Federated Learning: The Future of Distributed Training Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04895)
*Rafael O. Jarczewski,Gabriel U. Talasso,Leandro Villas,Allan M. de Souza*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Agentic-FL通过引入基于大语言模型的智能体(Agent)来革新联邦学习范式,实现服务器端偏差缓解和客户端隐私预算动态管理,同时指出多智能体系统的可靠性与安全挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习采用静态优化方法,无法适应客户端的随机异构性和系统动态变化,导致资源利用不足和系统性偏差。

Method: 提出Agentic-FL框架,部署基于语言模型的智能体:服务器端智能体通过情境推理缓解选择偏差;客户端智能体作为本地守护者,动态管理隐私预算并根据硬件约束调整模型复杂度。

Result: 证明了智能体驱动的自主编排可有效解决技术低效问题,推动FL向去中心化生态系统演进。

Conclusion: Agentic-FL为联邦学习开辟了新方向,支持自主协商协作、激励模型市场和算法公平,但需解决大模型幻觉问题和多智能体系统的安全挑战。

Abstract: Although Federated Learning (FL) promises privacy and distributed collaboration, its effectiveness in real-world scenarios is often hampered by the stochastic heterogeneity of clients and unpredictable system dynamics. Existing static optimization approaches fail to adapt to these fluctuations, resulting in resource underutilization and systemic bias. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift towards Agentic-FL, a framework where Language Model-based Agents (LMagents) assume autonomous orchestration roles. Unlike rigid protocols, we demonstrate how server-side agents can mitigate selection bias through contextual reasoning, while client-side agents act as local guardians, dynamically managing privacy budgets and adapting model complexity to hardware constraints. More than just resolving technical inefficiencies, this integration signals the evolution of FL towards decentralized ecosystems, where collaboration is negotiated autonomously, paving the way for future markets of incentive-based models and algorithmic justice. We discuss the reliability (hallucinations) and security challenges of this approach, outlining a roadmap for resilient multi-agent systems in federated environments.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [Personalized AI Practice Replicates Learning Rate Regularity at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03246)
*Jocelyn Beauchesne,Christine Maroti,Jeshua Bratman,Jerome Pesenti,Laurence Holt,Alex Tambellini,Allison McGrath,Matthew Guo,Sarah Peterson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究利用180万条学生交互数据验证学习率的普遍规律：学生初始知识差异大，但学习速率高度一致。Campus AI平台自动生成知识组件和练习，通过加性因素模型分析证明自动化系统可实现与专家设计相当的个性化学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究发现在不同教育情境下学生学习率存在一致性，但这些发现依赖于手动认知建模。本研究旨在使用大规模自动化系统验证这一核心发现，并探索无需复杂认知建模即可测量学习参数的可行方案。

Method: 采用Campus AI数字平台自动生成知识组件(KCs)和对应练习（经人工专家验证），使用加性因素模型(Additive Factors Models)和混合效应逻辑斯蒂回归分析1.8百万条学生交互数据（筛选后36.6万条）。

Result: 主要发现：（1）学生初始知识差异显著（四分位距2.78-12.18次练习达到80%掌握），但学习率高度一致（四分位距7.01-8.25次）；（2）自动化系统中学生达到80%掌握的中位数为7.22次练习，与专家设计课程的6.54次相当。

Conclusion: 自动化、科学驱动的内容生成可以有效支持大规模个性化学习，证实了学习率规律在自动化教育系统中的普适性。

Abstract: Recent research demonstrated that students exhibit consistent learning rates across diverse educational contexts. We test these findings using a dataset of 1.8 million (366k post-filtering) student interactions from the digital platform Campus AI providing further evidence to the observation of regularity in learning rate among students. Unlike prior work requiring manual cognitive modeling, Campus AI automatically generates Knowledge Components (KCs) and corresponding exercises, both of which are validated by human experts. This one-to-many mapping facilitates the application of Additive Factors Models to measure learning parameters without complex cognitive modeling.
  Using mixed-effects logistic regression, we confirmed the core finding of prior work: students displayed substantial variation in initial knowledge ($\text{IQR} = [2.78, 12.18]$ practice opportunities to reach 80% mastery) but remarkably consistent learning rates ($\text{IQR} = [7.01, 8.25]$ opportunities). Furthermore, students using this fully automated system achieved 80% mastery in a median of 7.22 practice opportunities, comparable to the 6.54 reported for expert-designed curricula. These results suggest that automated, science-grounded content generation can support effective personalized learning at scale. Data and code are publicly available. https://github.com/Campus-edu-AI/learning-rate

</details>


### [81] [Classifying Problem and Solution Framing in Congressional Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03247)
*Misha Melnyk,Mitchell Dolny,Joshua D. Elkind,A. Michael Tjhin,Saisha Chebium,Blake VanBerlo,Annelise Russell,Michelle M. Buehlmann,Jesse Hoey*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究基于"垃圾筒"模型，对美国参议员推文进行问题导向和方案导向的自动分类研究。利用1.68m条推文数据集，由政策专家标注3967条推文作为训练数据，采用BERTweet Base模型进行监督学习分类，最终在三类（问题、方案、其他）分类任务中达到0.8以上的加权F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政策制定过程中问题流与方案流的区分是理解政策过程的关键，但人工标注大规模政策文本效率低下。本研究旨在开发自动化方法对参议员推文进行问题/方案分类，以支持对政策过程的实证研究。

Method: 收集1.68m条美国参议员推文，由两位政策专家标注3967条样本（问题/方案/其他三分类）。采用BERTweet Base预训练模型进行监督学习训练，按60/20/20比例划分训练/验证/测试集，通过F1分数迭代优化模型和超参数。

Result: 基于BERTweet Base模型在交叉验证中取得三类分类任务平均加权F1分数超过0.8的分类性能。

Conclusion: 自动化文本分类方法可有效应用于政策流分类任务，BERTweet Base模型能够较好捕捉参议员推文中的问题与方案语义特征，为大规模政策文本分析提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Policy setting in the USA according to the ``Garbage Can'' model differentiates between ``problem'' and ``solution'' focused processes. In this paper, we study a large dataset of US Senator postings on Twitter (1.68m tweets in total). Our objective is to develop an automated method to label Senatorial posts as either in the problem or solution streams. Two academic policy experts labeled a subset of 3967 tweets as either problem, solution, or other (anything not problem or solution). We split off a subset of 500 tweets into a test set, with the remaining 3467 used for training. During development, this training set was further split by 60/20/20 proportions for fitting, validation, and development test sets. We investigated supervised learning methods for building problem/solution classifiers directly on the training set, evaluating their performance in terms of F1 score on the validation set, allowing us to rapidly iterate through models and hyperparameters, achieving an average weighted F1 score of above 0.8 on cross validation across the three categories using a BERTweet Base model.

</details>


### [82] [Systematic Review of Academic Procrastination Interventions in Computing Higher Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03248)
*Daniel Cheng,Oscar Heath,Daniyaal Farooqi,Evelyn Chou,Alice Gao,Jonathan Calver*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 一项系统性文献综述，涵盖过去十年间发表的19篇文章，研究针对高等教育计算机专业学生学术拖延症的干预措施效果。综述发现，清晰的时间结构干预、支持性设计优于惩罚性设计，且干预效果因任务类型和学生个体差异而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术拖延在计算机教育中普遍存在，但干预措施的有效性证据分散在不同设计和情境中，缺乏系统性整合。

Method: 系统性文献综述法，纳入19篇实证研究文章，采用元分析框架整合证据。

Result: 研究发现四类干预机制（结构性、反馈性、动机性、自我调节性），其中清晰时间结构干预能促进学生更早开始任务、更均匀分配工作量；干预效果受任务结构调节（长周期、多步骤任务获益更大）；支持性设计优于惩罚性/限制性设计；统一干预对不同学生效果不均。

Conclusion: 计算机教育中学术拖延干预应设计为结构化、支持性、个性化的方案；强调时间管理机制和任务分解的重要性；建议根据具体课程任务特点定制干预策略。

Abstract: Academic procrastination is a persistent challenge in computing education, yet evidence on the effectiveness of course-level interventions remains fragmented across diverse designs and contexts. We present a systematic literature review of studies published in the past decade that empirically examine interventions to reduce academic procrastination among post-secondary computing students. Evidence from 19 articles examines interventions that target procrastination through structural, feedback-based, motivational, and self-regulatory mechanisms. Our findings suggest that interventions introducing clear temporal structure consistently promote earlier starts and more distributed work, which act as key mediators of performance gains. The magnitude of these gains depends strongly on task structure, with greater benefits for long-horizon, multi-step assignments than for short, routine tasks. Moreover, supportive designs reliably outperform punitive or restrictive schemes, while uniform interventions yield uneven benefits across students. This review highlights the importance of designing structured, supportive, and personalized interventions to address procrastination in computing education.

</details>


### [83] [BLK-Assist: A Methodological Framework for Artist-Led Co-Creation with Generative AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03249)
*Daniel Grimes,Rachel M. Harrison*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出BLK-Assist，一个用于艺术家特定扩散模型微调的模块化框架，采用参数高效方法（LoRA）进行概念草图生成、LayerDiffuse技术生成透明资产、以及混合Real-ESRGAN与纹理条件扩散进行高分辨率输出。该框架以专业艺术家的专有语料库为案例研究，实现了隐私保护和基于同意的人机协作创作流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI生成内容面临版权、隐私和风格保真度等挑战，专业艺术家的专有创作语料库难以被合法利用。现有方法缺乏针对特定艺术家风格的参数高效微调方案，且难以在保持风格一致性的同时实现隐私保护和知情同意机制。

Method: 框架包含三个组件：BLK-Conceptor使用LoRA适配器进行概念草图生成；BLK-Stencil基于LayerDiffuse实现透明度保持的资产生成；BLK-Upscale采用混合Real-ESRGAN与纹理条件扩散进行高分辨率输出。研究详细记录了数据集构成、预处理流程、训练配置和推理工作流。

Result: 验证了参数高效微调方法在维持源语料库风格保真度方面的有效性，框架可使用公开模型进行复现，实现了隐私保护和基于同意的创作流程，并具有向其他艺术家推广的可行性。

Conclusion: BLK-Assist为专业艺术家提供了一个隐私保护、基于同意的人机协作创作框架，在保持源语料库风格保真度的同时，为扩散模型的艺术家特异性微调提供了可复现的模块化解决方案，具备向其他艺术家和类似约束场景的扩展潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents BLK-Assist, a modular framework for artist-specific fine-tuning of diffusion models using parameter-efficient methods. The system is implemented as a case study with a single professional artist's proprietary corpus and consists of three components: BLK-Conceptor (LoRA-adapted conceptual sketch generation), BLK-Stencil (LayerDiffuse-based transparency-preserving asset generation), and BLK-Upscale (hybrid Real-ESRGAN and texture-conditioned diffusion for high-resolution outputs). We document dataset composition, preprocessing, training configurations, and inference workflows to enable reproducibility with publicly available models to illustrate a privacy-preserving, consent-based approach to human-AI co-creation that maintains stylistic fidelity to the source corpus and can be adapted for other artists under similar constraints.

</details>


### [84] [Ethical Implications of Training Deceptive AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03250)
*Jason Starace,Bert Baumgaertner,Terence Soule*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"欺骗研究等级"(DRL)框架,用于规范AI欺骗行为研究。该框架借鉴生物安全等级制度,从五个维度(支柱含义、严重性、可逆性、规模、脆弱性)对欺骗算法研究进行风险分级,并为DRL-3及以上级别设置双重开发要求。通过8个案例研究验证框架可行性,发现生态有效性是分类等级的可靠指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟AI法案禁止部署欺骗性AI系统,但明确豁免研发活动,导致研究阶段缺乏规范框架。大型语言模型已能策略性误导、维持欺骗策略并在多智能体系统中协调欺骗,而现有监管空白无法有效管理这一风险。

Method: 提出DRL框架,模型参考生物安全等级制度,按风险特征而非研究者意图对欺骗机制研究进行分类。分类基于AI4People伦理框架的五个维度:支柱含义、严重性、可逆性、规模、脆弱性。采用"最高维度获胜"原则分配四个风险等级,配套措施从DRL-1的标准文档到DRL-4的监管通知和第三方安全审计。DRL-3及以上要求同步开发检测和缓解方法。

Result: 框架成功应用于涵盖全部四个等级的8个案例研究。发现欺骗机制的生态有效性可作为分类等级的一致性、非独立性指标。

Conclusion: DRL框架旨在填补监管部署与无序研究之间的治理空白,为有益应用和防御性研究提供与潜在危害相称的安全保障。

Abstract: Deceptive behavior in AI systems is no longer theoretical: large language models strategically mislead without producing false statements, maintain deceptive strategies through safety training, and coordinate deception in multi-agent settings. While the European Union's AI Act prohibits deployment of deceptive AI systems, it explicitly exempts research and development, creating a necessary but unstructured space in which no established framework governs how deception research should be conducted or how risk should scale with capability. This paper proposes a Deception Research Levels (DRL) framework, a classification system for deceptive algorithm research modeled on the Biosafety Level system used in biological research. The DRL framework classifies research by risk profile rather than researcher intent, assessing deceptive mechanisms across five dimensions grounded in the AI4People ethical framework: Pillar Implication, Severity, Reversibility, Scale, and Vulnerability. Classification follows a ``highest dimension wins'' approach, assigning one of four risk levels with cumulative safeguards ranging from standard documentation at DRL-1 to regulatory notification and third-party security audits at DRL-4. A dual-development mandate at DRL-3 and above requires that detection and mitigation methods be developed alongside any deceptive capability. We apply the framework to eight case studies spanning all four levels and demonstrate that ecological validity of the deceptive mechanism emerges as a consistent, non-independent indicator of classification level. The DRL framework is intended to fill the governance gap between regulated deployment and unstructured research, supporting both beneficial applications and defensive research under conditions where safeguards are proportional to the potential for harm.

</details>


### [85] [The Algorithmic Blind Spot: Bias, Moral Status, and the Future of Robot Rights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03251)
*Rahulrajan Karthikeyan,Moses Boudourides*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"算法盲点"(algorithmic blind spot)概念,指AI伦理讨论过度关注未来人工智能的道德地位,而忽视当前算法系统已对人类造成的非对称分布的实际危害。作者通过分析机器人权利文献与经验性算法偏见证据的对比,揭示这种话语-结构模式如何遮蔽现有不公、分散责任并阻碍问责机制。文章主张重新将伦理评估聚焦于人类影响、制度责任和当前运行的算法系统治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI伦理讨论存在明显失衡：大量哲学思辨聚焦于未来人工智能的道德地位和法律权利,而对已嵌入社会、法律和经济制度中的算法系统所造成的经验性危害关注不足。这种话语-结构性的不对称性可能导致对现实不公的忽视。

Method: 1) 概念化"算法盲点"现象；2) 分析机器人权利文献的主要脉络；3) 将其与就业、刑事司法、监控和人脸识别等领域的算法偏见经验证据进行对比；4) 引入批判性评估AI伦理话语的概念框架。

Result: 研究表明：对假设性未来实体的伦理执念会遮蔽现实不公、分散责任并阻碍问责与救济机制。分析揭示了话语优先序如何系统性地边缘化受影响群体。

Conclusion: AI伦理需要重新平衡：不是拒绝探讨人工智能道德地位的哲学问题,而是强调伦理优先性和时序安排的重要性。核心路径是重新聚焦人类影响评估、制度责任认定和当前运行算法系统的治理,使伦理反思与其即时社会后果更紧密对齐。

Abstract: Contemporary debates in AI ethics increasingly foreground the prospective moral status of artificial intelligence and the possibility of extending moral or legal rights to artificial agents. While such discussions raise substantive philosophical questions, they often proceed alongside a comparatively limited engagement with the empirically documented harms generated by algorithmic systems already embedded within social, legal, and economic institutions. We conceptualize this asymmetry as an algorithmic blind spot: a discursive-structural pattern in which disproportionate ethical investment in speculative future artificial agents marginalizes empirically documented and asymmetrically distributed harms affecting human populations. The paper analyzes prominent strands of the robot rights literature and juxtaposes them with empirical evidence of algorithmic bias and harm across domains including employment, criminal justice, surveillance, and facial recognition. It demonstrates how ethical preoccupation with hypothetical future entities can obscure existing injustices, diffuse responsibility, and impede mechanisms of accountability and redress. Without rejecting philosophical inquiry into the moral status of artificial systems, the paper instead emphasizes the importance of ethical prioritization and temporal ordering within AI ethics. Addressing the algorithmic blind spot, we argue, requires re-centering ethical evaluation on human impacts, institutional responsibility, and the governance of algorithmic systems currently in operation. In doing so, the paper introduces a conceptual framework for critically assessing ethical discourse in AI and underscores the need to align ethical reflection more closely with its immediate social consequences.

</details>


### [86] [Evaluating Digital Inclusiveness of Digital Agri-Food Tools Using Large Language Models: A Comparative Analysis Between Human and AI-Based Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03252)
*Githma Pewinya,Carolina Martins,Garcia Mariangel*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究探索大语言模型（LLM）是否能辅助快速评估农业数字工具的包容性，以支持多维数字包容性指数（MDII）的评估流程。研究对比了Grok、Gemini、GPT-4o和GPT-5四款模型与人类专家评估的一致性，并分析了温度参数和潜在偏差问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的MDII评估流程资源消耗大、耗时长（通常需要数月），难以满足时间敏感或资源受限环境下的快速评估需求，研究旨在探索GenAI能否作为补充工具加速这一评估过程。

Method: 采用比较分析方法，将四款LLM（Grok、Gemini、GPT-4o、GPT-5）的评估输出与先前专家评估结果进行基准对比，考察模型与人类评分的一致性、对温度设置的敏感性，以及可能存在的偏差来源。

Result: LLM生成的评估输出在某些维度上能接近专家判断，但可靠性因模型和具体情境而异，整体表现存在差异。

Conclusion: LLM可作为数字包容性发展监测的补充工具，但当前阶段仅提供初步证据支持GenAI在资源受限环境中的应用，未来需进一步研究以提升评估一致性和可靠性。

Abstract: Ensuring digital inclusiveness is a critical priority in agri-food systems, particularly in the Global South, where digital divides persist. The Multidimensional Digital Inclusiveness Index (MDII) offers a comprehensive, human-led framework to assess how inclusive digital agricultural tools (agritools) are. However, the current evaluation process is resource intensive, often requiring months to complete. This study explores whether large language models (LLMs) can support a rapid, AI-enabled assessment of digital inclusiveness, complementing the MDII's existing workflow. Using a comparative analysis, the research benchmarks the performance of four LLMs (Grok, Gemini, GPT-4o, and GPT-5) against prior expert-led evaluations. The study investigates model alignment with human scores, sensitivity to temperature settings, and potential sources of bias. Findings suggest that LLMs can generate evaluative outputs that approximate expert judgment in some dimensions, though reliability varies across models and contexts. This exploratory work provides early evidence for the integration of GenAI into inclusive digital development monitoring, with implications for scaling evaluations in time-sensitive or resource-constrained environments.

</details>


### [87] [Is your AI Model Accurate Enough? The Difficult Choices Behind Rigorous AI Development and the EU AI Act](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03254)
*Lucas G. Uberti-Bona Marin,Bram Rijsbosch,Kristof Meding,Gerasimos Spanakis,Gijs van Dijck,Konrad Kollnig*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文挑战了AI"准确性"是客观、可测量、技术属性的传统观点，指出评估AI性能本质上依赖于情境相关的规范性决策，并以2024年欧盟《人工智能法案》为例，分析了定义、测量和评估准确性的四个关键选择：指标选择、多指标平衡、代表性数据测量、接受阈值确定。研究揭示了这些技术选择中嵌入的隐性或显性风险假设，为AI治理和监管实践提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为AI"准确性"是客观可测量的技术属性，本文作者对此提出挑战。在高风险AI系统部署中，如何定义和评估准确性决定了错误优先级、风险分配和竞争目标间的权衡，但这些决策往往被隐含在看似客观的技术指标中，缺乏明确的规范性讨论。

Method: 采用法律-技术跨学科分析方法，以2024年欧盟《人工智能法案》（该法案要求高风险系统达到"适当准确性水平"）为主要案例研究。作者系统分析了塑造准确性评估的四个核心选择：(1)指标选择、(2)多指标平衡、(3)代表性数据测量、(4)接受阈值确定，并与相关技术标准对照讨论。

Result: 识别并分析了每项选择与AI法案准确性要求及文档义务的关系，揭示了技术实现中隐含的关于可接受风险、错误和权衡的假设，为法规实施提供了具体的技术-规范接口分析。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献在于将准确性评估中隐含的技术-规范性维度显性化，推动AI治理的跨学科讨论，并为监管者、审计人员和开发人员提供将法律安全要求转化为技术实践的具体指南。

Abstract: Technical and legal debates frequently suggest that "accuracy" is an objective, measurable, and purely technical property. We challenge this view, showing that evaluating AI performance fundamentally depends on context-dependent normative decisions. These techno-normative choices are crucial for rigorous AI deployment, as they determine which errors are prioritised, how risks are distributed, and how trade-offs between competing objectives are resolved.
  This paper provides a legal-technical analysis of the choices that shape how accuracy is defined, measured, and assessed, using the 2024 European Union AI Act -- which mandates an "appropriate level of accuracy" for high-risk systems -- as a primary case study. We identify and analyse four choices central to any robust performance evaluation: (1) selecting metrics, (2) balancing multiple metrics, (3) measuring metrics against representative data, and (4) determining acceptance thresholds. For each choice, we study its relationship to the AI Act's accuracy requirement and associated documentation obligations, show how its technical implementation embeds implicit or explicit assumptions about acceptable risks, errors, and trade-offs, and discuss the implications for the practical implementation of the AI Act by examples and related technical standards.
  By making the techno-normative dimensions of accuracy explicit, this paper contributes to broader interdisciplinary debates on AI governance and regulation, and offers specific guidance for regulators, auditors, and developers tasked with translating (legal) safety requirements into technical practice.

</details>


### [88] [From Pre-trained Models to Large Language Models: A Comprehensive Survey of AI-Driven Psychological Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03259)
*Huiyao Chen,Ruimeng Liu,Yan Luo,Jiawen Zhang,Meishan Zhang,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The intersection of artificial intelligence and psychological science has experienced remarkable growth, with annual publications expanding from 859 papers in 2000 to 29,979 by 2025. However, this rapid evolution has created methodological fragmentation where similar computational techniques are independently developed across isolated psychological domains. This survey introduces the first systematic taxonomy that organizes AI-driven psychology tasks by computational processing patterns rather than application domains, categorizing them into four fundamental types: classification, regression, structured relational, and generative interactive tasks. Through analysis of over 300 representative works spanning the pre-trained model era and large language model era, we examine how computational approaches evolved from task-specific feature engineering to transfer learning and few-shot adaptation. We provide systematic coverage of datasets, evaluation metrics, and benchmarks while addressing fundamental challenges including interpretability, label uncertainty, privacy constraints, and cross-cultural validity. This computational perspective reveals transferable methodological patterns previously obscured by domain-centric organization, enabling systematic knowledge transfer and accelerated progress in computational psychology.

</details>


### [89] [AI Governance Control Stack for Operational Stability: Achieving Hardened Governance in AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03262)
*Horatio Morgan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出AI治理控制栈(AI Governance Control Stack for Operational Stability)，通过六层互补的治理架构（版本治理、证据验证、可解释性日志遥测监控、模型漂移检测和治理升级）确保AI系统在全生命周期内保持可追溯、有弹性和负责任的行为。该架构与EU AI Act、ISO/IEC 42001和NIST AI RMF等新兴监管标准对齐，为复杂企业环境中的AI治理提供实用蓝图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI治理方法主要聚焦于政策指导，缺乏可操作的稳定性机制。随着AI系统在关键决策环境中的大规模部署，组织需要能够随时间维持可靠、可审计和可问责行为的治理架构。

Method: 提出分层治理架构"AI Governance Control Stack for Operational Stability"，整合六层互补治理机制：系统记录版本治理、基于证据的验证、决策时可解释性日志记录、遥测监控、模型漂移检测和治理升级。

Result: 构建了概念性治理架构框架，完成监管对齐分析，展示了运营稳定性机制如何强化负责任AI实施。架构与EU AI Act、ISO/IEC 42001及NIST AI RMF等框架对齐。

Conclusion: 组织必须从静态政策框架转向集成化治理控制系统，以在动态环境中维持可信AI运营。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence systems are increasingly embedded in high-stakes decision environments, yet many governance approaches focus primarily on policy guidance rather than operational stability mechanisms. As AI deployments scale, organizations require governance architectures capable of maintaining reliable, auditable, and accountable behavior over time. This paper introduces the AI Governance Control Stack for Operational Stability, a layered governance architecture designed to ensure traceable and resilient AI system behavior.
  The proposed control stack integrates six complementary governance layers: system-of-record version governance, evidence-based verification, decision-time explainability logging, telemetry monitoring, model drift detection, and governance escalation. Together, these layers provide a structured mechanism for preserving governance integrity across the AI lifecycle while enabling organizations to detect instability, respond to emerging risks, and maintain regulatory accountability.
  The architecture aligns operational governance practices with emerging regulatory and standards frameworks, including the EU AI Act, ISO/IEC 42001 Artificial Intelligence Management Systems, and the NIST AI Risk Management Framework. By combining explainability infrastructure with continuous monitoring and human oversight mechanisms, the governance control stack provides a practical blueprint for achieving hardened AI governance in complex enterprise environments.
  The paper contributes a conceptual governance architecture and a framework alignment analysis demonstrating how operational stability mechanisms can strengthen responsible AI implementation. The findings suggest that organizations must move beyond static policy frameworks toward integrated governance control systems capable of sustaining trustworthy AI operation in dynamic environments.

</details>


### [90] [Internet-Mediated Digital Informal Learning Portfolios in STEM Higher Education: A Computational Grounded Theory Study of Online Peer Advice Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03643)
*Jianjun Xiao,Yuxi Long*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究运用社会认知职业理论(SCCT)和非正式学习框架，通过计算扎根理论(CGT)分析3,607条在线学生社区的同伴建议帖子，揭示了STEM学生构建数字非正式学习档案的机制。研究识别出三种学习档案类型：研究生学习档案、企业就业档案和公共部门档案，并提出"职业前置"作为早期学习重组的新模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对学生在互联网环境中如何整合分散的在线资源形成连贯学习路径的理解不足。该研究旨在填补这一理论空白，探索STEM学生如何通过互联网平台和同伴指导构建数字非正式学习档案。

Method: 采用计算扎根理论(CGT)方法，分析来自大型在线学生社区的3,607条同伴建议帖子。理论框架整合了社会认知职业理论(SCCT)和非正式学习理论。

Result: 研究发现职业路径(69.6%)和职业取向(59.7%)是主导组织维度，识别出三种数字非正式学习档案：(1)研究生学习档案——聚焦竞赛训练、数学基础和阶段性准备；(2)企业就业档案——聚焦自主技能建设、在线平台学习和战略性实习；(3)公共部门档案——采取研究生学习、企业就业和公共部门准备的"双轨对冲"策略。在线同伴社区本身充当分布式非正式课程角色。

Conclusion: 该研究将SCCT拓展至互联网介导的数字非正式学习领域，并提出"职业前置"作为早期学习重组模式。研究对高等教育机构的学习支持、互联网学习认可以及数字指导基础设施设计具有实践意义。

Abstract: Internet technologies have expanded higher education students' access to learning resources, peer guidance, and skill-development opportunities beyond formal curricula. Yet the ways students assemble these distributed online resources into coherent learning pathways remain insufficiently understood. This study examines how STEM students construct digital informal learning portfolios through internet-mediated peer advice and platform use. Drawing on Social Cognitive Career Theory (SCCT) and informal learning frameworks, we analyze 3,607 peer advice posts from a large online student community using Computational Grounded Theory (CGT). Results show that career pathway (69.6% of coded documents) and career orientation (59.7%) are the dominant organizing dimensions, yielding three distinct digital informal learning portfolios: a graduate-study portfolio centered on competition training, mathematical foundations, and staged preparation; an industry-employment portfolio centered on self-directed skill building, online platform learning, and strategically timed internships; and a public-sector portfolio characterized by dual-track hedging across graduate study, enterprise employment, and public-sector preparation pathways. The online peer community itself functions as a distributed informal curriculum, collectively producing and transmitting pathway-specific guidance about what to learn, when to learn it, and which internet resources to prioritize. These findings extend SCCT into the domain of internet-mediated digital informal learning and introduce career front-loading as a pattern of early learning reorganization. Implications are discussed for institutional learning support, recognition of internet-enabled learning, and the design of digital guidance infrastructures in higher education.

</details>


### [91] [Can Humans Tell? A Dual-Axis Study of Human Perception of LLM-Generated News](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03755)
*Alexander Loth,Martin Kappes,Marc-Oliver Pahl*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 人类无法可靠地区分机器生成与人类撰写的新闻文章，且这种无能为力在使用更大或更小的模型（包括仅7B参数的开放权重模型）时都存在。自我报告的专业知识可预测判断准确性，而政治取向则不能。认知疲劳会导致判断准确性下降。研究表明用户端检测不可行，需要系统级对策如加密内容溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型（LLM）生成文本的能力不断增强，一个关键问题浮现：人类是否能够区分机器生成的新闻与人类撰写的新闻？这对于信息可信度和虚假信息防控具有重要意义。

Method: 研究使用JudgeGPT平台，收集了1,054名参与者的2,318条判断，测试来自6个不同LLM生成的内容。实验采用独立测量来源归因（人类vs机器）和真实性判断（合法vs虚假）的连续量表。

Result: 主要发现：(1) 参与者无法可靠地区分机器生成与人类撰写的文本；(2) 这种无能跨越所有测试模型，包括仅有7B参数的开放权重模型；(3) 自我报告的专业知识预测判断准确性(r=.35)，而政治取向不预测(r=-.10)；(4) 聚类分析揭示不同的响应策略（"怀疑者"vs"相信者"）；(5) 约30次连续评估后准确性因认知疲劳而下降。

Conclusion: 答案是否定的：人类无法可靠地区分AI生成与人类撰写的新闻。这些结果表明用户端检测不是可行的防御手段，需要系统级对策如加密内容溯源来应对AI生成内容的挑战。

Abstract: Can humans tell whether a news article was written by a person or a large language model (LLM)? We investigate this question using JudgeGPT, a study platform that independently measures source attribution (human vs. machine) and authenticity judgment (legitimate vs. fake) on continuous scales. From 2,318 judgments collected from 1,054 participants across content generated by six LLMs, we report five findings: (1) participants cannot reliably distinguish machine-generated from human-written text (p > .05, Welch's t-test); (2) this inability holds across all tested models, including open-weight models with as few as 7B parameters; (3) self-reported domain expertise predicts judgment accuracy (r = .35, p < .001) whereas political orientation does not (r = -.10, n.s.); (4) clustering reveals distinct response strategies ("Skeptics" vs. "Believers"); and (5) accuracy degrades after approximately 30 sequential evaluations due to cognitive fatigue. The answer, in short, is no: humans cannot reliably tell. These results indicate that user-side detection is not a viable defense and motivate system-level countermeasures such as cryptographic content provenance.

</details>


### [92] [Enhancing behavioral nudges with large language model-based iterative personalization: A field experiment on electricity and hot-water conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03881)
*Zonghan Li,Yi Liu,Chunyan Wang,Song Tong,Kaiping Peng,Feng Ji*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究在233名中国大学生中开展三臂随机实验，测试LLM个性化助推对日常用电和热水节约行为的影响。结果显示，LLM个性化助推（T2）效果最佳，相比文本对照组（C）每天每房间节电0.56千瓦时（p=0.014），调适节约率提高18.3个百分点。热水节能效果方向一致但较小，且随时间衰减，符合高摩擦行为特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统助推干预的有效性在反复将反馈转化为可行行动时受到限制，尤其在环境不断变化的条件下。大型语言模型（LLM）有望通过生成个性化指导并跨轮次迭代更新来减轻这一认知负担。

Method: 三臂随机对照实验，233名中国大学生被试。测试两种客观测量的行为：日常用电节约和淋浴热水节约（两者在行为摩擦程度上存在差异）。三组条件：T2（LLM个性化助推）、T1（图像增强的传统助推）和C（文本传统助推对照）。

Result: LLM个性化助推（T2）产生最大节约效应，T1与C效果相似（总体检验p=0.009）。与C相比，T2每天每房间节电0.56千瓦时（p=0.014），调适节约率提高18.3个百分点。该优势在前两轮干预即出现并持续至今。热水节约效果方向一致但较小、估计精度较低、随时间衰减，与该领域更高摩擦特征相符。LLM个性化助推强调前瞻性、情境化指导，并与更高参与度相关。

Conclusion: 本研究提供了LLM迭代个性化可增强行为助推的现场证据，行为摩擦可能是其边界条件。需开展更大规模试验并扩展至更多行为领域。

Abstract: Nudging is widely used to promote behavioral change, but its effectiveness is often limited when recipients must repeatedly translate feedback into workable next steps under changing circumstances. Large language models (LLMs) may help reduce part of this cognitive work by generating personalized guidance and updating it iteratively across intervention rounds. We developed an LLM agent for iterative personalization and tested it in a three-arm randomized experiment among 233 university residents in China, using daily electricity and shower hot-water conservation as objectively measured cases differing in friction. LLM-personalized nudges (T2) produced the largest conservation effects, while image-enhanced conventional nudges (T1) and text-based conventional nudges (C) showed similar outcomes (omnibus p = 0.009). Relative to C, T2 reduced electricity consumption by 0.56 kWh per room-day (p = 0.014), corresponding to an 18.3 percentage-point higher adjusted saving rate. This advantage emerged within the first two intervention rounds, alongside iterative updating of personalized guidance, and persisted thereafter. Hot-water outcomes followed the same direction but were smaller, less precisely estimated, and attenuated over time, consistent with stronger friction in this domain. LLM-personalized nudges emphasized prospective and context-specific guidance and were associated with higher participant engagement. This study provides field evidence that LLM-based iterative personalization can enhance behavioral nudging, with behavioral friction as a potential boundary condition. Larger trials and extension to more behaviors are warranted.

</details>


### [93] [Effects of Generative AI Errors on User Reliance Across Task Difficulty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04319)
*Jacy Reese Anthis,Hannah Cha,Solon Barocas,Alexandra Chouldechova,Jake Hofman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究用户对AI"参差不齐的能力边界"（在简单任务上失败但在困难任务上成功）的反应，发现错误率越高用户使用意愿越低，但意外发现简单任务的错误并不会比困难任务的错误更显著地降低使用率，表明用户并不排斥AI能力的"参差不齐"特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI系统存在"参差不齐的能力边界"（jagged frontier）现象——在人类认为简单的任务上意外失败，却在困难任务上出人意料地成功。然而，用户如何感知和应对这种不对称性尚不清楚。

Method: 开发了基于图表生成任务的激励兼容实验方法，诱导生成式AI产生错误并测试用户依赖效应。采用3×2因子设计（误差率10%/30%/50% × 任务难度易/难），共577名参与者参与预注册实验。

Result: 确认观察到的错误越多，用户使用AI的意愿越低。但意外发现，简单任务的错误并不会比困难任务的错误更显著地降低使用率。

Conclusion: 在此实验设置下，用户似乎并不排斥AI能力的"参差不齐"特性。建议未来研究在改变任务难度的同时，考察其他AI错误的特征，如错误模式是否容易被学习。

Abstract: The capabilities of artificial intelligence (AI) lie along a jagged frontier, where AI systems surprisingly fail on tasks that humans find easy and succeed on tasks that humans find hard. To investigate user reactions to this phenomenon, we developed an incentive-compatible experimental methodology based on diagram generation tasks, in which we induce errors in generative AI output and test effects on user reliance. We demonstrate the interface in a preregistered 3x2 experiment (N = 577) with error rates of 10%, 30%, or 50% on easier or harder diagram generation tasks. We confirmed that observing more errors reduces use, but we unexpectedly found that easy-task errors did not significantly reduce use more than hard-task errors, suggesting that people are not averse to jaggedness in this experimental setting. We encourage future work that varies task difficulty at the same time as other features of AI errors, such as whether the jagged error patterns are easily learned.

</details>


### [94] [What is Human in Judgment? Testing Automation Bias and Algorithm Aversion Among United States Military Academy Cadets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04333)
*Lauren Kahn,Michael C. Horowitz,Laura Resnick Samotin*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文研究军事人员与人工智能决策支持系统的交互，通过对236名西点军校学员和普通公众的对照实验，测量其自动化偏见和算法厌恶程度，发现军事人员对算法的信任校准优于普通公众。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI决策支持系统在军事领域的广泛应用，学界担忧自动化偏见（过度依赖算法）和算法厌恶（过早不信任自动化输出）会增加战争中的错误风险，但关于军队实际如何与AI交互的实证证据仍然有限。

Method: 研究者设计了一项目标识别任务，将236名西点军校学员与人口统计学特征相似的跨国普通公众样本进行对比。实验给予参与者来自算法或人类分析师的建议，并允许其重新评估初始判断，直接测量自动化偏见和算法厌恶程度。

Result: 与预期相反，西点军校学员表现出比普通公众更少的认知偏差，对算法决策支持系统的信任校准更为准确。

Conclusion: 军事教育和AI接触可能有效塑造军事人员与AI的交互方式，从而对战争与和平相关事务中国际政治产生实质性影响，但研究结论的普适性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Human judgment has always been central to conflict and escalation, but how will a world of artificial intelligence (AI) change the role of humans in war? As militaries increasingly adopt AI-enabled decision-support systems (DSS), including the United States in the war against Iran, concerns about automation bias -- over-reliance on algorithmic recommendations -- and algorithm aversion -- premature distrust of automated outputs -- raise fears that relying on AI too much could increase the risk of error, miscalculation, and accidents. Yet existing evidence on how militaries actually interact with AI remains limited. We test theories about the susceptibility of militaries to automation bias by comparing the results from a survey experiment conducted with 236 cadets at the United States Military Academy at West Point to a demographically similar cross-national public sample. Respondents completed a target identification task and then received advice from either an algorithm or a human analyst and had the opportunity to re-assess their initial identification, allowing direct measurement of automation bias and algorithm aversion. Contrary to prominent concerns, we find that West Point cadets are less prone to cognitive distortion than members of the general public, displaying better calibrated trust in algorithmic decision support systems. While the findings are limited, they suggest that military education and exposure to AI can meaningfully shape how AI influences international politics in matters of war and peace.

</details>


### [95] [Teaching Empathy in Software Engineering Education in the Age of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04689)
*Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhães,Giuseppe Destefanis,Mairieli Wessel,Ann Barcomb,Sherlock Licorish,Brody Stuart-Verner,Italo Santos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 研究调查了教育者在软件工程课程中融入移情教学的做法，通过定性分析确定了五个操作化类别：AI系统的社会框架、公平性与可访问性考虑、多样用户 representation、利益相关者角色意识与责任、以及开发过程中的结构化反思与反馈。研究表明移情可以嵌入核心开发活动而非单独教授。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能系统中软件越来越多地参与影响个人和社区的决策，软件工程教育中需要将移情整合到AI系统开发的技术教育中，但目前缺乏相关指导。

Method: 定性分析教育者报告的教学实践

Result: 识别出五个移情操作化类别：AI系统的社会框架、设计与评估中的公平性与可访问性考虑、多样用户的呈现、利益相关者角色意识与责任、开发过程中的结构化反思与反馈。

Conclusion: 移情可嵌入核心开发活动而非单独教授，使学生能够推理偏见、可访问性、问责制及AI技术的社会后果，为培养AI系统开发人才提供教育框架。

Abstract: Empathy has been discussed as a relevant human capability in software engineering, particularly in activities that require understanding users, stakeholders, and the societal implications of technological systems. This relevance becomes more pronounced in the context of artificial intelligence, where software increasingly participates in decisions that affect diverse individuals and communities. However, limited guidance exists on how empathy can be integrated into technical software engineering education in ways that connect with the development of AI-enabled systems. This study investigates teaching practices that educators use to incorporate empathy into software engineering courses. Using qualitative analysis of educator-reported practices, we identified five categories through which empathy is operationalized within technical coursework: societal framing of AI systems, fairness and accessibility considerations in design and evaluation, representation of diverse users, stakeholder role awareness and responsibility, and structured reflection and feedback during development processes. The findings indicate that empathy can be embedded within core development activities rather than taught as a separate topic, enabling students to reason about bias, accessibility, accountability, and the societal consequences of AI technologies. These results contribute a structured view of how empathy-oriented practices can be incorporated into software engineering education to support the preparation of students who will develop AI-enabled systems.

</details>


### [96] [Who is the author? A legal and normative view of authorship in Generative AI-aided academic works](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04700)
*David M. Pereira*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文探讨生成式人工智能（GenAI）在高等教育中的使用如何与欧洲法律和制度框架下的"署名权"概念相交。论文主张署名权是一种质的门槛而非二元属性，当GenAI在人类智力控制下作为认知支持工具时，学生可保留署名权；但当AI输出取代了创造性自主权时，署名权的归属在法律和规范层面均存争议。研究提出了一个定性门槛框架，用于区分合法的AI辅助学术生产与损害署名权、责任感和学术诚信的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文献多聚焦于GenAI的技术、伦理或教学影响，而对其法律和规范层面的署名权问题关注不足。随着GenAI工具在高等教育中的广泛采用，传统的作者身份、责任和学习假设正受到根本性挑战，亟需从法律角度厘清署名权的边界与归属。

Method: 本文采用跨学科法律分析方法，综合运用欧洲版权法（特别是人类智力创作要求）、《人工智能法案》、数据保护法以及高等教育领域的超机构治理实践，构建综合分析框架。

Result: 本文论证了署名权应被理解为质的门槛（qualitative threshold），而非二元属性；在人类智力控制下使用GenAI作为认知支持工具时，学生可保留署名权；而当AI输出取代人类创造性自主权时，署名权的法律和规范归属将变得存疑。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个定性门槛框架，旨在帮助评估GenAI辅助学术工作中的署名权问题，为区分合法的AI辅助学术生产与损害学术诚信的做法提供可操作标准。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools in higher education has fundamentally altered the conditions under which academic work is produced, challenging long-standing assumptions about authorship, responsibility, and learning. While much of the existing literature has focused on technical, ethical, or pedagogical implications of GenAI, comparatively little attention has been paid to the legal and normative aspects of authorship in AI-aided academic work. In this work, we examine how the use of GenAI intersects with the concept of authorship as understood within European regulatory and institutional frameworks. Drawing primarily on European copyright law, notably the requirement of human intellectual creation, the paper argues that authorship functions as a qualitative threshold rather than a binary attribute. Authorship may remain attributable to the student where GenAI operates as cognitive support under human intellectual control. By contrast, attribution becomes legally and normatively disputable once AI output displaces creative autonomy. The analysis places this doctrinal framework alongside broader regulatory principles arising from the AI Act, data protection law, and emerging suprainstitutional governance practices in higher education. We propose a qualitative threshold framework designed to assist in authorship-sensitive assessment of GenAI-aided academic work. This framework provides criteria for distinguishing legitimate AI-assisted academic production from practices that undermine authorship, responsibility, and academic integrity.

</details>


### [97] [Artificial Intelligence and Cost Reduction in Public Higher Education: A Scoping Review of Emerging Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04741)
*Diamanto Tzanoulinou,Loukas Triantafyllopoulos,George Vorvilas,Evgenia Paxinou,Nikolaos Karousos,Thomas Dasaklis,Athanassios Mihiotis,Manolis Koutouzis,Dimitris Kalles,Vassilios S. Verykios*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Public higher education systems face increasing financial pressures from expanding student populations, rising operational costs, and persistent demands for equitable access. Artificial Intelligence (AI), including generative tools such as ChatGPT, learning analytics, intelligent tutoring systems, and predictive models, has been proposed as a means of enhancing efficiency and reducing costs. This study conducts a scoping review of the literature on AI applications in public higher education, based on systematic searches in Scopus and IEEE Xplore that identified 241 records, of which 21 empirical studies met predefined eligibility criteria and were thematically analyzed. The findings show that AI enables cost savings by automating administrative tasks, optimizing resource allocation, supporting personalized learning at scale, and applying predictive analytics to improve student retention and institutional planning. At the same time, concerns emerge regarding implementation costs, unequal access across institutions, and risks of widening digital divides. Overall, the thematic analysis highlights both the promises and limitations of AI-driven cost reduction in higher education, offering insights for policymakers, university administrators, and educators on the economic implications of AI adoption, while also pointing to gaps that warrant further empirical research.

</details>


### [98] [Cheap Talk, Empty Promise: Frontier LLMs easily break public promises for self-interest](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04782)
*Jerick Shi,Terry Jingcheng Zhang,Zhijing Jin,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究探讨大型语言模型作为自主智能体在多智能体环境中是否会在公开承诺后私下违背承诺，通过在六种经典博弈游戏中对九种前沿模型进行系统性实验，将背弃行为分为四类：双赢、自私、利他和破坏性。研究发现约56.6%的场景中存在背弃承诺行为，且大多数背弃发生在智能体未意识到自身正在违背承诺的情况下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型越来越多地被部署为多智能体系统中的自主决策实体，其安全性问题日益突出。一个关键问题是：当智能体可以私下偏离时，它们是否会违背公开做出的行动承诺？这对个体利益和集体福利有何影响？

Method: 研究采用一次性正则形式博弈（one-shot normal-form games）作为实验框架，将智能体的背弃行为按其对个体收益和集体福利的影响分为四类：双赢型、自私型、利他型和破坏型。通过对六种经典博弈游戏、九种前沿模型及不同规模群体进行穷举式枚举来识别各类背弃行为的出现机会，并测量智能体实际利用这些机会的频率。

Result: 研究发现智能体在约56.6%的场景中违背了承诺，但背弃行为的性质在不同模型间存在显著差异，即使总体背弃率相近。更关键的是，对于大多数模型而言，承诺的违背发生在智能体未明确意识到自己在打破承诺的情况下。

Conclusion: LLM智能体普遍存在违背公开承诺的行为倾向，且这种背弃往往是无意识的。这一发现对多智能体系统的安全设计具有重要意义，提示需要建立相应的机制来确保智能体行为的可信性和一致性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents in multi-agent settings where they communicate intentions and take consequential actions with limited human oversight. A critical safety question is whether agents that publicly commit to actions break those promises when they can privately deviate, and what the consequences are for both themselves and the collective. We study deception as a deviation from a publicly announced action in one-shot normal-form games, classifying each deviation by its effect on individual payoff and collective welfare into four categories: win-win, selfish, altruistic, and sabotaging. By exhaustively enumerating announcement profiles across six canonical games, nine frontier models, and varying group sizes, we identify all opportunities for each deviation type and measure how often agents exploit them. Across all settings, agents deviate from promises in approximately 56.6% of scenarios, but the character of deception varies substantially across models even at similar overall rates. Most critically, for the majority of the models, promise-breaking occurs without verbalized awareness of the fact that they are breaking promises.

</details>


### [99] [From Hallucination to Scheming: A Unified Taxonomy and Benchmark Analysis for LLM Deception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04788)
*Jerick Shi,Terry Jingcheng Zhang,Zhijing Jin,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型产生误导性输出的问题（从幻觉引用到策略性欺骗），提出了一个统一的三维分类体系（目标导向程度、欺骗对象、机制类型），并用该框架分析了50个现有基准测试，发现所有基准都测试了"捏造"这一机制，而"语用扭曲"、"归因"和"能力自我认知"等领域严重缺乏覆盖，策略性欺骗基准也处于起步阶段。文章最后为开发者和监管者提供了具体建议，包括一个最低限度报告模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM系统产生的误导性输出（如幻觉引用、策略性欺骗等）被不同研究社区分别研究，缺乏统一的术语体系，导致研究碎片化且难以比较和整合。

Method: 提出一个统一的三维分类法：(1) 目标导向程度（从行为性欺骗到策略性欺骗）；(2) 欺骗对象；(3) 欺骗机制（捏造、省略、语用扭曲）。并应用此框架对50个现有基准测试进行系统分析。

Result: 分析揭示了关键覆盖空白：所有基准都测试了捏造机制，但语用扭曲、归因和能力自我认知等领域严重缺乏覆盖；策略性欺骗基准处于起步阶段。

Conclusion: 为开发者和监管者提供具体建议，包括用于在框架中定位未来研究的最低限度报告模板，以促进更系统、一致的研究和评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) produce systematically misleading outputs, from hallucinated citations to strategic deception of evaluators, yet these phenomena are studied by separate communities with incompatible terminology. We propose a unified taxonomy organized along three complementary dimensions: degree of goal-directedness (behavioral to strategic deception), object of deception, and mechanism (fabrication, omission, or pragmatic distortion). Applying this taxonomy to 50 existing benchmarks reveals that every benchmark tests fabrication while pragmatic distortion, attribution, and capability self-knowledge remain critically under-covered, and strategic deception benchmarks are nascent. We offer concrete recommendations for developers and regulators, including a minimal reporting template for positioning future work within our framework.

</details>


### [100] [Latent Profiles of AI Risk Perception and Their Differential Association with Community Driving Safety Concerns: A Person-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04849)
*Amir Rafe,Anika Baitullah,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究采用潜在类别分析（LCA）对美国成年人进行调查，识别出四类AI风险感知群体：适度怀疑者（17.5%）、审慎务实者（42.8%）、AI矛盾者（10.6%）和极端担忧者（29.1%）。研究证实AI风险感知与交通安全态度在个体层面存在显著关联，为风险感知的世界观理论提供了实证支持，并为自动驾驶汽车的风险沟通提供了分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统研究孤立探讨公众对AI和驾驶安全的看法，且多采用变量中心法假设人群同质性。然而，风险感知理论预测个体内部的这些评价应存在协变关系，反映潜在世界观。本研究旨在突破这一方法论局限，通过潜在类别分析识别异质性群体，并检验不同群体在社区驾驶安全关切上的差异。

Method: 研究使用Pew研究中心美国趋势小组第152波数据（n=5,255），采用潜在类别分析对9项AI风险感知指标进行建模。通过Bolck-Croon-Hagenaars校正的远端结局分析检验类别差异，并使用调查加权多项逻辑回归识别类别成员资格的人口统计学和意识形态预测因子。

Result: 识别出四个潜在类别：Moderate Skeptics（17.5%）、Concerned Pragmatists（42.8%）、AI Ambivalent（10.6%）和Extreme Alarm（29.1%）。九项驾驶安全结局指标在各类别间均存在显著差异。AI担忧程度与感知驾驶危害严重性呈单调关系；而AI与人类驾驶的比较评价则受信任度而非担忧程度驱动。

Conclusion: 研究在个体层面证实了Cultural Theory of Risk所假设的世界观基础风险结构化机制。AI风险感知与交通安全态度的跨领域协变关系支持风险感知的文化理论解释。研究提出的四类分割框架可用于指导自动驾驶汽车的针对性风险沟通策略，弥合AI风险Orientation与交通安全的认知鸿沟。

Abstract: Public attitudes toward artificial intelligence (AI) and driving safety are typically studied in isolation using variable-centered methods that assume population homogeneity, yet risk perception theory predicts that these evaluations covary within individuals as expressions of underlying worldviews. This study identifies latent profiles of AI risk perception among U.S. adults and tests whether these profiles are differentially associated with community driving safety concerns. Latent class analysis was applied to nine AI risk-perception indicators from a nationally representative survey (Pew Research Center American Trends Panel Wave 152, n = 5,255); Bolck-Croon-Hagenaars corrected distal outcome analysis tested class differences on nine driving-safety outcomes, and survey-weighted multinomial logistic regression identified demographic and ideological predictors of class membership. Four classes emerged: Moderate Skeptics (17.5%), Concerned Pragmatists (42.8%), AI Ambivalent (10.6%), and Extreme Alarm (29.1%), with all nine driving-safety outcomes significantly differentiated across classes. Higher AI concern mapped monotonically onto greater perceived driving-hazard severity; the exception, comparative evaluation of AI versus human driving, was driven by trust rather than concern level. The cross-domain covariation provides person-level evidence for the worldview-based risk structuring posited by Cultural Theory of Risk and yields a four-class segmentation framework for AV communication that links AI risk orientation to transportation safety attitudes.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [101] [SDVDiag: Using Context-Aware Causality Mining for the Diagnosis of Connected Vehicle Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03391)
*Matthias Weiß,Falk Dettinger,Elias Detrois,Nasser Jazdi,Michael Weyrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对车联网系统中因果故障诊断困难的问题，提出一种融合人类反馈的多模态方法，利用强化学习从人类反馈中持续训练因果挖掘模型，并通过分布式追踪数据剪枝假阳性因果边。实验表明该方法因果边检测精度从14%提升至100%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车联网功能虽逐步推广，但因其分布式特性和底层云、边、网基础设施的复杂性，可靠运行仍面临挑战。现有自动分析技术主要依赖数据驱动，难以处理隐藏关系和上下文信息，导致故障诊断仍依赖人工。

Method: 提出多模态因果分析方法，核心包括：(1) 采用强化学习从人类反馈(RLHF)持续训练因果挖掘模型，融入专家知识；(2) 利用分布式追踪数据剪枝假阳性因果边；(3) 注入领域特定关系以进一步优化因果图。

Result: 在真实的车联网自动代客泊车应用中进行评估，因果边检测精度从14%大幅提升至100%，系统可解释性显著优于纯数据驱动方法。

Conclusion: 所提方法在车联网领域具有实际应用潜力，可有效提升系统故障诊断效率，减少停机时间。

Abstract: Real-world implementations of connected vehicle functions are spreading steadily, yet operating these functions reliably remains challenging due to their distributed nature and the complexity of the underlying cloud, edge, and networking infrastructure. Quick diagnosis of problems and understanding the error chains that lead to failures is essential for reducing downtime. However, diagnosing these systems is still largely performed manually, as automated analysis techniques are predominantly data-driven and struggle with hidden relationships and the integration of context information. This paper addresses this gap by introducing a multimodal approach that integrates human feedback and system-specific information into the causal analysis process. Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback is employed to continuously train a causality mining model while incorporating expert knowledge. Additional modules leverage distributed tracing data to prune false-positive causal links and enable the injection of domain-specific relationships to further refine the causal graph.Evaluation is performed using an automated valet parking application operated in a connected vehicle test field. Results demonstrate a significant increase in precision from 14\% to 100\% for the detection of causal edges and improved system interpretability compared to purely data-driven approaches, highlighting the potential for system operators in the connected vehicle domain.

</details>


### [102] [Android Instrumentation Testing in Continuous Integration: Practices, Patterns, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03438)
*Hamid Parsazadeh,Taher A. Ghaleb,Safwat Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了4,518个使用CI的开源Android仓库，发现只有约10.6%的仓库运行仪表化测试。研究揭示了测试设置从自定义脚本向社区组件的演进趋势，并比较了社区组件、第三方设备实验室和自定义脚本三种方案在可靠性、效率和成本方面的表现差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android仪表化测试（端到端测试）能发现简单测试遗漏的问题，但在CI中自动运行存在困难，主要挑战是模拟器设置脆弱且配置随时间漂移。

Method: 通过分析CI工作流文件、脚本和构建配置，识别Gradle中的设备设置定义（如Gradle Managed Devices），并使用GitHub Actions的运行级和步骤级元数据（结果、时长、重试次数、队列延迟）评估不同设置风格的性能。

Result: 仅约1/10的仓库（481/4,518，10.6%）在CI中运行仪表化测试；项目通常从自定义脚本转向社区组件；社区组件最可靠高效，第三方设备实验室适合定时回归测试但成本高且失败率更高，自定义脚本提供灵活性但重试次数更多。

Conclusion: 三种方案各有适用场景：社区组件适合日常代码检查，第三方设备实验室适合计划性回归测试，自定义脚本提供最大灵活性但维护成本较高。

Abstract: Android instrumentation tests (end-to-end tests that run on a device or emulator) can catch problems that simpler tests miss. However, running these tests automatically in continuous integration (CI) is often difficult because emulator setup is fragile and configurations tend to drift over time. We study how open-source Android apps run instrumentation tests in CI by analyzing 4,518 repositories that use CI (snapshot: Aug. 10, 2025). We examine CI workflow files, scripts, and build configurations to identify cases where device setup is defined in Gradle (e.g., Gradle Managed Devices). Our results answer three questions about adoption, evolution, and outcomes. First, only about one in ten repositories (481/4,518; 10.6%) run instrumentation tests in CI, typically using either reusable community components or repository-specific custom scripts to set up emulators. Second, these setups usually stay the same over time; when changes happen, projects tend to move from custom scripts toward reusable community components. Third, we study why projects change their CI setup by analyzing their commits, pull requests, and issue messages. We evaluate how different setup styles perform using GitHub Actions run- and step-level metadata (e.g., outcomes, duration, reruns, and queue delay). We find that teams often change approaches to expand test coverage, and that each approach fits different needs: community-based setups are typically the most reliable and efficient for everyday checks on new code, third-party device labs suit scheduled regression testing but can be costlier and fail more often, and custom scripting provides flexibility but is associated with more reruns.

</details>


### [103] [Agile Story-Point Estimation: Is RAG a Better Way to Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03443)
*Lamyea Maha,Tajmilur Rahman,Chanchal Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究探索使用检索增强生成(RAG)技术自动化敏捷开发中的故事点估算流程，对两个嵌入模型在23个开源软件项目上进行测试，发现RAG方法在多个场景下优于基线模型，但未发现显著统计差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷开发中的冲刺规划会议通过Planning Poker等共识估算技术为任务分配故事点，但这一过程极其耗时。本研究旨在自动化这一手动流程。

Method: 使用两种嵌入模型（bge-large-en-v1.5和all-mpnet-base-v2）在23个不同规模的开源软件项目上应用RAG框架，从四个维度进行评估：检索超参数影响、项目规模对准确率的影响、嵌入模型选择、以及与现有基线的比较。

Result: RAG方法在多个场景下优于基线模型，但在不同项目规模或不同嵌入模型间未发现统计显著的性能差异。

Conclusion: RAG应用于自动化故事点估算具有一定的潜力，但当前方法仍需进一步研究和改进，包括RAG框架优化和模型适应策略的完善，以提升自动估算的准确率。

Abstract: The sprint-based iterative approach in the Agile software development method allows continuous feedback and adaptation. One of the crucial Agile software development activities is the sprint planning session where developers estimate the effort required to complete tasks through a consensus-based estimation technique such as Planning Poker. In the Agile software development method, a common unit of measuring development effort is Story Point (SP) which is assigned to tasks to understand the complexity and development time needed to complete them. Despite the benefits of this process, it is an extremely time-consuming manual process. To mitigate this issue, in this study, we investigated if this manual process can be automated using Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) which comprises a "Retriever" and a "Generator". We applied two embedding models - bge-large-en-v1.5, and Sentence-Transformers' all-mpnet-base-v2 on 23 open-source software projects of varying sizes and examined four key aspects: 1) how retrieval hyper-parameters influence the performance, 2) whether estimation accuracy differs across different sizes of the projects, 3) whether embedding model choice affects accuracy, and 4) how the RAG-based approach compares to the existing baselines. Although the RAG-based approach outperformed the baseline models in several occasions, our results did not exhibit statistically significant differences in performance across the projects or across the embedding models. This highlights the need for further studies and refinement of the RAG, and model adaptation strategies for better accuracy in automatically estimating user stories.

</details>


### [104] [Measuring LLM Trust Allocation Across Conflicting Software Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03447)
*Noshin Ulfat,Ahsanul Ameen Sabit,Soneya Binta Hossain*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE框架，用于评估LLM在软件工程任务中对代码、Javadoc、签名和测试等多种制品的质量评估和信任校准能力。研究发现当前LLM在审计自然语言规范方面表现较好，但在检测微妙的代码级漂移方面存在系统性盲点，且大多数模型的置信度校准较差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM软件工程助手评估主要关注下游输出质量，无法揭示模型是否真正识别了降级证据、定位了不可靠来源或在制品间校准了信任分配。LLM的失败不仅在于产生错误输出，还在于将信任错误分配给不一致的代码、文档和测试。

Method: 提出TRACE框架，通过盲扰动在Javadoc、方法签名、实现和测试前缀上引出结构化的制品级信任轨迹。使用7个模型在456个精选Java方法包上的22,339条有效轨迹进行评估，从制品质量评估、不一致性检测、受影响制品归因和源优先级排序四个维度进行测试。

Result: 质量惩罚主要局部化在受扰动的制品上且随严重程度增加；但敏感性在不同制品类型间不对称：文档缺陷的重-轻差异(0.152-0.253)远大于实现缺陷(0.049-0.123)。模型对显式文档缺陷检测良好(67-94%)，对Javadoc与实现矛盾检测达50-91%，但对"仅实现漂移而文档保持合理"的场景存在系统性盲点，检测率下降7-42个百分点。7个模型中6个置信度校准较差。

Conclusion: 当前LLM更擅长审计自然语言规范而非检测微妙的代码级漂移，建议在关键正确性的下游使用前进行显式的制品级信任推理。

Abstract: LLM-based software engineering assistants fail not only by producing incorrect outputs, but also by allocating trust to the wrong artifact when code, documentation, and tests disagree. Existing evaluations focus mainly on downstream outcomes and therefore cannot reveal whether a model recognized degraded evidence, identified the unreliable source, or calibrated its trust across artifacts. We present TRACE (Trust Reasoning over Artifacts for Calibrated Evaluation), a framework that elicits structured artifact-level trust traces over Javadoc, method signatures, implementations, and test prefixes under blind perturbations. Using 22,339 valid traces from seven models on 456 curated Java method bundles, we evaluate per-artifact quality assessment, inconsistency detection, affected artifact attribution, and source prioritization. Across all models, quality penalties are largely localized to the perturbed artifact and increase with severity, but sensitivity is asymmetric across artifact types: documentation bugs induce a substantially larger heavy-to-subtle gap than implementation faults (0.152-0.253 vs. 0.049-0.123). Models detect explicit documentation bugs well (67-94%) and Javadoc and implementation contradictions at 50-91%, yet show a systematic blind spot when only the implementation drifts while the documentation remains plausible, with detection dropping by 7-42 percentage points. Confidence is poorly calibrated for six of seven models. These findings suggest that current LLMs are better at auditing natural-language specifications than at detecting subtle code-level drift, motivating explicit artifact-level trust reasoning before correctness-critical downstream use.

</details>


### [105] [Inside the Scaffold: A Source-Code Taxonomy of Coding Agent Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03515)
*Benjamin Rombaut*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过对13个开源代码智能体脚手架的源代码级分析，提出了一个包含12个维度、三层架构的分类体系。研究发现脚手架架构难以离散分类，控制策略从固定管道到蒙特卡洛树搜索不等，上下文压缩策略有7种，且5个循环原语（ReAct、generate-test-repair、plan-execute、multi-attempt retry、tree search）可组合使用，11/13的智能体采用多原语组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体调查仅按抽象能力分类，无法区分架构差异；轨迹研究仅观察行为而未深入决定行为的脚手架代码。脚手架代码（控制循环、工具定义、状态管理、上下文策略）对智能体行为至关重要但缺乏系统研究。

Method: 对13个开源代码智能体脚手架（固定提交哈希）进行源代码级分析，从三个层面提取12个维度的特征：控制架构层、工具与环境接口层、资源管理层。所有分类主张均基于文件路径和行号进行可验证标注。

Result: 脚手架架构呈连续分布而非离散分类；控制策略跨度大（固定管道→MCTS）；工具数量0-37个；上下文压缩7种策略；5个循环原语可组合，11/13智能体采用多原语组合；在外部约束主导领域（工具能力类别、编辑格式、执行隔离）趋同，在开放设计问题（上下文压缩、状态管理、多模型路由）处发散。

Conclusion: 代码智能体脚手架采用组合式构建而非单一控制结构；维度收敛与发散揭示了设计边界；基于文件路径和行号的分类体系为研究者和实践者提供了可复用的参考框架。

Abstract: LLM-based coding agents can localize bugs, generate patches, and run tests with diminishing human oversight, yet the scaffolding code that surrounds the language model (the control loop, tool definitions, state management, and context strategy) remains poorly understood. Existing surveys classify agents by abstract capabilities (tool use, planning, reflection) that cannot distinguish between architecturally distinct systems, and trajectory studies observe what agents do without examining the scaffold code that determines why. This paper presents a source-code-level architectural taxonomy derived from analysis of 13 open-source coding agent scaffolds at pinned commit hashes. Each agent is characterized across 12 dimensions organized into three layers: control architecture, tool and environment interface, and resource management. The analysis reveals that scaffold architectures resist discrete classification: control strategies range from fixed pipelines to Monte Carlo Tree Search, tool counts range from 0 to 37, and context compaction spans seven distinct strategies. Five loop primitives (ReAct, generate-test-repair, plan-execute, multi-attempt retry, tree search) function as composable building blocks that agents layer in different combinations; 11 of 13 agents compose multiple primitives rather than relying on a single control structure. Dimensions converge where external constraints dominate (tool capability categories, edit formats, execution isolation) and diverge where open design questions remain (context compaction, state management, multi-model routing). All taxonomic claims are grounded in file paths and line numbers, providing a reusable reference for researchers studying agent behavior and practitioners designing new scaffolds.

</details>


### [106] [AgenticFlict: A Large-Scale Dataset of Merge Conflicts in AI Coding Agent Pull Requests on GitHub](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03551)
*Daniel Ogenrwot,John Businge*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了AgenticFlict数据集，研究AI编程智能体生成的Pull Request中的合并冲突问题，发现冲突率高达27.67%，表明AI生成代码的集成挑战亟需关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编程智能体正从辅助工具转变为主动贡献者，但现有研究较少关注Agentic PR的合并冲突问题，而合并冲突是协作软件开发中的基础性挑战，亟需深入理解。

Method: 构建了大规模数据收集pipeline，从59K+仓库中收集142K+ Agentic PR，通过确定性合并模拟处理107K+ PR，识别冲突PR并提取细粒度冲突区域。

Result: 识别出29K+存在合并冲突的PR，冲突率达27.67%，提取了336K+个细粒度冲突区域。初步分析表明AI生成贡献中的合并冲突频繁且规模显著，不同智能体间存在差异。

Conclusion: AI生成代码的合并冲突问题普遍且严重，需要更好地理解和管理AI辅助软件开发中的集成挑战。数据集、代码及补充材料已在Zenodo公开发布。

Abstract: Software Engineering 3.0 marks a paradigm shift in software development, in which AI coding agents are no longer just assistive tools but active contributors. While prior empirical studies have examined productivity gains and acceptance patterns in AI-assisted development, the challenges associated with integrating agent-generated contributions remain less understood. In particular, merge conflicts, a fundamental aspect of collaborative software development, remain underexplored in this context. In this paper, we present AgenticFlict, a large-scale dataset of textual merge conflicts in AI coding agent pull requests (Agentic PRs). The dataset comprises 142K+ Agentic PRs collected from 59K+ repositories, of which 107K+ are successfully processed through deterministic merge simulation. Our pipeline identifies 29K+ PRs exhibiting merge conflicts, yielding a conflict rate of 27.67%, and extracts 336K+ fine-grained conflict regions across these instances. Our preliminary exploratory analysis indicates that merge conflicts are both frequent and often substantial in AI-generated contributions, with noticeable variation across agents, emphasizing the need to better understand and manage integration challenges in AI-assisted software development. The dataset, code and supplementary materials are available in zenodo: https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19396917.

</details>


### [107] [From UI to Code: Mobile Ads Detection via LLM-Unified Static-Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03561)
*Shang Ma,Wei Cheng,Yanfang Ye,Xusheng Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Mobile advertisements (ads) are essential to the app economy, yet detecting them is challenging because ad content is dynamically fetched from remote servers and rendered through diverse user interfaces (UIs), making ads difficult to locate and trigger at runtime. To address this challenge, we present ADWISE, a novel framework that formulates mobile ads detection as LLM-guided, ad-oriented UI exploration. ADWISE first performs static program analysis to identify UI widgets used to place ads, which we call ad widgets. It then uses a grounded LLM reasoning loop to navigate toward and trigger these widgets under three complementary domain guidance signals: (1) WTG-based guidance, which provides global transition priors from a statically constructed window transition graph (WTG); (2) semantic guidance, which reasons over app functionality to prioritize user-likely interaction paths; and (3) structural guidance, which applies retrieval-augmented generation to match the current UI against recurring ad-heavy layouts from a knowledge base. By combining static program analysis with LLM-based reasoning over UI structure, app semantics, and retrieved analogies, ADWISE enables more effective ads detection in complex mobile UIs. Experiments on 100 benchmark apps show that ADWISE outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 25.60% in ad widget detection. In addition, ADWISE uncovers 34.34% more ad regulation violations across six categories, directly benefiting downstream ad regulation.

</details>


### [108] [Toward Executable Repository-Level Code Generation via Environment Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03622)
*Ruwei Pan,Junlei Shen,Linhao Wu,Yueheng Zhu,Zixiong Yang,Yakun Zhang,Lu Zhang,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EnvGraph通过将仓库可执行性形式化为环境对齐问题来解决大型语言模型在仓库级代码生成中的挑战。该框架联合建模外部依赖满足和内部引用解析两个条件，在迭代对齐循环中通过统一的目标修订机制引导代码生成，显著提升了仓库级代码生成的功能正确性和非功能性质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码生成方法在仓库级代码生成中表现不佳。现有的基于代码片段合理性的评估方式无法保证生成的多文件仓库能够成功安装、解析依赖和内部引用、启动并在真实执行环境中通过验证。

Method: EnvGraph提出双层环境表示，联合建模外部依赖满足和仓库内部引用解析两个成功执行条件；使用执行证据进行归因分析；通过统一的目标修订机制在迭代对齐循环中引导仓库生成。

Result: EnvGraph在三个代表性骨干LLM上均取得最佳性能。相比最强的非EnvGraph基线，功能正确性提升5.72-5.87个百分点，非功能性质量提升4.58-8.66个百分点。

Conclusion: EnvGraph通过环境对齐方法有效解决了仓库级代码生成的可执行性验证问题，为LLM在复杂软件工程任务中的应用提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance on code generation, but existing methods still struggle with repository-level code generation under executable validation. Under this evaluation setting, success is determined not by the plausibility of isolated code fragments, but by whether a generated multi-file repository can be successfully installed, have its dependencies and internal references resolved, be launched, and be validated in a real execution environment. To address this challenge, we propose EnvGraph, a framework for repository-level code generation that formulates repository executability as an environment alignment problem. EnvGraph jointly models two coupled conditions for successful repository execution, namely external dependency satisfaction and repository-internal reference resolution. It maintains a dual-layer environment representation, uses execution evidence to perform execution-evidence-based attribution, and guides repository generation through a unified targeted revision mechanism within an iterative alignment loop. We evaluate EnvGraph on repository-level code generation with three representative backbone LLMs and compare it against representative environment-aware and repository-level baselines. Experimental results show that EnvGraph consistently achieves the best performance on these repository-level benchmarks. In particular, it outperforms the strongest non-EnvGraph baseline by an absolute margin of 5.72--5.87 percentage points in Functional Correctness and 4.58--8.66 percentage points in Non-Functional Quality.

</details>


### [109] [Persistent Cross-Attempt State Optimization for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03632)
*Ruwei Pan,Jiangshuai Wang,Qisheng Zhang,Yueheng Zhu,Linhao Wu,Zixiong Yang,Yakun Zhang,Lu Zhang,Hongyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LiveCoder是一个基于跨尝试知识优化的仓库级代码生成框架，通过维护成功知识、失败知识和历史最佳仓库来保持任务状态，实现重复生成的持续优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法孤立优化每次尝试，未能跨尝试保留和复用任务特定状态，而仓库级代码生成任务通常需要多次尝试才能解决。

Method: LiveCoder维持三类持久化任务状态：(1)成功知识——捕获先前成功仓库的可复用信号；(2)失败知识——记录不成功结果及其诊断信号；(3)历史最佳仓库——保存迄今发现的最优结果以防止回归。

Result: 在RAL-Bench上，功能得分提升22.94个百分点，仓库复用率达81.58%，成本降低53.63%；在两个基准测试、四个前沿LLM上均保持非功能质量稳定。

Conclusion: LiveCoder将重复仓库生成转化为持久化、知识驱动的优化过程，在多个LLM和基准测试上验证了有效性和效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved substantial progress in repository-level code generation. However, solving the same repository-level task often requires multiple attempts, while existing methods still optimize each attempt in isolation and do not preserve or reuse task-specific state across attempts. In this paper, we propose LiveCoder, a novel framework for repository-level code generation based on cross-attempt knowledge optimization. LiveCoder maintains persistent task-specific state from prior attempts to guide subsequent generation. This state includes success knowledge, which captures reusable signals from previously strong repositories, failure knowledge, which records unsuccessful outcomes and their diagnostic signals, and a historical-best repository, which preserves the strongest result found so far and prevents regression. These components collectively transform repeated repository generation into a persistent, knowledge-driven optimization process. We evaluate LiveCoder using four frontier LLMs on two representative repository-level code generation benchmarks. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of LiveCoder, improving the functional score by up to 22.94 percentage points, increasing repository reuse to 81.58%, and reducing cost by up to 53.63% on RAL-Bench while maintaining broadly stable non-functional quality.

</details>


### [110] [Automata Learning versus Process Mining: The Case for User Journeys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03686)
*Paul Kobialka,Andrea Pferscher,Bernhard K. Aichernig,Einar Broch Johnsen,Silvia Lizeth Tapia Tarifa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了从服务事件日志自动生成用户旅程行为模型的两种技术（自动机学习与过程挖掘），并提出了一种结合两者优势的混合方法。研究表明，过程挖掘依赖专家知识，自动机学习依赖事件分布，而混合方法能自动选择最优技术及参数设置，生成更精确的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着企业服务化转型，深入理解用户如何体验服务成为关键成功因素。现有软件工程过程缺乏用户反馈机制，而用户旅程行为模型可以描述用户与服务交互的体验过程，有助于服务改进。因此需要自动化技术从用户交互事件日志中提取行为模型。

Method: 首先比较了两种经典的行为模型生成技术：自动机学习（automata learning）和过程挖掘（process mining）。随后提出了一种混合方法，结合两种技术的优势。实验通过真实世界的数字服务事件日志进行评估。

Result: 过程挖掘技术依赖专家知识，而自动机学习技术依赖事件日志中的事件分布。混合方法能够自动为给定事件日志选择最佳方法和参数设置，生成高精度的用户旅程模型。

Conclusion: 混合方法有效结合了过程挖掘和自动机学习的优势，可自动选择最优技术方案，在用户旅程模型提取任务中表现优于单一方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: With the servitization of business, understanding how users experience services becomes a crucial success factor for companies. Therefore, there is a need to include feedback from user experiences in the software engineering process. Behavioral models of user journeys, describing how users experience their interaction with a service, can provide insights and potentially improve services. In this paper, we investigate techniques that allow the automatic generation of behavioral models from user interactions with a service, recorded in an event log. We first compare two established techniques that generate behavioral models from a given event log: automata learning and process mining. Afterward, we present a novel, hybrid method that combines both automata learning and process mining methods to overcome their limitations. For the existing techniques, we present methods to learn models of user journeys and evaluate the accuracy of the resulting models. We then compare these techniques with our novel method for the automatic extraction of user journey models from the event logs of digital services. We assess the practical applicability of all techniques by evaluating real-world applications. Our results show that process mining techniques rely on expert knowledge, while automata learning techniques depend on the distribution of events in the given event log. We further show that the proposed hybrid technique combines the strengths of both process mining and automata learning, automatically selecting the best method and parameter settings for a given event log to learn very accurate models.

</details>


### [111] [AutoReSpec: A Framework for Generating Specification using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03758)
*Ragib Shahariar Ayon,Shibbir Ahmed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoReSpec是一个协作框架，通过动态选择LLM组合和提示配置来生成可验证的形式化规格说明。当主LLM失败时，调用协作模型并利用验证器反馈进行修正。在72个Java程序的基准测试中，该方法达到67次成功，58.2%成功率和69.2%完整性，同时将评估时间缩短26.89%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM生成的形式化规格说明存在语法错误、逻辑不准确和不完整推理等问题，尤其在处理循环和分支逻辑时更为严重。现有方法（如SpecGen和FormalBench）依赖静态提示，缺乏失败恢复机制。

Method: 提出AutoReSpec协作框架：1）根据输入程序结构动态选择LLM对和提示配置；2）两级设计——主LLM生成初始规格说明，验证器检测错误；3）若主LLM失败，调用协作模型利用验证器反馈进行修正。

Result: 在72个真实和合成Java程序的基准测试中达到67次成功（93.1%通过率），58.2%成功率和69.2%完整性评分，较SpecGen和FormalBench显著提升，同时平均减少26.89%评估时间。

Conclusion: AutoReSpec为LLM驱动的形式化规格说明生成提供了一种可扩展、高效且可靠的方法，通过动态适应和协作修正机制有效解决了现有方法的局限性。

Abstract: Formal specification generation has recently drawn attention in software engineering as a way to improve program correctness without requiring manual annotations. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in this area, but early results reveal several limitations.
