### [146] [Minos: Systematically Classifying Performance and Power Characteristics of GPU Workloads on HPC Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03591)
*Rutwik Jain,Yiwei Jiang,Matthew D. Sinclair,Shivaraman Venkataraman*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Minos是一个针对GPU加速HPC集群的系统性应用分类框架，通过低成本剖析识别具有相似功耗和性能特征的负载，将多样化的应用归类为有限数量的类别，从而减少新应用的剖析开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代HPC集群因采用GPU等加速器以满足高算力需求而面临日益严峻的功耗约束问题。应用程序常出现"功耗峰值"超过加速器额定功率的情况，且不同应用对硬件优化需求各异，传统的针对特定集群剖析优化方法在规模化场景下面临效率低下、工具缺乏等挑战。

Method: Minos提出一种系统性分类机制：首先通过低开销剖析收集应用的功耗与性能特征，然后基于这些特征识别应用的相似性，最后将行为相似的负载归入有限数量的离散类别，以实现新负载的快速分类预测。

Result: 在18种主流图分析、HPC、HPC+ML和ML负载上的评估表明：Minos功耗预测平均误差为4%，性能预测平均误差为3%，较现有最优方法提升10%；在预测未知应用频率限制行为时，剖析时间减少89%。

Conclusion: Minos通过将多样化工作负载归类为有限类别，显著降低了HPC集群中功耗与性能协同优化的剖析开销，为大规模异构计算系统的资源管理提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: As large-scale HPC compute clusters increasingly adopt accelerators such as GPUs to meet the voracious demands of modern workloads, these clusters are increasingly becoming power constrained. Unfortunately, modern applications can often temporarily exceed the power ratings of the accelerators ("power spikes"). Thus, current and future HPC systems must optimize for both power and performance together. However, this is made difficult by increasingly diverse applications, which often require bespoke optimizations to run efficiently on each cluster. Traditionally researchers overcome this problem by profiling applications on specific clusters and optimizing, but the scale, algorithmic diversity, and lack of effective tools make this challenging. To overcome these inefficiencies, we propose Minos, a systematic classification mechanism that identifies similar application characteristics via low-cost profiling for power and performance. This allows us to group similarly behaving workloads into a finite number of distinct classes and reduce the overhead of extensively profiling new workloads. For example, when predicting frequency capping behavior for a previously unseen application, Minos reduces profiling time by 89%. Moreover, across 18 popular graph analytics, HPC, HPC+ML, and ML workloads, Minos achieves a mean error of 4% for power predictions and 3% for performance predictions, significantly improving predictions over state-of-the-art approaches by 10%.

</details>


### [147] [Lemonshark: Asynchronous DAG-BFT With Early Finality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03974)
*Michael Yiqing Hu,Alvin Hong Yao Yan,Yang Yihan,Liu Xiang,Li Jialin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Lemonshark是一种异步DAG-BFT协议，通过在事务级别重新解释DAG，识别出承诺（commitment）对于安全结果是充分但非必要的条件，使得节点能够在官方承诺之前完成交易，从而将延迟降低最多65%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG-BFT协议（如DAG-Rider）中，只有leader区块才能获得最优延迟，非leader区块无法独立提交，限制了系统的整体性能表现。

Method: 重新在事务级别解释DAG结构，识别出承诺对于安全结果的充分非必要条件，从而允许节点在官方commitment之前提前finalize交易。

Result: 与最先进的异步BFT协议相比，Lemonshark将延迟降低最多65%。

Conclusion: Lemonshark通过解耦dissemination与consensus的传统范式，利用事务级DAG解释实现了更早的交易最终性，在不牺牲正确性的前提下显著提升延迟性能。

Abstract: DAG-Rider popularized a new paradigm of DAG-BFT protocols, separating dissemination from consensus: all nodes disseminate transactions as blocks that reference previously known blocks, while consensus is reached by electing certain blocks as leaders. This design yields high throughput but confers optimal latency only to leader blocks; non-leader blocks cannot be committed independently.
  We present Lemonshark, an asynchronous DAG-BFT protocol that reinterprets the DAG at a transactional level and identifies conditions where commitment is sufficient -- but not necessary -- for safe results, enabling nodes to finalize transactions before official commitment, without compromising correctness. Compared to the state-of-the-art asynchronous BFT protocol, Lemonshark reduces latency by up to 65\%.

</details>


### [148] [Round-Delayed Amnesiac Flooding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04260)
*Oluwatobi Alafin,George B. Mertzios,Paul G. Spirakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RDAF是Amnesiac Flooding的变体，引入基于轮次的异步性和对抗性延迟。研究建立了RDAF的基本性质，包括不同图类型上的终止特性，并在多种对抗模型下证明了可判定性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究通信延迟环境下洪泛算法的行为，特别是在存在对抗性延迟时，理解和确保分布式协议的正确终止。

Method: 通过形式化建模RDAF，结合基于轮次的异步性，采用图论分析和可计算性理论方法进行证明。

Result: (1) 提出了包含轮次异步性的RDAF形式化模型；(2) 证明了在对抗性延迟下洪泛在无环图上必然终止；(3) 构造表明在任意有环图上可能出现非终止；(4) 在任意可计算对抗者下终止性不可判定；(5) 引入了最终周期对抗者(EPA)模型，在该模型下终止性可判定。

Conclusion: 研究结果深化了对通信延迟设置下洪泛算法的理解，为设计健壮的分布式协议提供了理论依据和指导。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive analysis of Round-Delayed Amnesiac Flooding (RDAF), a variant of Amnesiac Flooding that introduces round-based asynchrony through adversarial delays. We establish fundamental properties of RDAF, including termination characteristics for different graph types and decidability results under various adversarial models. Our key contributions include: (1) a formal model of RDAF incorporating round-based asynchrony, (2) a proof that flooding always terminates on acyclic graphs despite adversarial delays, (3) a construction showing non-termination is possible on any cyclic graph, (4) a demonstration that termination is undecidable with arbitrary computable adversaries, and (5) the introduction of Eventually Periodic Adversaries (EPA) under which termination becomes decidable. These results enhance our understanding of flooding in communication-delay settings and provide insights for designing robust distributed protocols.

</details>


### [149] [GENSERVE: Efficient Co-Serving of Heterogeneous Diffusion Model Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04335)
*Fanjiang Ye,Zhangke Li,Xinrui Zhong,Ethan Ma,Russell Chen,Kaijian Wang,Jingwei Zuo,Desen Sun,Ye Cao,Triston Cao,Myungjin Lee,Arvind Krishnamurthy,Yuke Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as the prevailing approach for text-to-image (T2I) and text-to-video (T2V) generation, yet production platforms must increasingly serve both modalities on shared GPU clusters while meeting stringent latency SLOs. Co-serving such heterogeneous workloads is challenging: T2I and T2V requests exhibit vastly different compute demands, parallelism characteristics, and latency requirements, leading to significant SLO violations in existing serving systems. We present GENSERVE, a co-serving system that leverages the inherent predictability of the diffusion process to optimize serving efficiency. A central insight is that diffusion inference proceeds in discrete, predictable steps and is naturally preemptible at step boundaries, opening a new design space for heterogeneity-aware resource management. GENSERVE introduces step-level resource adaptation through three coordinated mechanisms: intelligent video preemption, elastic sequence parallelism with dynamic batching, and an SLO-aware scheduler that jointly optimizes resource allocation across all concurrent requests. Experimental results show that GENSERVE improves the SLO attainment rate by up to 44% over the strongest baseline across diverse configurations.

</details>


### [150] [NBI-Slurm: Simplified submission of Slurm jobs with energy saving mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04558)
*Andrea Telatin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: NBI-Slurm是一个Perl软件包，为SLURM HPC集群提供简化的作业提交和管理界面，包含Perl模块库、命令行工具、TUI应用、工具包装器生成功能，以及名为"eco mode"的节能调度模式，可在非高峰时段自动延迟灵活作业，降低计算碳足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLURM原生接口复杂，用户认知负担高；生物信息学研究机构需要更简便的作业管理方式；同时面临降低计算碳排放的环保需求。

Method: 开发Perl软件包，包含：(a) Perl模块库用于程序化作业管理；(b) 命令行工具套件；(c) TUI应用程序查看和取消作业；(d) 生物信息学工具专用包装器生成功能；(e) "eco mode"节能调度模式，自动将灵活作业推迟至非高峰时段。

Result: 提供了一套完整的用户友好型SLURM作业管理解决方案，集成了交互式界面、自动化工具包装和环保调度功能。

Conclusion: NBI-Slurm有效降低了SLURM使用门槛，并通过eco mode帮助研究机构在不增加用户负担的情况下实现绿色计算目标。

Abstract: NBI-Slurm is a Perl package that provides a simplified, user-friendly interface for submitting and managing jobs on SLURM high-performance computing (HPC) clusters. It offers both a library of Perl modules for programmatic job management and a suite of command-line tools designed to reduce the cognitive overhead of SLURM's native interface. Distinctive features of NBI-Slurm are (a) TUI applications to view and cancel jobs, (b) the possibility to generate tool-specific wrappers for (bioinformatic) tools and (c) an energy-aware scheduling mode -- "eco mode" -- that automatically defers flexible jobs to off-peak periods, helping research institutions reduce their computational carbon footprint without requiring users to manually plan submission times.

</details>


### [151] [LP-GEMM: Integrating Layout Propagation into GEMM Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04599)
*César Guedes Carneiro,Lucas Alvarenga,Guido Araujo,Sandro Rigo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出LP-GEMM方法，通过在连续GEMM操作间传播打包布局来消除冗余数据重打包。实验表明在x86和RISC-V架构上平均加速2.25倍，并成功应用于Llama-3.2推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代科学计算和机器学习工作负载中，顺序依赖的GEMM操作占据主导执行时间。尽管当前BLAS库针对单个GEMM进行了激进优化，但受限于BLAS API的约束——每次调用需独立打包输入矩阵并恢复输出到规范内存布局。这导致顺序GEMM中存在冗余的打包/解包操作，浪费计算资源。

Method: 提出LP-GEMM方法，将GEMM内核分解为支持打包布局传播的组件。通过在顺序GEMM操作间传播和复用打包布局，消除不必要的数据重打包，同时在边界处保持完整的BLAS语义正确性。

Result: 在x86 (AVX-512)和RISC-V (RVV 1.0)架构上，针对MLP类和Attention类工作负载进行评估：
- 相比OpenBLAS平均加速2.25倍
- 与Intel MKL等厂商优化库相比具有竞争力
- 成功实现纯BLAS级GEMM调用的Llama-3.2推理路径

Conclusion: 利用操作间的数据布局传播可显著提升性能。LP-GEMM证明了布局传播策略在实际应用（如LLM推理）中的实用价值，为BLAS级优化提供了新方向。

Abstract: In Scientific Computing and modern Machine Learning (ML) workloads, sequences of dependent General Matrix Multiplications (GEMMs) often dominate execution time. While state-of-the-art BLAS libraries aggressively optimize individual GEMM calls, they remain constrained by the BLAS API, which requires each call to independently pack input matrices and restore outputs to a canonical memory layout. In sequential GEMMs, these constraints cause redundant packing and unpacking, wasting valuable computational resources.
  This paper introduces LP-GEMM, a decomposition of the GEMM kernel that enables packing-layout propagation across sequential GEMM operations. This approach eliminates unnecessary data repacking while preserving full BLAS semantic correctness at the boundaries. We evaluate LP-GEMM on x86 (AVX-512) and RISC-V (RVV 1.0) architectures across MLP-like and Attention-like workloads. Our results show average speedups of 2.25x over OpenBLAS on Intel x86 for sequential GEMMs and competitive gains relative to vendor-optimized libraries such as Intel MKL.
  We demonstrate the practicality of the approach beyond microbenchmarks by implementing a standalone C++ version of the Llama-3.2 inference path using exclusively BLAS-level GEMM calls. These results confirm that leveraging data layout propagation between operations can significantly boost performance.

</details>


### [152] [Edge-Oriented Orchestration of Energy Services Using Graph-Driven Swarm Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04645)
*Liana Toderean,Dragos Lazea,Vasile Ofrim,Stefania Dumbrava,Anca Hangan,Tudor Cioara*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对智能电网中物联网设备依赖增加和分布式能源管理需求，本文提出一种面向智能能源系统的边缘-雾-云统一框架，采用图数据模型进行基础设施和负载建模，使用蜂群启发式算法实现资源感知的低延迟任务卸载，并通过KubeEdge实际部署验证了动态负载下的零宕机服务迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能电网日益依赖物联网设备和分布式能源管理，需要去中心化、低延迟的能源服务编排方案。现有云计算架构无法满足实时能源管理的延迟敏感需求。

Method: 提出统一边缘雾云框架，包含：1）基于图的数据模型捕获基础设施拓扑和工作负载；2）蜂群启发式算法实现资源感知、延迟敏感的任务卸载；3）符合能源数据空间标准确保数据互操作性；4）基于区块链的工作负载认证保证可追溯性。

Result: 在真实KubeEdge环境中验证，展示了动态负载下零停机时间的服务迁移能力，同时保持服务连续性。

Conclusion: 所提框架能有效支持智能电网的去中心化低延迟能源服务编排，图模型与蜂群算法的结合为边缘环境下的任务卸载提供了可行方案，区块链与数据空间标准的集成保障了安全性和互操作性。

Abstract: As smart grids increasingly depend on IoT devices and distributed energy management, they require decentralized, low latency orchestration of energy services. We address this with a unified framework for edge fog cloud infrastructures tailored to smart energy systems. It features a graph based data model that captures infrastructure and workload, enabling efficient topology exploration and task placement. Leveraging this model, a swarm-based heuristic algorithm handles task offloading in a resource-aware, latency sensitive manner. Our framework ensures data interoperability via energy data space compliance and guarantees traceability using blockchain based workload notarization. We validate our approach with a real-world KubeEdge deployment, demonstrating zero downtime service migration under dynamic workloads while maintaining service continuity.

</details>


### [153] [The Energy Cost of Execution-Idle in GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04745)
*Yiran Lei,Jared Fernandez,Vasilis Kypriotis,Dimitrios Skarlatos,Emma Strubell,Justine Sherry,Daniel Vosler*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究发现GPU存在"执行空闲"状态（活动低但功耗高），占运行时间的19.7%和能耗的10.7%，提出两种原型解决方案来降低这一状态的能耗成本和暴露时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU已成为数据中心耗电的主要贡献者，但与CPU不同，GPU即使在可见活动接近零时仍可能保持高功耗状态。传统能效优化方法未充分考虑这一现象，导致GPU集群能耗管理存在盲区。

Method: 研究团队从大型学术AI集群收集秒级遥测数据，对多种工作负载和多个GPU代际进行特征分析，量化"执行空闲"状态的频率和能耗占比，并构建两个原型系统进行优化验证。

Result: 执行空闲状态占GPU运行时间的19.7%和能耗的10.7%。两种原型方案（自动降频和负载不平衡优化）均能有效降低能耗，但均伴随性能权衡。

Conclusion: 未来高能效GPU系统设计应将执行空闲作为一等公民（first-class）操作系统状态，从架构层面解决低活动高功耗的根本问题。

Abstract: GPUs are becoming a major contributor to data center power, yet unlike CPUs, they can remain at high power even when visible activity is near zero. We call this state execution-idle. Using per-second telemetry from a large academic AI cluster, we characterize execution-idle as a recurring low-activity yet high-power state in real deployments. Across diverse workloads and multiple GPU generations, it accounts for 19.7% of in-execution time and 10.7% of energy. This suggests a need to both reduce the cost of execution-idle and reduce exposure to it. We therefore build two prototypes: one uses automatic downscaling during execution-idle, and the other uses load imbalance to reduce exposure, both with performance trade-offs. These findings suggest that future energy-efficient GPU systems should treat execution-idle as a first-class operating state.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [154] [Scaling DPPs for RAG: Density Meets Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03240)
*Xun Sun,Baiheng Xie,Li Huang,Qiang Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ScalDPP，一种基于确定性子集过程(DPP)的多样性感知的RAG检索机制，通过轻量级P-Adapter建模检索候选项间的依赖关系，并提出Diverse Margin Loss (DML)损失函数优化多样性与密度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RAG系统采用逐点评分方式构建上下文，忽略了检索候选项之间的交互关系，导致检索结果冗余、信息密度低且缺乏互补性证据覆盖。

Method: 1) 引入确定性子集过程(DPP)建模chunks间的依赖关系；2) 设计轻量级P-Adapter实现可扩展的多样性建模；3) 提出Diverse Margin Loss (DML)set级目标函数，在DPP几何空间强制正样本互补证据链优于等规模冗余替代方案。

Result: 实验结果验证了ScalDPP的优越性，表明该方法能有效提升检索上下文的密度与多样性覆盖。

Conclusion: 多样化检索对RAG系统至关重要，ScalDPP通过DPP和DML实现了密度与多样性的联合优化，为RAG检索提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) enhances Large Language Models (LLMs) by grounding generation in external knowledge, yielding relevance responses that are aligned with factual evidence and evolving corpora. Standard RAG pipelines construct context through relevance ranking, performing point-wise scoring between the user query and each corpora chunk. This formulation, however, ignores interactions among retrieved candidates, leading to redundant contexts that dilute density and fail to surface complementary evidence. We argue that effective retrieval should optimize jointly for both density and diversity, ensuring the grounding evidence that is dense in information yet diverse in coverage. In this study, we propose ScalDPP, a diversity-aware retrieval mechanism for RAG that incorporates Determinantal Point Processes (DPPs) through a lightweight P-Adapter, enabling scalable modeling of inter-chunk dependencies and complementary context selection. In addition, we develop a novel set-level objective, Diverse Margin Loss (DML), that enforces ground-truth complementary evidence chains to dominate any equally sized redundant alternatives under DPP geometry. Experimental results demonstrate the superiority of ScalDPP, substantiating our core statement in practice.

</details>


### [155] [DRAFT: Task Decoupled Latent Reasoning for Agent Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03242)
*Lin Wang,Junfeng Fang,Dan Zhang,Fei Shen,Xiang Wang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DRAFT提出了一种用于工具使用LLM智能体安全监控的潜在推理框架，通过解耦的Extractor和Reasoner两阶段结构，在长轨迹中稀疏的风险证据中实现高效安全判断，准确率从63.27%提升至91.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型LLM智能体产生长而嘈杂的交互轨迹，其中风险关键证据稀疏，标准二元监督方法难以进行有效的信用分配，需要新的安全监控范式。

Method: DRAFT将安全判断解耦为两个可训练阶段：Extractor将完整轨迹提炼为紧凑的连续潜在向量，Reasoner联合关注潜在向量和原始轨迹进行安全预测。通过在潜在空间进行证据聚合，避免了传统的"先总结后判断"的有损管道，实现端到端可微训练。

Result: 在ASSEBench和R-Judge等基准上，DRAFT持续优于强基线，平均准确率从63.27%（LoRA）提升至91.18%，且学习到更具可分性的表示。消融实验证实Extractor和Reasoner之间存在明显协同效应。

Conclusion: 在长上下文监督和稀疏证据条件下，先进行连续潜在推理再读取判断是实现鲁棒智能体安全的可行路径。

Abstract: The advent of tool-using LLM agents shifts safety monitoring from output moderation to auditing long, noisy interaction trajectories, where risk-critical evidence is sparse-making standard binary supervision poorly suited for credit assignment. To address this, we propose DRAFT (Task Decoupled Latent Reasoning for Agent Safety), a latent reasoning framework that decouples safety judgment into two trainable stages: an Extractor that distills the full trajectory into a compact continuous latent draft, and a Reasoner that jointly attends to the draft and the original trajectory to predict safety. DRAFT avoids lossy explicit summarize-then-judge pipelines by performing evidence aggregation in latent space, enabling end-to-end differentiable training.Across benchmarks including ASSEBench and R-Judge, DRAFT consistently outperforms strong baselines, improving accuracy from 63.27% (LoRA) to 91.18% averaged over benchmarks, and learns more separable representations. Ablations demonstrate a clear synergy between the Extractor and the Reasoner.Overall, DRAFT suggests that continuous latent reasoning prior to readout is a practical path to robust agent safety under long-context supervision with sparse evidence.

</details>


### [156] [Apparent Age Estimation: Challenges and Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03335)
*Justin Rainier Go,Lorenz Bernard Marqueses,Mikaella Kaye Martinez,John Kevin Patrick Sarmiento,Abien Fred Agarap*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文针对人脸图像 apparent age estimation（表观年龄估计）中的 demographic bias（人口统计偏差）问题，对 DEX 方法进行改进（集成 MVL 和 AMRL 分布学习技术），在 IMDB-WIKI、APPA-REAL 和 FairFace 数据集上验证表明：尽管 AMRL 达到 SOTA 精度，但在不同人口统计群体（Asian、African American）上性能显著下降，且 saliency maps 显示模型对不同人群的特征关注不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Apparent age estimation 在商业个性化场景中具有重要应用价值，但现有模型普遍存在 demographic bias（人口统计偏差），即对特定种族/族裔群体的估计精度明显偏低，这限制了技术的公平部署。

Method: 采用 distribution learning（分布学习）方法改进 DEX（Deep EXpectation）框架，具体使用 Mean-Variance Loss (MVL) 和 Adaptive Mean-Residue Loss (AMRL) 两种策略；在 FairFace 数据集上进行 fairness 评估，并利用 UMAP 降维分析年龄聚类特征，通过 saliency maps 可视化模型对不同 demographic groups 的特征关注模式。

Result: AMRL 在 accuracy 上达到 SOTA 水平，但在 precision 和 demographic equity 之间存在明显 trade-off；尽管 UMAP embedding 呈现清晰的年龄聚类，saliency maps 显示模型对 Asian 和 African American 人群的特征关注模式不一致，导致这些群体的性能显著下降。

Conclusion: 单纯的技术改进（如 loss function 优化）不足以解决公平性问题；实现 accurate and fair apparent age estimation 需要：1) 整合 localized and diverse datasets（本地化、多样化数据集），2) 严格遵循 fairness validation protocols（公平性验证协议）。

Abstract: Apparent age estimation is a valuable tool for business personalization, yet current models frequently exhibit demographic biases. We review prior works on the DEX method by applying distribution learning techniques such as Mean-Variance Loss (MVL) and Adaptive Mean-Residue Loss (AMRL), and evaluate them in both accuracy and fairness. Using IMDB-WIKI, APPA-REAL, and FairFace, we demonstrate that while AMRL achieves state-of-the-art accuracy, trade-offs between precision and demographic equity persist. Despite clear age clustering in UMAP embeddings, our saliency maps indicate inconsistent feature focus across demographics, leading to significant performance degradation for Asian and African American populations. We argue that technical improvements alone are insufficient; accurate and fair apparent age estimation requires the integration of localized and diverse datasets, and strict adherence to fairness validation protocols.

</details>


### [157] [The limits of bio-molecular modeling with large language models : a cross-scale evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03361)
*Yaxin Xu,Yue Zhou,Tianyu Zhao,Fengwei An,Zhixiang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BioMol-LLM-Bench是一个跨尺度生物分子基准测试，包含26个下游任务和4个难度级别，用于系统评估LLM在生物分子发现中的能力。评估13个模型后发现：思维链数据收益有限；Mamba-Attention混合架构更适合长序列；监督微调以牺牲泛化能力为代价提升专业性能；LLM在分类任务表现良好但回归任务较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物分子系统跨分子尺度的建模是科学研究的中心挑战，现有LLM在生物分子发现中的应用缺乏系统性评估，且工具增强能力的严格评估不足。

Method: 提出BioMol-LLM-Bench跨尺度生物分子基准测试，包含26个下游任务、4个难度级别，并集成计算工具进行综合评估。

Result: 对13个代表性模型的评估揭示4个主要发现：(1)思维链数据对生物任务收益有限甚至降低性能；(2)混合Mamba-Attention架构对长序列更有效；(3)监督微调以泛化能力为代价换取专业化；(4)当前LLM分类任务表现良好但回归任务较弱。

Conclusion: 该研究为未来基于LLM的分子系统建模提供了实践指导，揭示了LLM性能与机械理解之间存在系统性差距。

Abstract: The modeling of bio-molecular system across molecular scales remains a central challenge in scientific research. Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to bio-molecular discovery, yet systematic evaluation across multi-scale biological problems and rigorous assessment of their tool-augmented capabilities remain limited. We reveal a systematic gap between LLM performance and mechanistic understanding through the proposed cross-scale bio-molecular benchmark: BioMol-LLM-Bench, a unified framework comprising 26 downstream tasks that covers 4 distinct difficulty levels, and computational tools are integrated for a more comprehensive evaluation. Evaluation on 13 representative models reveals 4 main findings: chain-of-thought data provides limited benefit and may even reduce performance on biological tasks; hybrid mamba-attention architectures are more effective for long bio-molecular sequences; supervised fine-tuning improves specialization at the cost of generalization; and current LLMs perform well on classification tasks but remain weak on challenging regression tasks. Together, these findings provide practical guidance for future LLM-based modeling of molecular systems.

</details>


### [158] [Scalable Variational Bayesian Fine-Tuning of LLMs via Orthogonalized Low-Rank Adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03388)
*Haotian Xiang,Bingcong Li,Qin Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PoLAR-VBLL框架，通过将极分解低秩适配器（PoLAR）与贝叶斯最后一层（BLL）模型结合，使用变分推断进行可扩展的贝叶斯微调，旨在解决大语言模型在安全关键应用中参数高效微调后的过度自信问题，同时实现可扩展的不确定性量化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署到安全关键应用时存在过度自信问题，尤其是在使用参数高效微调（PEFT）处理下游领域特定任务时表现更为严重。现有方法存在局限性：基于拉普拉斯近似的后验框架可能产生次优校准；变分贝叶斯训练需要在推理时多次完整前向传播整个LLM backbone，计算成本高。

Method: 1）采用贝叶斯最后一层（BLL）模型架构，将LLM作为确定性特征提取器，仅对最后一层参数进行贝叶斯推断；2）针对LoRA的秩崩溃问题，提出极分解低秩适配器表示（PoLAR），采用正交参数化结合黎曼优化提升表达能力和稳定性；3）在VBLL框架下，通过交替优化联合学习PoLAR参数和最后一层参数的后验近似。

Result: 实验结果表明，PoLAR-VBLL在多种常识推理任务的分布内和分布外数据上，均能有效提升模型的泛化能力和不确定性估计性能。

Conclusion: PoLAR-VBLL是一个灵活的框架，成功将架构增强优化与可扩展贝叶斯推断相结合，能够为LLM提供校准良好的不确定性量化能力，在保持PEFT效率的同时有效缓解过度自信问题。

Abstract: When deploying large language models (LLMs) to safety-critical applications, uncertainty quantification (UQ) is of utmost importance to self-assess the reliability of the LLM-based decisions. However, such decisions typically suffer from overconfidence, particularly after parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) for downstream domain-specific tasks with limited data. Existing methods to alleviate this issue either rely on Laplace approximation based post-hoc framework, which may yield suboptimal calibration depending on the training trajectory, or variational Bayesian training that requires multiple complete forward passes through the entire LLM backbone at inference time for Monte Carlo estimation, posing scalability challenges for deployment. To address these limitations, we build on the Bayesian last layer (BLL) model, where the LLM-based deterministic feature extractor is followed by random last layer parameters for uncertainty reasoning. Since existing low-rank adapters (LoRA) for PEFT have limited expressiveness due to rank collapse, we address this with Polar-decomposed Low-rank Adapter Representation (PoLAR), an orthogonalized parameterization paired with Riemannian optimization to enable more stable and expressive adaptation. Building on this PoLAR-BLL model, we leverage the variational (V) inference framework to put forth a scalable Bayesian fine-tuning approach which jointly seeks the PoLAR parameters and approximate posterior of the last layer parameters via alternating optimization. The resulting PoLAR-VBLL is a flexible framework that nicely integrates architecture-enhanced optimization with scalable Bayesian inference to endow LLMs with well-calibrated UQ. Our empirical results verify the effectiveness of PoLAR-VBLL in terms of generalization and uncertainty estimation on both in-distribution and out-of-distribution data for various common-sense reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [159] [Adaptive Threshold-Driven Continuous Greedy Method for Scalable Submodular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03419)
*Mohammadreza Rostami,Solmaz S. Kia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Submodular maximization under matroid constraints is a fundamental problem in combinatorial optimization with applications in sensing, data summarization, active learning, and resource allocation. While the Sequential Greedy (SG) algorithm achieves only a $\frac{1}{2}$-approximation due to irrevocable selections, Continuous Greedy (CG) attains the optimal $\bigl(1-\frac{1}{e}\bigr)$-approximation via the multilinear relaxation, at the cost of a progressively dense decision vector that forces agents to exchange feature embeddings for nearly every ground-set element. We propose \textit{ATCG} (\underline{A}daptive \underline{T}hresholded \underline{C}ontinuous \underline{G}reedy), which gates gradient evaluations behind a per-partition progress ratio $η_i$, expanding each agent's active set only when current candidates fail to capture sufficient marginal gain, thereby directly bounding which feature embeddings are ever transmitted. Theoretical analysis establishes a curvature-aware approximation guarantee with effective factor $τ_{\mathrm{eff}}=\max\{τ,1-c\}$, interpolating between the threshold-based guarantee and the low-curvature regime where \textit{ATCG} recovers the performance of CG. Experiments on a class-balanced prototype selection problem over a subset of the CIFAR-10 animal dataset show that \textit{ATCG} achieves objective values comparable to those of the full CG method while substantially reducing communication overhead through adaptive active-set expansion.

</details>


### [160] [MetaSAEs: Joint Training with a Decomposability Penalty Produces More Atomic Sparse Autoencoder Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03436)
*Matthew Levinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合训练目标，通过小型meta SAE重建主SAE的解码器列来惩罚子空间混合问题，使SAE潜在特征更加原子化（每个潜在特征代表单一一致概念）。在GPT-2 large上，mean |φ|降低7.5%，fuzzing分数提升7.6%；在Gemma 2 9B上，ΔFuzz提升8.6%。定性分析证实多语义token上的特征被分解为语义distinct的子特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前SAE的潜在特征存在"子空间混合"问题——单一特征可能在语义上distinct且无共同表征基础的不同上下文中激活，导致特征不够原子化。这对安全相关应用（对齐检测、模型引导）造成困扰。

Method: 引入小型meta SAE与主SAE联合训练：meta SAE的任务是稀疏重建主SAE的解码器方向。当主SAE的解码器方向位于其他解码器方向张成的子空间中时，容易被meta字典稀疏压缩，此时对主SAE施加惩罚。这创造梯度压力促使解码器方向更加相互独立，从而抵抗稀疏meta压缩。

Result: 在GPT-2 large (layer 20)上，mean |φ|降低7.5%（相对于相同数据和架构的solo SAE基线），fuzzing分数提升7.6%。在Gemma 2 9B上，ΔFuzz提升8.6%。定性分析显示，多语义token上的特征被分解为语义distinct的子特征。

Conclusion: 通过meta SAE辅助的联合训练目标可有效减少SAE的子空间混合问题，提高特征的原子性，且重构开销适中，方法具有一定的跨模型迁移性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly used for safety-relevant applications including alignment detection and model steering. These use cases require SAE latents to be as atomic as possible. Each latent should represent a single coherent concept drawn from a single underlying representational subspace. In practice, SAE latents blend representational subspaces together. A single feature can activate across semantically distinct contexts that share no true common representation, muddying an already complex picture of model computation. We introduce a joint training objective that directly penalizes this subspace blending. A small meta SAE is trained alongside the primary SAE to sparsely reconstruct the primary SAE's decoder columns; the primary SAE is penalized whenever its decoder directions are easy to reconstruct from the meta dictionary. This occurs whenever latent directions lie in a subspace spanned by other primary directions. This creates gradient pressure toward more mutually independent decoder directions that resist sparse meta-compression.
  On GPT-2 large (layer 20), the selected configuration reduces mean $|\varphi|$ by 7.5% relative to an identical solo SAE trained on the same data. Automated interpretability (fuzzing) scores improve by 7.6%, providing external validation of the atomicity gain independent of the training and co-occurrence metrics. Reconstruction overhead is modest. Results on Gemma 2 9B are directional. On not-fully-converged SAEs, the same parameterization yields the best results, a $+8.6\%$ $Δ$Fuzz. Though directional, this is an encouraging sign that the method transfers to a larger model. Qualitative analysis confirms that features firing on polysemantic tokens are split into semantically distinct sub-features, each specializing in a distinct representational subspace.

</details>


### [161] [Olmo Hybrid: From Theory to Practice and Back](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03444)
*William Merrill,Yanhong Li,Tyler Romero,Anej Svete,Caia Costello,Pradeep Dasigi,Dirk Groeneveld,David Heineman,Bailey Kuehl,Nathan Lambert,Jacob Morrison,Luca Soldaini,Finbarr Timbers,Pete Walsh,Noah A. Smith,Hannaneh Hajishirzi,Ashish Sabharwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了混合注意力与线性RNN的混合模型相比纯Transformer的优势。理论上证明混合模型可表达超越两者的任务（如代码执行），实践上训练了7B参数的Olmo Hybrid模型，在标准预训练和中期训练评估中优于Olmo 3，且Scaling效率显著更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非Transformer语言模型（尤其是线性RNN和混合模型）展现出潜力，但学界对是否值得投入资源Scaling这些架构缺乏共识。现需证据证明混合模型的优势是否足以弥补其风险和努力。

Method: 1. 理论层面：证明混合模型不仅继承Transformer和线性RNN的表达力，还能表达超越两者的任务（如代码执行）。2. 实践层面：训练Olmo Hybrid（7B参数），将滑动窗口层替换为Gated DeltaNet层，与Olmo 3 7B进行对比实验。3. 理论分析：解释为何更强的表达力应转化为更好的Scaling效率。

Result: 1. 混合模型在理论上可超越Transformer和线性RNN的表达能力边界。2. Olmo Hybrid在标准预训练和中期训练评估中优于Olmo 3。3. 混合模型的Scaling效率显著高于Transformer。4. 理论解释了更强表达力与更好Scaling之间的联系。

Conclusion: 混合注意力与循环层的模型是语言建模范式的强力扩展。其价值不仅是推理时降低内存，更是作为获得更具表达力模型的基本方式，能在预训练阶段实现更好的Scaling。混合模型是未来大规模语言模型的重要发展方向。

Abstract: Recent work has demonstrated the potential of non-transformer language models, especially linear recurrent neural networks (RNNs) and hybrid models that mix recurrence and attention. Yet there is no consensus on whether the potential benefits of these new architectures justify the risk and effort of scaling them up. To address this, we provide evidence for the advantages of hybrid models over pure transformers on several fronts. First, theoretically, we show that hybrid models do not merely inherit the expressivity of transformers and linear RNNs, but can express tasks beyond both, such as code execution. Putting this theory to practice, we train Olmo Hybrid, a 7B-parameter model largely comparable to Olmo 3 7B but with the sliding window layers replaced by Gated DeltaNet layers. We show that Olmo Hybrid outperforms Olmo 3 across standard pretraining and mid-training evaluations, demonstrating the benefit of hybrid models in a controlled, large-scale setting. We find that the hybrid model scales significantly more efficiently than the transformer, explaining its higher performance. However, its unclear why greater expressivity on specific formal problems should result in better scaling or superior performance on downstream tasks unrelated to those problems. To explain this apparent gap, we return to theory and argue why increased expressivity should translate to better scaling efficiency, completing the loop. Overall, our results suggest that hybrid models mixing attention and recurrent layers are a powerful extension to the language modeling paradigm: not merely to reduce memory during inference, but as a fundamental way to obtain more expressive models that scale better during pretraining.

</details>


### [162] [Sampling Parallelism for Fast and Efficient Bayesian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04736)
*Asena Karolin Özdemir,Lars H. Heyen,Arvid Weyrauch,Achim Streit,Markus Götz,Charlotte Debus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Machine learning models, and deep neural networks in particular, are increasingly deployed in risk-sensitive domains such as healthcare, environmental forecasting, and finance, where reliable quantification of predictive uncertainty is essential. However, many uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods remain difficult to apply due to their substantial computational cost. Sampling-based Bayesian learning approaches, such as Bayesian neural networks (BNNs), are particularly expensive since drawing and evaluating multiple parameter samples rapidly exhausts memory and compute resources. These constraints have limited the accessibility and exploration of Bayesian techniques thus far. To address these challenges, we introduce sampling parallelism, a simple yet powerful parallelization strategy that targets the primary bottleneck of sampling-based Bayesian learning: the samples themselves. By distributing sample evaluations across multiple GPUs, our method reduces memory pressure and training time without requiring architectural changes or extensive hyperparameter tuning. We detail the methodology and evaluate its performance on a few example tasks and architectures, comparing against distributed data parallelism (DDP) as a baseline. We further demonstrate that sampling parallelism is complementary to existing strategies by implementing a hybrid approach that combines sample and data parallelism. Our experiments show near-perfect scaling when the sample number is scaled proportionally to the computational resources, confirming that sample evaluations parallelize cleanly. Although DDP achieves better raw speedups under scaling with constant workload, sampling parallelism has a notable advantage: by applying independent stochastic augmentations to the same batch on each GPU, it increases augmentation diversity and thus reduces the number of epochs required for convergence.

</details>


### [163] [Three Phases of Expert Routing: How Load Balance Evolves During Mixture-of-Experts Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.04230)
*Charafeddine Mouzouni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将MoE专家路由建模为拥塞博弈，通过单一参数γ_eff量化平衡-质量权衡。对OLMoE-1B-7B（20个检查点）和OpenMoE-8B（6个检查点）训练过程的追踪揭示了三阶段轨迹： surge phase（γ_eff: 14→36-39）、stabilization phase（B_0稳定在2.3-2.4）、relaxation phase（γ_eff: 27→9）。该非单调轨迹表明早期MoE训练优先平衡，晚期优先质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解MoE训练动态：从收敛模型的post-hoc分析无法揭示训练过程中的平衡-质量权衡演化，缺少统一理论框架。

Method: 1. 将MoE token路由建模为拥塞博弈（congestion game）
2. 引入单一有效参数γ_eff量化平衡-质量权衡
3. 跨训练检查点追踪γ_eff变化
4. 多类型扩展：基于token聚类的有效拥塞分解
5. 验证：16层平均提升30%负载预测精度，四种独立质量估计器验证（r≥0.89）

Result: 发现三阶段轨迹：Surge phase（γ_eff: 14→36-39，step 30K-40K峰值）→ Stabilization phase（B_0: 2.4→2.3，step 100K-400K）→ Relaxation phase（γ_eff: 27→9，step 400K-1.2M）。单类型均衡退化为温度缩放softmax（L1: MFG=0.199 vs softmax=0.200）。

Conclusion: 1. 博弈论揭示了温度参数的演化含义，而非替代softmax预测
2. 早期训练优先平衡（surge），晚期训练优先质量（relaxation）
3. 该非单调轨迹对收敛模型的post-hoc分析不可见
4. 多类型扩展有效提升负载预测（16层平均30%）

Abstract: We model Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) token routing as a congestion game with a single effective parameter, the congestion coefficient gamma_eff, that quantifies the balance-quality tradeoff. Tracking gamma_eff across training checkpoints of two open-source MoE models, OLMoE-1B-7B (20 checkpoints, with dense sampling in the surge region) and OpenMoE-8B (6 checkpoints), reveals a three-phase trajectory: a surge phase where the router learns to balance load (gamma_eff: 14 to 36-39, peaking in the step 30K-40K region), a stabilization phase where experts specialize under steady balance (B_0: 2.4 to 2.3, steps 100K-400K), and a relaxation phase where the router trades balance for quality as experts differentiate (gamma_eff: 27 to 9, steps 400K-1.2M). This non-monotone trajectory, invisible to post-hoc analysis of converged models, reveals that early MoE training prioritizes balance while late training prioritizes quality. The theoretical framework is honest about its limits: the single-type equilibrium reduces to temperature-scaled softmax (held-out L1: MFG = 0.199 vs. softmax = 0.200). The game is not a better predictor; it reveals what the temperature means and, critically, how that temperature evolves. We complement the dynamics with an effective congestion decomposition, a multi-type extension that improves load prediction via token clustering on all 16 layers (mean: 30%), scope diagnostics (K/M, epsilon_l), and robustness verification across four independent quality estimators (r >= 0.89). All confidence intervals are from bootstrap resampling over 50 independent text batches.

</details>


### [164] [Earth Embeddings Reveal Diverse Urban Signals from Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03456)
*Wenjing Gong,Udbhav Srivastava,Yuchen Wang,Yuhao Jia,Qifan Wu,Weishan Bai,Yifan Yang,Xiao Huang,Xinyue Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基准测试了三种地球嵌入模型（AlphaEarth、Prithvi、Clay）在城市监测中的表现，发现这些模型能够有效从卫星图像预测社区级城市指标，尤其对与建成环境结构直接相关的指标（如慢性病负担、主要通勤模式）预测效果最佳，但对行为性指标（如骑行）预测困难。模型性能在不同城市间差异显著，但在时间维度上保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统城市指标（普查、调查、行政记录）存在成本高、空间不一致、更新慢等问题。地球嵌入模型（如AlphaEarth、Prithvi、Clay）能否成为可扩展、低成本的城市监测替代方案尚不明确。

Method: 在6个美国大都市区（2020-2023年）构建统一监督学习框架，使用地球嵌入预测14项社区级指标（犯罪、收入、健康、出行行为），设置全球、城市级、年份级、城市-年份级四种评估场景。

Result: （1）地球嵌入捕获大量城市变异；（2）与建成环境结构直接相关的指标（如慢性健康负担、主要通勤模式）预测效果最佳；（3）行为性/政策敏感性指标（如骑行）难以预测；（4）跨城市性能差异显著，跨年性能稳定；（5）64维AlphaEarth嵌入优于64维Prithvi/Clay。

Conclusion: 本研究为评估地球嵌入在城市遥感中的应用提供了基准，证明了其在可持续发展目标（SDG）对标的城市尺度监测中作为可扩展、低成本特征的潜力。

Abstract: Conventional urban indicators derived from censuses, surveys, and administrative records are often costly, spatially inconsistent, and slow to update. Recent geospatial foundation models enable Earth embeddings, compact satellite image representations transferable across downstream tasks, but their utility for neighborhood-scale urban monitoring remains unclear. Here, we benchmark three Earth embedding families, AlphaEarth, Prithvi, and Clay, for urban signal prediction across six U.S. metropolitan areas from 2020 to 2023. Using a unified supervised-learning framework, we predict 14 neighborhood-level indicators spanning crime, income, health, and travel behavior, and evaluate performance under four settings: global, city-wise, year-wise, and city-year. Results show that Earth embeddings capture substantial urban variation, with the highest predictive skill for outcomes more directly tied to built-environment structure, including chronic health burdens and dominant commuting modes. By contrast, indicators shaped more strongly by fine-scale behavior and local policy, such as cycling, remain difficult to infer. Predictive performance varies markedly across cities but remains comparatively stable across years, indicating strong spatial heterogeneity alongside temporal robustness. Exploratory analysis suggests that cross-city variation in predictive performance is associated with urban form in task-specific ways. Controlled dimensionality experiments show that representation efficiency is critical: compact 64-dimensional AlphaEarth embeddings remain more informative than 64-dimensional reductions of Prithvi and Clay. This study establishes a benchmark for evaluating Earth embeddings in urban remote sensing and demonstrates their potential as scalable, low-cost features for SDG-aligned neighborhood-scale urban monitoring.

</details>


### [165] [Investigating Data Interventions for Subgroup Fairness: An ICU Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03478)
*Erin Tan,Judy Hanwen Shen,Irene Y. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了医疗场景中组合不同数据源改善子群体模型性能的局限性，发现数据添加既可能改善也可能损害模型公平性与性能，且直觉性的数据选择策略往往不可靠，需要结合模型后校准与数据策略两种方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在医疗等高风险场景中，算法偏见会加剧对特定人群的伤害。尽管"修复数据"是常见干预手段，但实际中可用的额外数据源往往不理想，数据规模的增加可能被分布偏移所抵消，导致子群体性能改善具有不稳定性。

Method: 研究在eICU和MIMIC-IV两个数据集上，通过实验比较模型无关的后验校准（post-hoc calibration）与以数据为中心的添加策略（data-centric addition），评估不同数据组合方式对子群体性能与公平性的影响。

Result: 数据添加对模型公平性和性能的改善效果不稳定——既可能帮助也可能伤害模型；直觉性的数据选择策略不可靠；模型校准与数据策略相结合才能有效改善子群体表现。

Conclusion: 质疑"更好的数据"就能解决公平性挑战的传统观点，证明单纯依靠数据质量改进并不充分，必须同时采用数据策略与模型校准两种手段才能提升机器学习在敏感应用中的公平性。

Abstract: In high-stakes settings where machine learning models are used to automate decision-making about individuals, the presence of algorithmic bias can exacerbate systemic harm to certain subgroups of people. These biases often stem from the underlying training data. In practice, interventions to "fix the data" depend on the actual additional data sources available -- where many are less than ideal. In these cases, the effects of data scaling on subgroup performance become volatile, as the improvements from increased sample size are counteracted by the introduction of distribution shifts in the training set. In this paper, we investigate the limitations of combining data sources to improve subgroup performance within the context of healthcare. Clinical models are commonly trained on datasets comprised of patient electronic health record (EHR) data from different hospitals or admission departments. Across two such datasets, the eICU Collaborative Research Database and the MIMIC-IV dataset, we find that data addition can both help and hurt model fairness and performance, and many intuitive strategies for data selection are unreliable. We compare model-based post-hoc calibration and data-centric addition strategies to find that the combination of both is important to improve subgroup performance. Our work questions the traditional dogma of "better data" for overcoming fairness challenges by comparing and combining data- and model-based approaches.

</details>


### [166] [Improving Feasibility via Fast Autoencoder-Based Projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03489)
*Maria Chzhen,Priya L. Donti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于自编码器的数据驱动方法，将非凸可行集映射到凸潜在空间实现快速约束修正。相比传统求解器，该方法计算成本低，适用于约束优化和强化学习问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在真实世界的学习和控制系统中，强制执行复杂（如非凸）操作约束是一项关键挑战。现有方法难以高效地强制执行一般类别的约束。

Method: 提出一种新的数据驱动摊销方法，使用训练好的自编码器作为近似投影器。具体地，使用对抗性目标训练自编码器，学习可行集的凸潜在表示，然后通过将潜在表示投影到简单凸形状上再解码回原始可行集来快速修正不可行预测。

Result: 该方法在具有挑战性非凸约束的约束优化和强化学习问题上进行了测试，结果表明该方法能以较低的计算成本有效强制执行约束。

Conclusion: 该方法为昂贵的传统求解器可行性修正技术提供了一种实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Enforcing complex (e.g., nonconvex) operational constraints is a critical challenge in real-world learning and control systems. However, existing methods struggle to efficiently enforce general classes of constraints. To address this, we propose a novel data-driven amortized approach that uses a trained autoencoder as an approximate projector to provide fast corrections to infeasible predictions. Specifically, we train an autoencoder using an adversarial objective to learn a structured, convex latent representation of the feasible set. This enables rapid correction of neural network outputs by projecting their associated latent representations onto a simple convex shape before decoding into the original feasible set. We test our approach on a diverse suite of constrained optimization and reinforcement learning problems with challenging nonconvex constraints. Results show that our method effectively enforces constraints at a low computational cost, offering a practical alternative to expensive feasibility correction techniques based on traditional solvers.

</details>


### [167] [Online learning of smooth functions on $\mathbb{R}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03525)
*Jesse Geneson,Kuldeep Singh,Alexander Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究实数线上对抗性在线学习问题，考察累积p-损失下函数类G_q的学习难度。主要发现：标准模型在ℝ上对任意p≥1和q>1是不适定的（对手可强迫无限损失）。为此提出三种修改场景限制远离历史查询的影响，并在这些场景下获得尖锐特征。特别地，场景3存在相变现象；多维情形(d≥2)即使在修改场景下仍无法避免无限损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准对抗性在线学习模型在实数域ℝ上对函数类G_q（导数L^q范数有界的绝对连续函数）变得不适定：对于任意p≥1和q>1，对手可通过策略性查询强迫学习器产生无限累积损失。这促使作者探索三种修改场景来规避这一根本性障碍。

Method: 设计三种修改学习场景：(1)限制对手每次查询在历史查询的距离1以内；(2)仅对距离历史查询1以内的轮次计算损失；(3)引入与最近历史查询距离相关的衰减权重函数g(·)。对每种场景进行最坏情况分析，寻找对手策略与学习器最优上界的匹配下界。

Result: 场景1-2在多种机制下获得尖锐特征；场景3揭示清洁的相变现象——若g衰减过慢对手可强迫无限加权损失，而快速衰减如指数衰减g(z)=e^{-cz}在二次情形p=q=2下可获得有限且尖锐的保证；多维切片类G_{q,d}对任意d≥2，即使在三种修改场景下对手仍可强迫无限损失。

Conclusion: 一维情形在修改场景下可学习，核心在于限制查询的空间分散性；多维扩展本质性地失效，d≥2时切片类过于宽松。指数权重方案为防止无限损失提供了有效机制，研究为高维在线学习提供了重要的负面结果和正面保证。

Abstract: We study adversarial online learning of real-valued functions on $\mathbb{R}$. In each round the learner is queried at $x_t\in\mathbb{R}$, predicts $\hat y_t$, and then observes the true value $f(x_t)$; performance is measured by cumulative $p$-loss $\sum_{t\ge 1}|\hat y_t-f(x_t)|^p$. For the class \[ \mathcal{G}_q=\Bigl\{f:\mathbb{R}\to\mathbb{R}\ \text{absolutely continuous}:\ \int_{\mathbb{R}}|f'(x)|^q\,dx\le 1\Bigr\}, \] we show that the standard model becomes ill-posed on $\mathbb{R}$: for every $p\ge 1$ and $q>1$, an adversary can force infinite loss. Motivated by this obstruction, we analyze three modified learning scenarios that limit the influence of queries that are far from previously observed inputs. In Scenario 1 the adversary must choose each new query within distance $1$ of some past query. In Scenario 2 the adversary may query anywhere, but the learner is penalized only on rounds whose query lies within distance $1$ of a past query. In Scenario 3 the loss in round $t$ is multiplied by a weight $g(\min_{j<t}|x_t-x_j|)$.
  We obtain sharp characterizations for Scenarios 1-2 in several regimes. For Scenario 3 we identify a clean threshold phenomenon: if $g$ decays too slowly, then the adversary can force infinite weighted loss. In contrast, for rapidly decaying weights such as $g(z)=e^{-cz}$ we obtain finite and sharp guarantees in the quadratic case $p=q=2$. Finally, we study a natural multivariable slice generalization $\mathcal{G}_{q,d}$ of $\mathcal{G}_q$ on $\mathbb{R}^d$ and show a sharp dichotomy: while the one-dimensional case admits finite opt-values in certain regimes, for every $d\ge 2$ the slice class $\mathcal{G}_{q,d}$ is too permissive, and even under Scenarios 1-3 an adversary can force infinite loss.

</details>


### [168] [Choosing the Right Regularizer for Applied ML: Simulation Benchmarks of Popular Scikit-learn Regularization Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03541)
*Benjamin S. Knight,Ahsaas Bajaj*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过134,400次模拟实验，系统比较了Ridge、Lasso、ElasticNet和Post-Lasso OLS四种正则化框架的性能表现，为机器学习工程师提供基于特征空间属性的框架选择决策指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正则化方法在机器学习中广泛使用，但不同框架在各种条件下的相对性能尚缺乏系统性实证比较，尤其是面对高条件数、多重共线性和低信噪比等挑战场景时。

Method: 基于8个生产级机器学习模型构建7维流形，执行134,400次蒙特卡洛模拟，系统评估四种经典正则化框架（Ridge、Lasso、ElasticNet、Post-Lasso OLS）。

Result: 当样本量充足（n/p ≥ 78）时，三种L1/L2正则化方法预测精度相近；但在高条件数（kappa）和低信噪比（SNR）条件下，Lasso召回率急剧下降至0.18，而ElasticNet仍维持0.93。

Conclusion: 建议在高条件数和小样本场景下避免使用Lasso或Post-Lasso OLS；提供目标驱动的决策流程图以指导scikit-learn框架选择。

Abstract: This study surveys the historical development of regularization, tracing its evolution from stepwise regression in the 1960s to recent advancements in formal error control, structured penalties for non-independent features, Bayesian methods, and l0-based regularization (among other techniques). We empirically evaluate the performance of four canonical frameworks -- Ridge, Lasso, ElasticNet, and Post-Lasso OLS -- across 134,400 simulations spanning a 7-dimensional manifold grounded in eight production-grade machine learning models. Our findings demonstrate that for prediction accuracy when the sample-to-feature ratio is sufficient (n/p >= 78), Ridge, Lasso, and ElasticNet are nearly interchangeable. However, we find that Lasso recall is highly fragile under multicollinearity; at high condition numbers (kappa) and low SNR, Lasso recall collapses to 0.18 while ElasticNet maintains 0.93. Consequently, we advise practitioners against using Lasso or Post-Lasso OLS at high kappa with small sample sizes. The analysis concludes with an objective-driven decision guide to assist machine learning engineers in selecting the optimal scikit-learn-supported framework based on observable feature space attributes.

</details>


### [169] [BlazeFL: Fast and Deterministic Federated Learning Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03606)
*Kitsuya Azuma,Takayuki Nishio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BlazeFL是一个轻量级单节点联邦学习模拟框架，通过自由线程共享内存执行和确定性随机数管理，在保证可重复性的同时实现高效并行训练，在CIFAR-10分类任务中最高获得3.1倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前联邦学习研究依赖单节点模拟，存在并行客户端训练引入的非确定性（共享随机状态和调度可变性）问题，导致研究者不得不在吞吐量与可重复性之间权衡，或在复杂框架中实现自定义控制逻辑。

Method: BlazeFL采用基于线程的并行化与内存内参数交换（避免序列化与进程间通信开销），并为每个客户端分配独立的随机数生成器（RNG）流，以支持确定性执行。在固定软硬件环境下，当随机算子使用BlazeFL管理的生成器时，可实现线程模式和进程模式下的位级一致结果。

Result: 在CIFAR-10图像分类实验中，BlazeFL相比广泛使用的开源基线显著缩短执行时间，在通信密集型工作负载上实现最高3.1倍加速，同时保持轻量级依赖。

Conclusion: BlazeFL通过创新性的线程并行设计和隔离RNG流管理，成功解决了联邦学习模拟中效率与可重复性之间的trade-off，为研究者提供了一个高效、可重复、轻量级的单节点模拟方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research increasingly relies on single-node simulations with hundreds or thousands of virtual clients, making both efficiency and reproducibility essential. Yet parallel client training often introduces nondeterminism through shared random state and scheduling variability, forcing researchers to trade throughput for reproducibility or to implement custom control logic within complex frameworks. We present BlazeFL, a lightweight framework for single-node FL simulation that alleviates this trade-off through free-threaded shared-memory execution and deterministic randomness management. BlazeFL uses thread-based parallelism with in-memory parameter exchange between the server and clients, avoiding serialization and inter-process communication overhead. To support deterministic execution, BlazeFL assigns isolated random number generator (RNG) streams to clients. Under a fixed software/hardware stack, and when stochastic operators consume BlazeFL-managed generators, this design yields bitwise-identical results across repeated high-concurrency runs in both thread-based and process-based modes. In CIFAR-10 image-classification experiments, BlazeFL substantially reduces execution time relative to a widely used open-source baseline, achieving up to 3.1$\times$ speedup on communication-dominated workloads while preserving a lightweight dependency footprint. Our open-source implementation is available at: https://github.com/kitsuyaazuma/blazefl.

</details>


### [170] [Algebraic Diversity: Group-Theoretic Spectral Estimation from Single Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03634)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We prove that temporal averaging over multiple observations can be replaced by algebraic group action on a single observation for second-order statistical estimation. A General Replacement Theorem establishes conditions under which a group-averaged estimator from one snapshot achieves equivalent subspace decomposition to multi-snapshot covariance estimation, and an Optimality Theorem proves that the symmetric group is universally optimal (yielding the KL transform). The framework unifies the DFT, DCT, and KLT as special cases of group-matched spectral transforms, with a closed-form double-commutator eigenvalue problem for polynomial-time optimal group selection. Five applications are demonstrated: MUSIC DOA estimation from a single snapshot, massive MIMO channel estimation with 64% throughput gain, single-pulse waveform classification at 90% accuracy, graph signal processing with non-Abelian groups, and a new algebraic analysis of transformer LLMs revealing that RoPE uses the wrong algebraic group for 70-80% of attention heads across five models (22,480 head observations), that the optimal group is content-dependent, and that spectral-concentration-based pruning improves perplexity at the 13B scale. All diagnostics require a single forward pass with no gradients or training.

</details>


### [171] [Delayed Homomorphic Reinforcement Learning for Environments with Delayed Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03641)
*Jongsoo Lee,Jangwon Kim,Soohee Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对强化学习中延迟反馈导致的马尔可夫假设破坏和状态空间爆炸问题，提出基于MDP同态的延迟同态强化学习(DHRL)框架，通过折叠信念等价状态来压缩状态空间，实现高效策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实系统中强化学习常面临延迟反馈问题，破坏马尔可夫假设；现有状态增强方法导致状态空间指数爆炸，增加样本复杂度；现有方法对actor和critic的处理不统一，无法高效解决延迟问题。

Method: 提出DHRL框架，基于MDP同态理论，将信念等价的状态折叠为抽象MDP中的单一状态，从而压缩状态空间；提供压缩边界和样本复杂度的理论分析；设计实现算法。

Result: 在MuJoCo连续控制任务上的实验表明，DHRL算法优于现有基于增强的基线方法，尤其在长延迟条件下表现突出。

Conclusion: DHRL通过MDP同态实现状态空间压缩，在保证最优性不损失的前提下，提供了一种结构化且样本高效的延迟反馈处理方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning in real-world systems is often accompanied by delayed feedback, which breaks the Markov assumption and impedes both learning and control. Canonical state augmentation approaches cause the state-space explosion, which introduces a severe sample-complexity burden. Despite recent progress, the state-of-the-art augmentation-based baselines remain incomplete: they either predominantly reduce the burden on the critic or adopt non-unified treatments for the actor and critic. To provide a structured and sample-efficient solution, we propose delayed homomorphic reinforcement learning (DHRL), a framework grounded in MDP homomorphisms that collapses belief-equivalent augmented states and enables efficient policy learning on the resulting abstract MDP without loss of optimality. We provide theoretical analyses of state-space compression bounds and sample complexity, and introduce a practical algorithm. Experiments on continuous control tasks in MuJoCo benchmark confirm that our algorithm outperforms strong augmentation-based baselines, particularly under long delays.

</details>


### [172] [Automated Attention Pattern Discovery at Scale in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03764)
*Jonathan Katzy,Razvan-Mihai Popescu,Erik Mekkes,Arie van Deursen,Maliheh Izadi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出通过挖掘Java代码数据集中的 Completion 场景来研究LLM的重复行为，利用注意力模式作为可扩展的可解释性信号。引入AP-MAE模型，基于Vision Transformer重建被遮蔽的注意力模式，实现跨模型泛化、重复模式发现、生成正确性预测及选择性干预等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可解释性方法的局限：(1) 精确解释仅适用于受控环境的小范围行为，难以泛化；(2) 计算资源需求过高，难以扩展到大规模研究。亟需一种可扩展的全局可解释性方法。

Method: 利用代码的结构化特性，从Java代码数据集中收集注意力头生成的注意力模式。提出AP-MAE（Attention Pattern - Masked Autoencoder），基于Vision Transformer架构高效重建被遮蔽的注意力模式。

Result: 在StarCoder2上的实验表明AP-MAE能够：(i) 高精度重建遮蔽注意力模式；(ii) 跨未见模型泛化且性能衰减极小；(iii) 发现推理过程中的重复模式；(iv) 在无真值情况下预测生成正确性，准确率55%-70%；(v) 选择性干预可提升准确率13.6%，过度干预则导致崩溃。

Conclusion: 注意力模式是可扩展的可解释性信号，AP-MAE为LLM的分析和干预提供了可迁移的基础框架，同时可作为细粒度机械可解释性方法的筛选程序。

Abstract: Large language models have found success by scaling up capabilities to work in general settings. The same can unfortunately not be said for interpretability methods. The current trend in mechanistic interpretability is to provide precise explanations of specific behaviors in controlled settings. These often do not generalize, or are too resource intensive for larger studies. In this work we propose to study repeated behaviors in large language models by mining completion scenarios in Java code datasets, through exploiting the structured nature of code. We collect the attention patterns generated in the attention heads to demonstrate that they are scalable signals for global interpretability of model components. We show that vision models offer a promising direction for analyzing attention patterns at scale. To demonstrate this, we introduce the Attention Pattern - Masked Autoencoder(AP-MAE), a vision transformer-based model that efficiently reconstructs masked attention patterns. Experiments on StarCoder2 show that AP-MAE (i) reconstructs masked attention patterns with high accuracy, (ii) generalizes across unseen models with minimal degradation, (iii) reveals recurring patterns across inferences, (iv) predicts whether a generation will be correct without access to ground truth, with accuracies ranging from 55% to 70% depending on the task, and (v) enables targeted interventions that increase accuracy by 13.6% when applied selectively, but cause collapse when applied excessively. These results establish attention patterns as a scalable signal for interpretability and demonstrate that AP-MAE provides a transferable foundation for both analysis and intervention in large language models. Beyond its standalone value, AP-MAE also serves as a selection procedure to guide fine-grained mechanistic approaches. We release code and models to support future work in large-scale interpretability.

</details>


### [173] [CountsDiff: A Diffusion Model on the Natural Numbers for Generation and Imputation of Count-Based Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03779)
*Renzo G. Soatto,Anders Hoel,Greycen Ren,Shorna Alam,Stephen Bates,Nikolaos P. Daskalakis,Caroline Uhler,Maria Skoularidou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CountsDiff是一个针对离散有序数据（自然数计数）设计的扩散模型框架，通过简化Blackout扩散框架的参数化方式（基于生存概率调度和显式损失加权），并引入连续时间训练、无分类器引导、churn/remasking反向动态等现代扩散模型技术，在图像生成和单细胞RNA-seq归因任务上取得了与或超越现有最优方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在连续数据和基于token的离散数据上表现优异，但对离散有序数据（如计数数据、自然数）的建模能力不足，这限制了其在对基因表达计数等生物数据上的应用。

Method: CountsDiff通过以下方式扩展Blackout扩散框架：(1) 引入基于生存概率调度和显式损失加权的直接参数化；(2) 采用连续时间训练目标；(3) 实现无分类器引导；(4) 开发churn/remasking反向动态机制以支持非单调反向轨迹。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和CelebA图像数据集上验证了设计参数的有效性；在胎儿细胞和心脏细胞图谱的单细胞RNA-seq归因任务中，该简单实例化方法匹配或超越了最先进的离散生成模型和领先的RNA-seq归因方法。

Conclusion: 即使采用简单的实例化配置，CountsDiff也展现出竞争力，为未来通过优化设计选择实现进一步性能提升留有相当空间。

Abstract: Diffusion models have excelled at generative tasks for both continuous and token-based domains, but their application to discrete ordinal data remains underdeveloped. We present CountsDiff, a diffusion framework designed to natively model distributions on the natural numbers. CountsDiff extends the Blackout diffusion framework by simplifying its formulation through a direct parameterization in terms of a survival probability schedule and an explicit loss weighting. This introduces flexibility through design parameters with direct analogues in existing diffusion modeling frameworks. Beyond this reparameterization, CountsDiff introduces features from modern diffusion models, previously absent in counts-based domains, including continuous-time training, classifier-free guidance, and churn/remasking reverse dynamics that allow non-monotone reverse trajectories. We propose an initial instantiation of CountsDiff and validate it on natural image datasets (CIFAR-10, CelebA), exploring the effects of varying the introduced design parameters in a complex, well-studied, and interpretable data domain. We then highlight biological count assays as a natural use case, evaluating CountsDiff on single-cell RNA-seq imputation in a fetal cell and heart cell atlas. Remarkably, we find that even this simple instantiation matches or surpasses the performance of a state-of-the-art discrete generative model and leading RNA-seq imputation methods, while leaving substantial headroom for further gains through optimized design choices in future work.

</details>


### [174] [Automated Conjecture Resolution with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.03789)
*Haocheng Ju,Guoxiong Gao,Jiedong Jiang,Bin Wu,Zeming Sun,Leheng Chen,Yutong Wang,Yuefeng Wang,Zichen Wang,Wanyi He,Peihao Wu,Liang Xiao,Ruochuan Liu,Bryan Dai,Bin Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个结合自然语言推理与形式验证的自动化框架，用于解决研究级数学问题。框架包含两个组件：从事非形式推理的 Rethlas（含定理搜索引擎 Matlas）和进行形式验证的 Archon（含 LeanSearch）。该框架成功自动解决了一个交换代数中的开放问题，并在 Lean 4 中完成了形式化证明，几乎无需人工干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在解决和验证研究级数学问题时仍面临挑战，主要由于自然语言推理固有的歧义性。现有方法难以实现端到端的、自动化的数学问题求解。

Method: 框架由两个核心组件构成：1) Rethlas：模拟人类数学家工作流程的非形式推理智能体，结合推理原语与定理搜索引擎 Matlas 探索解题策略；2) Archon：配备形式定理搜索引擎 LeanSearch 的形式验证智能体，通过结构化任务分解、迭代优化和自动证明综合，将非形式论证转化为 Lean 4 项目。

Result: 成功自动解决了一个交换代数领域的开放问题，并在 Lean 4 中完成形式化验证，几乎无需人工参与。实验表明：强大的定理检索工具能够发现并应用跨领域数学技术；形式化智能体能够自主填补非形式论证中的非平凡缺口。

Conclusion: 本文展示了一种有前景的数学研究范式：配备定理检索工具的非形式推理系统与形式推理系统协同运作，可产生可验证结果、大幅减少人力投入，是人类-AI协作数学研究的有效实现方式。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have significantly improved their ability to perform mathematical reasoning, extending from elementary problem solving to increasingly capable performance on research-level problems. However, reliably solving and verifying such problems remains challenging due to the inherent ambiguity of natural language reasoning. In this paper, we propose an automated framework for tackling research-level mathematical problems that integrates natural language reasoning with formal verification, enabling end-to-end problem solving with minimal human intervention. Our framework consists of two components: an informal reasoning agent, Rethlas, and a formal verification agent, Archon. Rethlas mimics the workflow of human mathematicians by combining reasoning primitives with our theorem search engine, Matlas, to explore solution strategies and construct candidate proofs. Archon, equipped with our formal theorem search engine LeanSearch, translates informal arguments into formalized Lean 4 projects through structured task decomposition, iterative refinement, and automated proof synthesis, ensuring machine-checkable correctness. Using this framework, we automatically resolve an open problem in commutative algebra and formally verify the resulting proof in Lean 4 with essentially no human involvement. Our experiments demonstrate that strong theorem retrieval tools enable the discovery and application of cross-domain mathematical techniques, while the formal agent is capable of autonomously filling nontrivial gaps in informal arguments. More broadly, our work illustrates a promising paradigm for mathematical research in which informal and formal reasoning systems, equipped with theorem retrieval tools, operate in tandem to produce verifiable results, substantially reduce human effort, and offer a concrete instantiation of human-AI collaborative mathematical research.

</details>
